=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\index.js ===
/**
 * Discord Music Bot â€” Entry Point
 *
 * index.js chá»‰ lÃ m 4 viá»‡c:
 * 1. Khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng HTTP health server (cho Render / Railway / uptime monitor)
 * 2. Khá»Ÿi táº¡o Discord Client + Kazagumo/Shoukaku + cÃ¡c service
 * 3. ÄÄƒng kÃ½ slash command vÃ  gáº¯n router tÆ°Æ¡ng tÃ¡c
 * 4. Xá»­ lÃ½ táº¯t mÃ¡y an toÃ n (graceful shutdown)
 *
 * ToÃ n bá»™ logic nghiá»‡p vá»¥ náº±m trong `src/` â€” xem `src/core/` cho pháº§n Ä‘á»‹nh tuyáº¿n.
 */

require('dotenv').config();

const path = require('node:path');
const { Client, GatewayIntentBits, REST, Routes, Events } = require('discord.js');
const { Kazagumo } = require('kazagumo');
const KazagumoSpotify = require('kazagumo-spotify');
const { Connectors } = require('shoukaku');
const express = require('express');

// --- Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh & háº¡ táº§ng ---
const { Nodes } = require('./src/config/lavalink');
const constants = require('./src/config/constants');
const { createLogger, attachErrorReporter } = require('./src/services/logger');

// --- Services ---
const PlayerService = require('./src/services/player/PlayerService');
const { getStateCount } = require('./src/services/player/PlayerState');
const StorageService = require('./src/services/storage/StorageService');
const VoicePresenceService = require('./src/services/voice/VoicePresenceService');
const SearchCache = require('./src/services/cache/SearchCache');
const AutoplayService = require('./src/services/autoplay/AutoplayService');
const cooldown = require('./src/services/cooldown/CooldownService');
const SearchUI = require('./src/services/ui/SearchUI');

// --- Lá»›p Ä‘á»‹nh tuyáº¿n ---
const { createContext } = require('./src/core/context');
const CommandHandler = require('./src/core/CommandHandler');
const ButtonHandler = require('./src/core/ButtonHandler');
const SelectHandler = require('./src/core/SelectHandler');

// --- TÆ°Æ¡ng tÃ¡c ---
const nowPlayingButtons = require('./src/interactions/nowPlayingButtons');
const queueButtons = require('./src/interactions/queueButtons');
const filterSelect = require('./src/interactions/filterSelect');
const searchSelect = require('./src/interactions/searchSelect');

// --- Danh sÃ¡ch lá»‡nh ---
const commandModules = require('./src/commands');

const log = createLogger('Bootstrap');

// ============================================================
// 0. Thu tháº­p lá»—i bÃªn ngoÃ i (tÃ¹y chá»n â€” Sentry)
// ============================================================
if (process.env.SENTRY_DSN) {
    try {
        // Chá»‰ hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng náº¿u @sentry/node Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c cÃ i; thiáº¿u gÃ³i cÅ©ng khÃ´ng sao.
        const Sentry = require('@sentry/node');
        Sentry.init({ dsn: process.env.SENTRY_DSN, tracesSampleRate: 0.1 });
        attachErrorReporter((error, context) => Sentry.captureException(error, { extra: context }));
        log.info('ÄÃ£ báº­t Sentry Ä‘á»ƒ thu tháº­p lá»—i.');
    } catch {
        log.warn('CÃ³ SENTRY_DSN nhÆ°ng chÆ°a cÃ i @sentry/node â€” bá» qua thu tháº­p lá»—i.');
    }
}

// ============================================================
// 1. HTTP Health Server
// ============================================================
const app = express();
const startedAt = Date.now();

/** Chuyá»ƒn mÃ£ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i node Lavalink thÃ nh tÃªn dá»… Ä‘á»c (Shoukaku: 0=CONNECTING, 1=CONNECTED, 2=DISCONNECTING, 3=DISCONNECTED) */
const NODE_STATES = {
    0: 'connecting',
    1: 'connected',
    2: 'disconnecting',
    3: 'disconnected'
};

/** CÃ¡c Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c gÃ¡n sau khi khá»Ÿi táº¡o */
let client = null;
let manager = null;
let playerService = null;
let storage = null;
let voicePresence = null;

app.get('/', (_req, res) => res.send('Bot Node.js Ä‘ang hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng!'));

app.get('/health', (_req, res) => {
    const memory = process.memoryUsage();

    const nodes = manager
        ? [...manager.shoukaku.nodes.values()].map((node) => ({
              name: node.name,
              state: NODE_STATES[node.state] || String(node.state),
              players: node.stats?.playingPlayers ?? null,
              penalty: node.penalty ?? null
          }))
        : [];

    const connectedNodes = nodes.filter((node) => node.state === 'connected').length;
    const healthy = connectedNodes > 0 || nodes.length === 0;

    res.status(healthy ? 200 : 503).json({
        status: healthy ? 'ok' : 'degraded',
        uptimeSeconds: Math.round((Date.now() - startedAt) / 1000),
        memory: {
            rssMb: Math.round(memory.rss / 1024 / 1024),
            heapUsedMb: Math.round(memory.heapUsed / 1024 / 1024)
        },
        discord: {
            ready: Boolean(client?.isReady()),
            guilds: client?.guilds?.cache?.size ?? 0,
            ping: client?.ws?.ping ?? null
        },
        players: manager ? manager.players.size : 0,
        guildStates: getStateCount(),
        searchCache: playerService ? playerService.searchCache.stats : null,
        cooldownKeys: cooldown.size,
        pendingVoiceLeave: voicePresence ? voicePresence.pendingCount : 0,
        storage: storage ? { ready: storage.ready, guilds: Object.keys(storage.data.guilds).length } : null,
        lavalink: nodes
    });
});

const server = app.listen(process.env.PORT || 8080, '0.0.0.0', () => {
    log.info(`Health server Ä‘ang láº¯ng nghe trÃªn cá»•ng ${process.env.PORT || 8080}`);
});

// ============================================================
// 2. Anti-Crash Handlers
// ============================================================
process.on('unhandledRejection', (reason) => log.error('[ANTI-CRASH] Promise bá»‹ tá»« chá»‘i:', reason));
process.on('uncaughtException', (error) => log.error('[ANTI-CRASH] Lá»—i há»‡ thá»‘ng:', error));
process.on('uncaughtExceptionMonitor', (error) => log.error('[ANTI-CRASH] GiÃ¡m sÃ¡t:', error));

// ============================================================
// 3. Kiá»ƒm tra cáº¥u hÃ¬nh Spotify
// ============================================================
const spotifyClientId = (process.env.SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID || '').trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');
const spotifyClientSecret = (process.env.SPOTIFY_CLIENT_SECRET || '').trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');

if (!spotifyClientId || !spotifyClientSecret) {
    log.warn(
        'ChÆ°a cáº¥u hÃ¬nh SPOTIFY_CLIENT_ID / SPOTIFY_CLIENT_SECRET â€” bot sáº½ dÃ¹ng node Lavalink dá»± phÃ²ng Ä‘á»ƒ phÃ¡t nháº¡c.'
    );
} else {
    log.info('ÄÃ£ nháº­n diá»‡n Spotify API Key.');
}

// ============================================================
// 4. Discord Client + Kazagumo
// ============================================================
client = new Client({
    intents: [GatewayIntentBits.Guilds, GatewayIntentBits.GuildVoiceStates, GatewayIntentBits.GuildMessages]
});

manager = new Kazagumo(
    {
        defaultSearchEngine: 'youtube_music',
        plugins: [
            new KazagumoSpotify({
                clientId: spotifyClientId,
                clientSecret: spotifyClientSecret,
                playlistPageLimit: 1,
                albumPageLimit: 1,
                searchLimit: 10,
                searchMarket: 'VN'
            })
        ],
        send: (guildId, payload) => {
            const guild = client.guilds.cache.get(guildId);
            if (guild && guild.shard) {
                guild.shard.send(payload);
            } else if (client.ws) {
                client.ws.broadcast(payload);
            }
        }
    },
    new Connectors.DiscordJS(client),
    Nodes,
    {
        moveOnDisconnect: true,
        resume: true,
        reconnectTries: 10,
        restTimeout: 10000
    }
);

// ============================================================
// 5. Khá»Ÿi táº¡o Services
// ============================================================
storage = new StorageService({
    filePath: path.join(constants.DATA_DIR, 'guilds.json'),
    maxHistory: constants.MAX_HISTORY
});

const searchCache = new SearchCache({
    max: constants.SEARCH_CACHE_MAX,
    ttlMs: constants.SEARCH_CACHE_TTL_MS
});

playerService = new PlayerService(client, manager, {
    storage,
    searchCache,
    autoplayService: new AutoplayService(manager),
    logger: createLogger('PlayerService')
});

voicePresence = new VoicePresenceService(client, manager, {
    idleMs: constants.VOICE_EMPTY_LEAVE_MS,
    onIdle: async (guildId) => {
        log.info(`KhÃ´ng cÃ²n ai trong kÃªnh thoáº¡i â€” rá»i phÃ²ng táº¡i guild ${guildId}.`);
        const player = manager.players.get(guildId);

        if (player) {
            const channel = client.channels.cache.get(player.textId);
            await playerService.stop(player);
            if (channel && typeof channel.send === 'function') {
                await channel.send('ðŸ‘‹ KhÃ´ng cÃ²n ai trong kÃªnh thoáº¡i nÃªn tÃ´i Ä‘Ã£ rá»i phÃ²ng.').catch(() => {});
            }
        } else {
            try { await manager.destroyPlayer(guildId); } catch (_) {}
            try { await manager.shoukaku.leaveVoiceChannel(guildId); } catch (_) {}
            try {
                const guild = client.guilds.cache.get(guildId);
                if (guild?.members?.me?.voice?.channelId) {
                    await guild.members.me.voice.disconnect();
                }
            } catch (_) {}
        }
    }
});

// ============================================================
// 6. Äá»‹nh tuyáº¿n tÆ°Æ¡ng tÃ¡c
// ============================================================
const ctx = createContext({
    client,
    manager,
    playerService,
    storage,
    voicePresence,
    logger: log
});

const commandHandler = new CommandHandler(ctx);
commandHandler.registerAll(commandModules);

const buttonHandler = new ButtonHandler(ctx);
buttonHandler.register(nowPlayingButtons.prefix, nowPlayingButtons.handle);
buttonHandler.register(queueButtons.prefix, queueButtons.handle);

const selectHandler = new SelectHandler(ctx);
selectHandler.register(filterSelect.customId, filterSelect.handle);
selectHandler.register(searchSelect.customId, searchSelect.handle);

// ============================================================
// 7. Kazagumo / Shoukaku Events
// ============================================================
manager.shoukaku.on('ready', (name) => log.info(`Node Lavalink "${name}" Ä‘Ã£ káº¿t ná»‘i.`));
manager.shoukaku.on('error', (name, error) => log.error(`Lá»—i node Lavalink "${name}":`, error));
manager.shoukaku.on('close', (name, code, reason) => {
    log.warn(`Node Lavalink "${name}" Ä‘Ã£ ngáº¯t káº¿t ná»‘i (code ${code}): ${reason || 'khÃ´ng rÃµ lÃ½ do'}`);
});
manager.shoukaku.on('disconnect', (name, count) => {
    log.warn(`Node Lavalink "${name}" máº¥t káº¿t ná»‘i, Ä‘ang thá»­ láº¡i (láº§n ${count})...`);
});

manager.on('playerStart', (player, track) => {
    playerService.onTrackStart(player, track).catch((err) => log.error('Lá»—i trong onTrackStart:', err));
});

manager.on('playerEnd', (player) => {
    try {
        playerService.onTrackEnd(player, player.queue.current);
    } catch (err) {
        log.error('Lá»—i trong onTrackEnd:', err);
    }
});

manager.on('playerEmpty', (player) => {
    playerService.onPlayerEmpty(player).catch((err) => log.error('Lá»—i trong onPlayerEmpty:', err));
});

manager.on('playerDestroy', (player) => {
    playerService.onPlayerDestroy(player.guildId);
});

manager.on('playerResolveError', async (player, track, message) => {
    log.error(`KhÃ´ng phÃ¢n giáº£i Ä‘Æ°á»£c bÃ i hÃ¡t "${track?.title}":`, message);
    const channel = client.channels.cache.get(player.textId);
    if (channel && typeof channel.send === 'function') {
        channel.send(`âš ï¸ KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ phÃ¡t bÃ i hÃ¡t **${track?.title || 'hiá»‡n táº¡i'}**: ${message || 'Lá»—i nguá»“n phÃ¡t'}. Äang chuyá»ƒn bÃ i...`).catch(() => {});
    }
    if (player.queue.size > 0) {
        player.skip();
    } else {
        await playerService.stop(player).catch(() => {});
    }
});

manager.on('playerException', async (player, data) => {
    log.error(`Lá»—i phÃ¡t nháº¡c táº¡i guild ${player.guildId}:`, data);
    const channel = client.channels.cache.get(player.textId);
    if (channel && typeof channel.send === 'function') {
        channel.send('âš ï¸ BÃ i hÃ¡t bá»‹ lá»—i luá»“ng phÃ¡t hoáº·c bá»‹ cháº·n báº£n quyá»n. Äang tá»± Ä‘á»™ng chuyá»ƒn bÃ i tiáº¿p theo...').catch(() => {});
    }
    if (player.queue.size > 0) {
        player.skip();
    } else {
        await playerService.stop(player).catch(() => {});
    }
});

manager.on('playerStuck', async (player, track, thresholdMs) => {
    log.warn(`BÃ i hÃ¡t "${track?.title}" bá»‹ ngháº½n phÃ¡t (${thresholdMs}ms) táº¡i guild ${player.guildId}.`);
    const channel = client.channels.cache.get(player.textId);
    if (channel && typeof channel.send === 'function') {
        channel.send(`âš ï¸ BÃ i hÃ¡t **${track?.title || 'hiá»‡n táº¡i'}** bá»‹ ngháº½n luá»“ng dá»¯ liá»‡u. Äang chuyá»ƒn bÃ i tiáº¿p theo...`).catch(() => {});
    }
    if (player.queue.size > 0) {
        player.skip();
    } else {
        await playerService.stop(player).catch(() => {});
    }
});

manager.on('playerClosed', (_player, data) => log.warn('Káº¿t ná»‘i thoáº¡i bá»‹ Ä‘Ã³ng:', data));

// ============================================================
// 8. ÄÄƒng kÃ½ Slash Commands
// ============================================================
client.once(Events.ClientReady, async () => {
    log.info(`Bot Ä‘Ã£ online: ${client.user.tag}`);

    await storage.init();

    const rest = new REST({ version: '10' }).setToken(process.env.DISCORD_TOKEN);
    const body = commandHandler.getDefinitions();

    try {
        log.info(`Äang Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ ${body.length} slash command...`);

        if (process.env.DEV_GUILD_ID) {
            try {
                await rest.put(Routes.applicationGuildCommands(client.user.id, process.env.DEV_GUILD_ID), { body });
                log.info(`ÄÃ£ Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ ${body.length} slash command cho guild ${process.env.DEV_GUILD_ID}.`);
            } catch (guildErr) {
                log.warn('KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ guild slash commands:', guildErr.message);
            }
        }

        await rest.put(Routes.applicationCommands(client.user.id), { body });
        log.info(`ÄÃ£ Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ ${body.length} global slash command.`);
    } catch (err) {
        log.error('Lá»—i Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ slash commands:', err);
    }

    voicePresence.start();
    startMaintenanceJobs();
});

// ============================================================
// 9. Interaction Router
// ============================================================
client.on('interactionCreate', async (interaction) => {
    try {
        if (interaction.isChatInputCommand()) {
            log.info(`[Command /${interaction.commandName}] bá»Ÿi ${interaction.user.tag} (guild: ${interaction.guildId})`);
            return await commandHandler.dispatch(interaction);
        }

        if (interaction.isButton()) {
            log.info(`[Button ${interaction.customId}] bá»Ÿi ${interaction.user.tag} (guild: ${interaction.guildId})`);
            return await buttonHandler.dispatch(interaction);
        }

        if (interaction.isStringSelectMenu()) {
            log.info(`[Select ${interaction.customId}] bá»Ÿi ${interaction.user.tag} (guild: ${interaction.guildId})`);
            return await selectHandler.dispatch(interaction);
        }
    } catch (err) {
        log.error('Lá»—i khÃ´ng mong Ä‘á»£i khi xá»­ lÃ½ tÆ°Æ¡ng tÃ¡c:', err);
    }
});

// ============================================================
// 10. Dá»n dáº¹p Ä‘á»‹nh ká»³ (cache, cooldown, phiÃªn tÃ¬m kiáº¿m)
// ============================================================
let maintenanceInterval = null;

function startMaintenanceJobs() {
    const run = () => {
        const removedCache = searchCache.prune();
        const removedSessions = SearchUI.prune();
        const removedCooldown = cooldown.sweep();
        if (removedCache || removedSessions || removedCooldown) {
            log.debug(
                `Dá»n dáº¹p Ä‘á»‹nh ká»³: cache=${removedCache}, phiÃªn tÃ¬m kiáº¿m=${removedSessions}, cooldown=${removedCooldown}`
            );
        }
    };

    maintenanceInterval = setInterval(run, 5 * 60 * 1000);
    if (typeof maintenanceInterval.unref === 'function') maintenanceInterval.unref();
}

// ============================================================
// 11. Graceful Shutdown
// ============================================================
let shuttingDown = false;

async function shutdown(signal) {
    if (shuttingDown) return;
    shuttingDown = true;
    log.info(`Nháº­n tÃ­n hiá»‡u ${signal} â€” Ä‘ang táº¯t bot an toÃ n...`);

    try {
        if (voicePresence) voicePresence.stop();
    } catch {
        /* bá» qua */
    }
    try {
        if (maintenanceInterval) clearInterval(maintenanceInterval);
    } catch {
        /* bá» qua */
    }

    try {
        for (const player of manager.players.values()) {
            await player.destroy().catch(() => {});
        }
    } catch (err) {
        log.warn('KhÃ´ng há»§y Ä‘Æ°á»£c toÃ n bá»™ player:', err.message);
    }

    try {
        await storage.close();
    } catch (err) {
        log.warn('KhÃ´ng ghi ná»‘t dá»¯ liá»‡u:', err.message);
    }
    try {
        server.close();
    } catch {
        /* bá» qua */
    }
    try {
        client.destroy();
    } catch {
        /* bá» qua */
    }

    log.info('ÄÃ£ táº¯t bot.');
    process.exit(0);
}

process.on('SIGTERM', () => shutdown('SIGTERM'));
process.on('SIGINT', () => shutdown('SIGINT'));

// ============================================================
// 12. ÄÄƒng nháº­p Discord
// ============================================================
client.login(process.env.DISCORD_TOKEN);

# ============================================================
# Stage 1 â€” CÃ i dependencies (chá»‰ production)
# ============================================================
FROM node:20-alpine AS deps

WORKDIR /app

# Chá»‰ copy manifest trÆ°á»›c Ä‘á»ƒ táº­n dá»¥ng cache layer cá»§a Docker
COPY package.json package-lock.json ./

# npm ci cÃ i Ä‘Ãºng theo lockfile â†’ build tÃ¡i láº­p Ä‘Æ°á»£c, nhanh hÆ¡n npm install
RUN npm ci --omit=dev --no-audit --no-fund && npm cache clean --force


# ============================================================
# Stage 2 â€” Image runtime
# ============================================================
FROM node:20-alpine

ENV NODE_ENV=production \
    PORT=8080 \
    DATA_DIR=/app/data

WORKDIR /app

# Chá»‰ copy node_modules Ä‘Ã£ tá»‘i Æ°u tá»« stage deps
COPY --from=deps /app/node_modules ./node_modules

# Copy mÃ£ nguá»“n (Ä‘Ã£ lá»c qua .dockerignore)
COPY . .

# ThÆ° má»¥c dá»¯ liá»‡u bá»n vá»¯ng + phÃ¢n quyá»n cho user khÃ´ng pháº£i root
RUN mkdir -p /app/data && chown -R node:node /app

# KhÃ´ng cháº¡y báº±ng root
USER node

EXPOSE 8080

# Docker tá»± khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng láº¡i container náº¿u /health khÃ´ng pháº£n há»“i
HEALTHCHECK --interval=30s --timeout=5s --start-period=20s --retries=3 \
    CMD wget -q --spider http://127.0.0.1:8080/health || exit 1

CMD ["node", "index.js"]

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\docker-compose.yml ===
# ============================================================
# Cháº¡y bot + Lavalink v4 tá»± host chá»‰ báº±ng má»™t lá»‡nh:
#   docker compose up -d --build
#
# Cáº§n táº¡o file .env trÆ°á»›c (xem .env.example), tá»‘i thiá»ƒu pháº£i cÃ³ DISCORD_TOKEN.
# ============================================================

services:
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  # MÃ¡y chá»§ Ã¢m thanh Lavalink v4
  # ----------------------------------------------------------
  lavalink:
    image: ghcr.io/lavalink-devs/lavalink:4
    container_name: lavalink
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:

/**
 * /autoplay â€” Báº­t/táº¯t tá»± Ä‘á»™ng phÃ¡t bÃ i hÃ¡t liÃªn quan khi háº¿t hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i.
 */

const { getState } = require('../services/player/PlayerState');

const definitions = [
    {
        name: 'autoplay',
        description: 'Báº­t/táº¯t tá»± Ä‘á»™ng phÃ¡t bÃ i hÃ¡t liÃªn quan khi háº¿t hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i',
        options: [
            {
                name: 'mode',
                type: 3, // STRING
                description: 'Báº­t hay táº¯t (bá» trá»‘ng Ä‘á»ƒ xem tráº¡ng thÃ¡i hiá»‡n táº¡i)',
                required: false,
                choices: [
                    { name: 'â™¾ï¸ Báº­t', value: 'on' },
                    { name: 'â¹ï¸ Táº¯t', value: 'off' }
                ]
            }
        ]
    }
];

const meta = {
    requiresPlayer: true,
    sameVoice: true,
    defer: 'ephemeral'
};

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const { playerService, player, t } = ctx;
    const mode = interaction.options.getString('mode');
    const state = getState(interaction.guild.id);

    if (!mode) {
        return interaction.editReply(
            state.autoplay ? t('commands.autoplay.currentOn') : t('commands.autoplay.currentOff')
        );
    }

    const enabled = mode === 'on';
    playerService.setAutoplay(player, enabled);
    await playerService.refreshPanels(player);

    await interaction.editReply(enabled ? t('commands.autoplay.enabled') : t('commands.autoplay.disabled'));
}

module.exports = { definitions, meta, execute };

/**
 * /filter â€” Báº­t/táº¯t cÃ¡c bá»™ lá»c Ã¢m thanh cá»§a Lavalink.
 */

const { getState } = require('../services/player/PlayerState');
const FilterService = require('../services/filters/FilterService');

/** Danh sÃ¡ch choice gá»­i lÃªn Discord (tá»‘i Ä‘a 25) */
const choices = FilterService.getNames()
    .slice(0, 25)
    .map((name) => ({
        name: `${FilterService.getEmoji(name)} ${FilterService.getLabel(name)}`.slice(0, 100),
        value: name
    }));

const definitions = [
    {
        name: 'filter',
        description: 'Báº­t/táº¯t bá»™ lá»c Ã¢m thanh (bassboost, nightcore, 8D...)',
        options: [
            {
                name: 'name',
                type: 3, // STRING
                description: 'TÃªn bá»™ lá»c muá»‘n Ã¡p dá»¥ng (bá» trá»‘ng Ä‘á»ƒ xem bá»™ lá»c hiá»‡n táº¡i)',
                required: false,
                choices
            }
        ]
    }
];

const meta = {
    requiresPlayer: true,
    sameVoice: true,
    defer: 'ephemeral'
};

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const { playerService, player, t } = ctx;
    const name = interaction.options.getString('name');

    // KhÃ´ng truyá»n tÃªn â†’ hiá»ƒn thá»‹ bá»™ lá»c hiá»‡n táº¡i kÃ¨m danh sÃ¡ch kháº£ dá»¥ng
    if (!name) {
        const state = getState(interaction.guild.id);
        const current = state.filter || FilterService.default;
        const list = FilterService.getNames()
            .map((item) => `${FilterService.getEmoji(item)} \`${item}\``)
            .join(' Â· ');

        return interaction.editReply(
            `${t('commands.filter.current', { name: FilterService.getLabel(current) })}\n` +
                t('commands.filter.list', { list })
        );
    }

    const result = await playerService.setFilter(player, name);

    if (!result.ok) {
        return interaction.editReply(t('commands.filter.invalid', { name }));
    }

    await playerService.refreshPanels(player);
    await interaction.editReply(t('commands.filter.applied', { name: result.label }));
}

module.exports = { definitions, meta, execute };

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\commands\forward.js ===
/**
 * /forward â€” Tua tá»›i trÆ°á»›c má»™t sá»‘ giÃ¢y.
 */

const { formatDuration } = require('../utils/formatters');

/** Sá»‘ giÃ¢y tua máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh */
const DEFAULT_SECONDS = 10;

const definitions = [
    {
        name: 'forward',
        description: 'Tua tá»›i trÆ°á»›c má»™t sá»‘ giÃ¢y',
        options: [
            {
                name: 'seconds',
                type: 4, // INTEGER
                description: `Sá»‘ giÃ¢y muá»‘n tua tá»›i (máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh ${DEFAULT_SECONDS})`,
                required: false,
                min_value: 1,
                max_value: 600
            }
        ]
    }
];

const meta = {
    requiresPlayer: true,
    requiresPlaying: true,
    sameVoice: true,
    defer: 'ephemeral'
};

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const { playerService, player, t } = ctx;
    const seconds = interaction.options.getInteger('seconds') || DEFAULT_SECONDS;

    const target = Math.max(0, (player.position || 0) + seconds * 1000);
    const result = await playerService.seek(player, target);

    if (!result.ok) {
        return interaction.editReply(t(result.error, result.vars));
    }

    await playerService.refreshPanels(player);
    await interaction.editReply(t('commands.forward.done', { position: formatDuration(target) }));
}

module.exports = { definitions, meta, execute };

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\commands\helpers.js ===
/**
 * helpers.js â€” HÃ m dÃ¹ng chung cho cÃ¡c lá»‡nh phÃ¡t nháº¡c.
 */

/**
 * Chuyá»ƒn káº¿t quáº£ `playerService.enqueue()` thÃ nh thÃ´ng bÃ¡o lá»—i hiá»ƒn thá»‹ cho ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng.
 * @param {{ error: string|null, vars: object|null }} result
 * @param {(key: string, vars?: object) => string} t - HÃ m dá»‹ch
 * @returns {string}
 */
function enqueueErrorText(result, t) {
    switch (result.error) {
        case 'userQueueFull':
            return t('common.userQueueFull', result.vars);
        case 'queueFull':
            return t('common.queueFull', result.vars);
        default:
            return t('common.systemError');
    }
}

/**
 * Táº¡o ghi chÃº cáº£nh bÃ¡o khi playlist bá»‹ cáº¯t bá»›t do vÆ°á»£t giá»›i háº¡n hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i.
 * @param {{ added: number, trimmed: number }} result
 * @param {number} total - Tá»•ng sá»‘ bÃ i ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng yÃªu cáº§u
 * @param {(key: string, vars?: object) => string} t - HÃ m dá»‹ch
 * @returns {string|null}
 */
function trimNote(result, total, t) {
    if (!result.trimmed) return null;
    return t('common.playlistTrimmed', { total, loaded: result.added });
}

/**
 * GhÃ©p ghi chÃº chÃ­nh vÃ  ghi chÃº cáº£nh bÃ¡o thÃ nh má»™t chuá»—i.
 * @param {string} main
 * @param {string|null} extra
 * @returns {string}
 */
function combineNotes(main, extra) {
    return extra ? `${main}\n${extra}` : main;
}

module.exports = { enqueueErrorText, trimNote, combineNotes };

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\commands\history.js ===
/**
 * /history â€” Xem lá»‹ch sá»­ cÃ¡c bÃ i hÃ¡t Ä‘Ã£ phÃ¡t.
 */

const { EmbedBuilder } = require('discord.js');
const { getState } = require('../services/player/PlayerState');
const { formatDuration, truncate } = require('../utils/formatters');

/** Sá»‘ bÃ i tá»‘i Ä‘a hiá»ƒn thá»‹ trong lá»‹ch sá»­ */
const MAX_DISPLAY = 15;

const definitions = [
    {
        name: 'history',
        description: 'Xem lá»‹ch sá»­ cÃ¡c bÃ i hÃ¡t Ä‘Ã£ phÃ¡t trong mÃ¡y chá»§'
    }
];

const meta = {
    defer: 'ephemeral'
};

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const state = getState(interaction.guild.id);
    const { t } = ctx;

    if (state.history.length === 0) {
        return interaction.editReply(t('commands.history.empty'));
    }

    const shown = Math.min(MAX_DISPLAY, state.history.length);
    const lines = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < shown; i++) {
        const track = state.history[i];
        const num = String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0');
        const dur = formatDuration(track.duration || 0);
        const title = truncate(track.title || 'Unknown', 45);
        const requester = track.requesterName || 'N/A';

        lines.push(`\`${num}.\` **${title}**\nã€€ã€€\`${dur}\` â€¢ ðŸ‘¤ ${requester}`);
    }

    const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
        .setColor(0x9b59b6)
        .setTitle(t('commands.history.title'))
        .setDescription(lines.join('\n'))
        .setFooter({ text: t('commands.history.footer', { shown, total: state.history.length }) })
        .setTimestamp();

    await interaction.editReply({ content: '', embeds: [embed], components: [] });
}

module.exports = { definitions, meta, execute };

/**
 * /nowplaying & /np â€” Gá»­i báº£ng Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn bÃ i hÃ¡t Ä‘ang phÃ¡t vÃ o kÃªnh chat.
 *
 * ÄÃ¢y lÃ  lá»‡nh DUY NHáº¤T gá»­i báº£ng Now Playing cÃ´ng khai cho má»i ngÆ°á»i tháº¥y.
 * Má»i lá»‡nh phÃ¡t nháº¡c khÃ¡c Ä‘á»u hiá»ƒn thá»‹ báº£ng dáº¡ng ephemeral (chá»‰ ngÆ°á»i gá»i tháº¥y).
 */

const NowPlayingUI = require('../services/ui/NowPlayingUI');
const { getState } = require('../services/player/PlayerState');
const { respondEphemeral } = require('../utils/reply');

const definitions = [
    {
        name: 'nowplaying',
        description: 'Gá»­i cÃ´ng khai báº£ng Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn bÃ i hÃ¡t Ä‘ang phÃ¡t vÃ o kÃªnh chat'
    },
    {
        name: 'np',
        description: 'Gá»­i cÃ´ng khai báº£ng Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn bÃ i hÃ¡t Ä‘ang phÃ¡t vÃ o kÃªnh chat'
    }
];

const meta = {
    requiresPlayer: true,
    defer: false
};

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const { playerService, player, t } = ctx;
    const currentTrack = player.queue.current;

    if (!currentTrack) {
        return respondEphemeral(interaction, t('commands.nowplaying.none'));
    }

    const state = getState(interaction.guild.id);
    const { embed, components } = NowPlayingUI.create(player, currentTrack, state);

    // CÃ”NG KHAI vÃ o kÃªnh chat â€” khÃ´ng truyá»n cá» EPHEMERAL
    await interaction.reply({ embeds: [embed], components });

    // Cho ticker tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh tiáº¿p tá»¥c cáº­p nháº­t báº£ng cÃ´ng khai nÃ y.
    // DÃ¹ng fetchReply() Ä‘á»ƒ tÆ°Æ¡ng thÃ­ch cáº£ discord.js 14.14 láº«n 14.16+.
    try {
        const message = await interaction.fetchReply();
        playerService.trackPublicPanel(player, message);
    } catch (err) {
        ctx.logger.warn('KhÃ´ng láº¥y Ä‘Æ°á»£c tin nháº¯n vá»«a gá»­i Ä‘á»ƒ theo dÃµi tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh:', err.message);
    }
}

module.exports = { definitions, meta, execute };

/**
 * /play â€” PhÃ¡t nháº¡c tá»« URL hoáº·c tá»« khÃ³a tÃ¬m kiáº¿m.
 */

const { COMMAND_COOLDOWN_MS } = require('../config/constants');
const { enqueueErrorText, trimNote, combineNotes } = require('./helpers');

const definitions = [
    {
        name: 'play',
        description: 'PhÃ¡t nháº¡c tá»« YouTube, Spotify hoáº·c tá»« khÃ³a',
        options: [
            {
                name: 'query',
                type: 3, // STRING
                description: 'TÃªn bÃ i hÃ¡t hoáº·c URL (YouTube, Spotify, SoundCloud...)',
                required: true
            }
        ]
    }
];

const meta = {
    requiresVoice: true,
    defer: 'ephemeral',
    cooldown: COMMAND_COOLDOWN_MS
};

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx - Ngá»¯ cáº£nh bot
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const { playerService, voiceChannel, t } = ctx;
    const query = interaction.options.getString('query');
    const guildId = interaction.guild.id;

    ctx.logger.info(`[play] Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u tÃ¬m kiáº¿m query="${query}" cho user=${interaction.user.tag}`);

    // Pháº£n há»“i ngay Ä‘á»ƒ ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng biáº¿t bot Ä‘ang tÃ¬m bÃ i
    await interaction.editReply('ðŸ” Äang tÃ¬m kiáº¿m bÃ i hÃ¡t...');

    // 1. TÃ¬m kiáº¿m bÃ i hÃ¡t trÆ°á»›c
    const result = await playerService.search(query, interaction.user);

    if (!result.tracks.length) {
        return interaction.editReply(t('commands.play.noResult'));
    }

    ctx.logger.info(`[play] TÃ¬m tháº¥y ${result.tracks.length} bÃ i (type=${result.type}), Ä‘ang káº¿t ná»‘i voice channel ${voiceChannel.id}...`);

    // 2. CÃ³ bÃ i hÃ¡t há»£p lá»‡ má»›i káº¿t ná»‘i vÃ o voice channel
    await interaction.editReply(`ðŸŸ¢ ÄÃ£ tÃ¬m tháº¥y **${result.tracks.length}** bÃ i. Äang káº¿t ná»‘i vÃ o kÃªnh thoáº¡i...`);

    const player = await playerService.getOrCreatePlayer({
        guildId,
        textId: interaction.channelId,
        voiceId: voiceChannel.id
    });

    playerService.registerEphemeralContext(interaction, player);

    if (result.type === 'PLAYLIST') {
        const enqueued = await playerService.enqueue(player, result.tracks, { requesterId: interaction.user.id });

        if (!enqueued.ok) {
            playerService.releaseEphemeralContext(guildId);
            return interaction.editReply(enqueueErrorText(enqueued, t));
        }

        const note = combineNotes(
            t('commands.play.playlistAdded', {
                name: result.playlistName || 'Playlist',
                count: enqueued.added
            }),
            trimNote(enqueued, result.tracks.length, t)
        );

        await playerService.renderEphemeralPanel(player, player.queue.current || result.tracks[0], note);
        return;
    }

    const track = result.tracks[0];
    const enqueued = await playerService.enqueue(player, [track], { requesterId: interaction.user.id });

    if (!enqueued.ok) {
        playerService.releaseEphemeralContext(guildId);
        return interaction.editReply(enqueueErrorText(enqueued, t));
    }

    await playerService.renderEphemeralPanel(
        player,
        player.queue.current || track,
        t('commands.play.added', { title: track.title })
    );
}

module.exports = { definitions, meta, execute };

/**
 * /queue â€” Xem, quáº£n lÃ½ danh sÃ¡ch chá» (view, remove, move, clear).
 */

const QueueUI = require('../services/ui/QueueUI');
const { checkSameVoiceChannel } = require('../utils/permissions');
const { respondEphemeral } = require('../utils/reply');

const definitions = [
    {
        name: 'queue',
        description: 'Xem vÃ  quáº£n lÃ½ danh sÃ¡ch chá»',
        options: [
            {
                name: 'page',
                type: 4, // INTEGER
                description: 'Sá»‘ trang muá»‘n xem (máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh: 1)',
                required: false,
                min_value: 1
            },
            {
                name: 'remove',
                type: 4, // INTEGER
                description: 'XÃ³a bÃ i hÃ¡t táº¡i vá»‹ trÃ­ nÃ y (VD: 3)',
                required: false,
                min_value: 1
            },
            {
                name: 'move_from',
                type: 4,
                description: 'Di chuyá»ƒn bÃ i: vá»‹ trÃ­ nguá»“n',
                required: false,
                min_value: 1
            },
            {
                name: 'move_to',
                type: 4,
                description: 'Di chuyá»ƒn bÃ i: vá»‹ trÃ­ Ä‘Ã­ch',
                required: false,
                min_value: 1
            },
            {
                name: 'clear',
                type: 5, // BOOLEAN
                description: 'XÃ³a toÃ n bá»™ hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i (giá»¯ bÃ i Ä‘ang phÃ¡t)',
                required: false
            }
        ]
    }
];

const meta = {
    // Báº£ng hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i chá»‰ hiá»‡n cho ngÆ°á»i gá»i lá»‡nh Ä‘á»ƒ kÃªnh chat khÃ´ng bá»‹ rÃ¡c.
    // Chá»‰ /nowplaying má»›i gá»­i cÃ´ng khai.
    defer: 'ephemeral'
};

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm tra quyá»n thao tÃ¡c hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i (pháº£i á»Ÿ cÃ¹ng kÃªnh thoáº¡i vá»›i bot).
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 * @param {object} player
 * @returns {boolean} true náº¿u Ä‘Æ°á»£c phÃ©p
 */
async function ensureCanModify(interaction, ctx, player) {
    const check = checkSameVoiceChannel(interaction.member, player);
    if (!check.ok) {
        await respondEphemeral(interaction, ctx.t(check.error));
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const { playerService, manager, queueService, t } = ctx;

    const player = manager.players.get(interaction.guild.id);
    if (!player) {
        return interaction.editReply(t('commands.queue.empty'));
    }

    // --- /queue clear ---
    if (interaction.options.getBoolean('clear')) {
        if (!(await ensureCanModify(interaction, ctx, player))) return;
        const count = queueService.clear(player);
        return interaction.editReply(t('commands.queue.cleared', { count }));
    }

    // --- /queue remove <index> ---
    const removeIndex = interaction.options.getInteger('remove');
    if (removeIndex !== null && removeIndex !== undefined) {
        if (!(await ensureCanModify(interaction, ctx, player))) return;

        // NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng nháº­p 1-based, service dÃ¹ng 0-based
        const removed = queueService.remove(player, removeIndex - 1);
        if (!removed) {
            return interaction.editReply(t('commands.queue.invalidPosition', { position: removeIndex }));
        }
        return interaction.editReply(
            t('commands.queue.removed', {
                title: removed.title || 'Unknown',
                position: removeIndex
            })
        );
    }

    // --- /queue move <from> <to> ---
    const moveFrom = interaction.options.getInteger('move_from');
    const moveTo = interaction.options.getInteger('move_to');
    if (moveFrom !== null && moveFrom !== undefined && moveTo !== null && moveTo !== undefined) {
        if (!(await ensureCanModify(interaction, ctx, player))) return;

        const success = queueService.move(player, moveFrom - 1, moveTo - 1);
        if (!success) {
            return interaction.editReply(t('commands.queue.invalidPosition', { position: moveFrom }));
        }
        return interaction.editReply(t('commands.queue.moved', { from: moveFrom, to: moveTo }));
    }

    // --- /queue [page] â€” Hiá»ƒn thá»‹ danh sÃ¡ch ---
    const page = interaction.options.getInteger('page') || 1;
    const { embed, components } = QueueUI.create(player, page);
    await interaction.editReply({ embeds: [embed], components });

    // KhÃ´ng dÃ¹ng playerService á»Ÿ Ä‘Ã¢y nhÆ°ng giá»¯ tham sá»‘ Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘á»“ng nháº¥t chá»¯ kÃ½
    void playerService;
}

module.exports = { definitions, meta, execute };

/**
 * /repeat â€” Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ láº·p (off / track / queue).
 */

const { getState } = require('../services/player/PlayerState');

const REPEAT_EMOJIS = { off: 'âž¡ï¸', track: 'ðŸ”‚', queue: 'ðŸ”' };

const definitions = [
    {
        name: 'repeat',
        description: 'Äá»•i cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ láº·p (Off / Track / Queue)',
        options: [
            {
                name: 'mode',
                type: 3, // STRING
                description: 'Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ láº·p mong muá»‘n',
                required: false,
                choices: [
                    { name: 'âž¡ï¸ Táº¯t láº·p', value: 'off' },
                    { name: 'ðŸ”‚ Láº·p bÃ i hiá»‡n táº¡i', value: 'track' },
                    { name: 'ðŸ” Láº·p danh sÃ¡ch', value: 'queue' }
                ]
            }
        ]
    }
];

const meta = {
    requiresPlayer: true,
    sameVoice: true,
    defer: 'ephemeral'
};

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const { playerService, player, t } = ctx;
    const state = getState(interaction.guild.id);

    const modeArg = interaction.options.getString('mode');
    const newMode = modeArg || state.cycleRepeatMode();
    if (modeArg) state.setRepeatMode(modeArg);

    playerService.persistState(interaction.guild.id, state, player.volume);
    await playerService.refreshPanels(player);

    const emoji = REPEAT_EMOJIS[newMode] || 'âž¡ï¸';
    const label = t(`commands.repeat.mode${newMode.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}${newMode.slice(1)}`);

    await interaction.editReply(`${emoji} ${t('commands.repeat.changed', { mode: label })}`);
}

module.exports = { definitions, meta, execute };

/**
 * /rewind â€” Tua láº¡i má»™t sá»‘ giÃ¢y.
 */

const { formatDuration } = require('../utils/formatters');

/** Sá»‘ giÃ¢y tua máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh */
const DEFAULT_SECONDS = 10;

const definitions = [
    {
        name: 'rewind',
        description: 'Tua láº¡i má»™t sá»‘ giÃ¢y',
        options: [
            {
                name: 'seconds',
                type: 4, // INTEGER
                description: `Sá»‘ giÃ¢y muá»‘n tua láº¡i (máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh ${DEFAULT_SECONDS})`,
                required: false,
                min_value: 1,
                max_value: 600
            }
        ]
    }
];

const meta = {
    requiresPlayer: true,
    requiresPlaying: true,
    sameVoice: true,
    defer: 'ephemeral'
};

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const { playerService, player, t } = ctx;
    const seconds = interaction.options.getInteger('seconds') || DEFAULT_SECONDS;

    const target = Math.max(0, (player.position || 0) - seconds * 1000);
    const result = await playerService.seek(player, target);

    if (!result.ok) {
        return interaction.editReply(t(result.error, result.vars));
    }

    await playerService.refreshPanels(player);
    await interaction.editReply(t('commands.rewind.done', { position: formatDuration(target) }));
}

module.exports = { definitions, meta, execute };

    const query = interaction.options.getString('query');
    const guildId = interaction.guild.id;

    const result = await playerService.search(query, interaction.user);

    if (!result.tracks.length) {
        return interaction.editReply(t('commands.search.noResult', { query }));
    }

    // LÆ°u káº¿t quáº£ Ä‘á»ƒ menu chá»n bÃ i tham chiáº¿u tá»›i
    SearchUI.storeResults(guildId, interaction.user.id, result.tracks);

    const { embed, components } = SearchUI.create(query, result.tracks, SEARCH_MENU_RESULTS);

    await interaction.editReply({ content: '', embeds: [embed], components });
}

module.exports = { definitions, meta, execute };

/**
 * /seek â€” Nháº£y tá»›i má»™t má»‘c thá»i gian trong bÃ i hÃ¡t.
 */

const { parseTime, formatDuration } = require('../utils/formatters');

const definitions = [
    {
        name: 'seek',
        description: 'Nháº£y tá»›i má»™t má»‘c thá»i gian trong bÃ i hÃ¡t',
        options: [
            {
                name: 'time',
                type: 3, // STRING
                description: 'Má»‘c thá»i gian, vÃ­ dá»¥ 1:30 hoáº·c 90 (giÃ¢y)',
                required: true
            }
        ]
    }
];

const meta = {
    requiresPlayer: true,
    requiresPlaying: true,
    sameVoice: true,
    defer: 'ephemeral'
};

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const { playerService, player, t } = ctx;
    const raw = interaction.options.getString('time');
    const positionMs = parseTime(raw);

    if (positionMs === null) {
        return interaction.editReply(t('commands.seek.invalidTime'));
    }

    const result = await playerService.seek(player, positionMs);

    if (!result.ok) {
        return interaction.editReply(t(result.error, result.vars));
    }

    await playerService.refreshPanels(player);
    await interaction.editReply(t('commands.seek.done', { position: formatDuration(positionMs) }));
}

module.exports = { definitions, meta, execute };

const definitions = [

            {
                name: 'mine',

                required: false
            }
        ]
    }

    requiresPlayer: true,
    sameVoice: true,
    defer: 'ephemeral'
};

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const { player, queueService, t } = ctx;

    if (player.queue.length <= 1) {
        return interaction.editReply(t('commands.shuffle.needMore'));

    const count = queueService.shuffle(player, mineOnly ? interaction.user.id : null);

/**
 * /stop â€” Dá»«ng nháº¡c, xÃ³a hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i, ngáº¯t káº¿t ná»‘i.
 */

const definitions = [
    {
        name: 'stop',
        description: 'Dá»«ng nháº¡c vÃ  thoÃ¡t khá»i kÃªnh thoáº¡i'
    }
];

const meta = {
    requiresVoice: false,
    defer: 'ephemeral'
};

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const player = ctx.player || ctx.manager.players.get(interaction.guild.id);

    if (player) {
        await ctx.playerService.stop(player);
    } else {
        // Giáº£i phÃ³ng vÃ  ngáº¯t káº¿t ná»‘i voice ngay cáº£ khi player khÃ´ng cÃ²n trong bá»™ nhá»›
        try { await ctx.manager.destroyPlayer(interaction.guild.id); } catch (_) {}
        try { await ctx.manager.shoukaku.leaveVoiceChannel(interaction.guild.id); } catch (_) {}
        try {
            const guild = ctx.client.guilds.cache.get(interaction.guild.id);
            if (guild?.members?.me?.voice?.channelId) {
                await guild.members.me.voice.disconnect();
            }
        } catch (_) {}
    }

    await interaction.editReply(ctx.t('commands.stop.done'));
}

/**
 * /volume â€” Äiá»u chá»‰nh Ã¢m lÆ°á»£ng phÃ¡t nháº¡c (1 - 200%).
 */

const { getState } = require('../services/player/PlayerState');

const definitions = [
    {
        name: 'volume',
        description: 'Xem hoáº·c Ä‘iá»u chá»‰nh Ã¢m lÆ°á»£ng phÃ¡t nháº¡c (1 - 200%)',
        options: [
            {
                name: 'level',
                type: 4, // INTEGER
                description: 'Má»©c Ã¢m lÆ°á»£ng (tá»« 1 Ä‘áº¿n 200%)',
                required: false,
                min_value: 1,
                max_value: 200
            }
        ]
    }
];

const meta = {
    requiresPlayer: true,
    sameVoice: true,
    defer: 'ephemeral'
};

/**
 * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx
 */
async function execute(interaction, ctx) {
    const { playerService, player, t } = ctx;
    const level = interaction.options.getInteger('level');

    if (level === null || level === undefined) {
        return interaction.editReply(t('commands.volume.current', { level: player.volume || 100 }));
    }

    const clamped = Math.max(1, Math.min(200, level));
    await player.setVolume(clamped);

    // LÆ°u cÃ i Ä‘áº·t vÃ  cáº­p nháº­t báº£ng Now Playing Ä‘ang má»Ÿ
    const state = getState(interaction.guild.id);
    playerService.persistState(interaction.guild.id, state, clamped);
    await playerService.refreshPanels(player);

    await interaction.editReply(t('commands.volume.changed', { level: clamped }));
}

module.exports = { definitions, meta, execute };

/**
 * Constants â€” Háº±ng sá»‘ cáº¥u hÃ¬nh toÃ n cá»¥c, cÃ³ thá»ƒ ghi Ä‘Ã¨ báº±ng biáº¿n mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng.
 */

/**
 * Äá»c sá»‘ nguyÃªn tá»« biáº¿n mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng, tráº£ vá» giÃ¡ trá»‹ máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh náº¿u khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡.
 * @param {string} name - TÃªn biáº¿n mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng
 * @param {number} fallback - GiÃ¡ trá»‹ máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh
 * @returns {number}
 */
function envInt(name, fallback) {
    const raw = process.env[name];
    if (raw === undefined || raw === null || String(raw).trim() === '') return fallback;
    const parsed = Number.parseInt(String(raw).trim(), 10);
    return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : fallback;
}

/**
 * Äá»c giÃ¡ trá»‹ boolean tá»« biáº¿n mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng ("true"/"1"/"yes").
 * @param {string} name
 * @param {boolean} fallback
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function envBool(name, fallback) {
    const raw = process.env[name];
    if (raw === undefined || raw === null || String(raw).trim() === '') return fallback;
    return ['true', '1', 'yes', 'on'].includes(String(raw).trim().toLowerCase());
}

/**
 * Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh danh sÃ¡ch Lavalink Nodes (Lavalink v4) & fallback.
 *
 * Thá»© tá»± Æ°u tiÃªn cáº¥u hÃ¬nh:
 *  1. `LAVALINK_NODES` â€” máº£ng JSON Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§, THAY THáº¾ hoÃ n toÃ n danh sÃ¡ch bÃªn dÆ°á»›i.
 *     VÃ­ dá»¥: LAVALINK_NODES=[{"name":"Node-1","url":"lavalink.example.com:443","auth":"mat-khau","secure":true}]
 *  2. `LAVALINK_HOST` + `LAVALINK_AUTH` + `LAVALINK_SECURE` â€” thÃªm má»™t node chÃ­nh
 *     lÃªn Ä‘áº§u danh sÃ¡ch (cÃ¡c node cÃ´ng khai bÃªn dÆ°á»›i váº«n lÃ m dá»± phÃ²ng).
 *  3. Danh sÃ¡ch node cÃ´ng khai miá»…n phÃ­ bÃªn dÆ°á»›i (chá»‰ dÃ¹ng Ä‘á»ƒ thá»­ nghiá»‡m).
 *     Äáº·t `LAVALINK_USE_PUBLIC_FALLBACK=false` Ä‘á»ƒ táº¯t hoÃ n toÃ n nhÃ³m nÃ y.
 *
 * LÆ¯U Ã: cÃ¡c node cÃ´ng khai bÃªn dÆ°á»›i lÃ  cá»§a cá»™ng Ä‘á»“ng, KHÃ”NG nÃªn dÃ¹ng cho
 * production. HÃ£y tá»± host Lavalink (xem docker-compose.yml) vÃ  khai bÃ¡o qua
 * `LAVALINK_NODES`.
 */

const { createLogger } = require('../services/logger');

const log = createLogger('LavalinkConfig');

/** Danh sÃ¡ch node cÃ´ng khai miá»…n phÃ­ â€” chá»‰ phÃ¹ há»£p Ä‘á»ƒ thá»­ nghiá»‡m */
const PUBLIC_FALLBACK_NODES = [
    {
        name: 'Kasawa_Direct',
        url: process.env.LAVALINK_PUBLIC_1_URL || 'lava2.kasawa.pro:2334',
        auth: process.env.LAVALINK_PUBLIC_1_AUTH || 'youshallnotpass',
        secure: false
    },
    {
        name: 'Nazha_SSL',
        url: process.env.LAVALINK_PUBLIC_2_URL || 'lavalink.nazha.online:443',
        auth: process.env.LAVALINK_PUBLIC_2_AUTH || 'nazhafreelava',
        secure: true
    },
    {
        name: 'Millohost_SSL',
        url: process.env.LAVALINK_PUBLIC_3_URL || 'lava-v4.millohost.my.id:443',
        auth: process.env.LAVALINK_PUBLIC_3_AUTH || 'https://discord.gg/mjS5J2K3ep',
        secure: true
    },
    {
        name: 'Jirayu_SSL',
        url: process.env.LAVALINK_PUBLIC_4_URL || 'lavalink.jirayu.net:443',
        auth: process.env.LAVALINK_PUBLIC_4_AUTH || 'youshallnotpass',
        secure: true
    }
];

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm tra má»™t Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng node cÃ³ Ä‘á»§ trÆ°á»ng báº¯t buá»™c hay khÃ´ng.
 * @param {unknown} node
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function isValidNode(node) {
    return Boolean(
        node &&
        typeof node === 'object' &&
        typeof node.name === 'string' &&
        node.name &&
        typeof node.url === 'string' &&
        node.url &&
        typeof node.auth === 'string'
    );
}

/**
 * Chuáº©n hoÃ¡ node vá» Ä‘Ãºng Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng Shoukaku yÃªu cáº§u.
 * @param {object} node
 * @returns {object}
 */
function normaliseNode(node) {
    return {
        name: node.name,
        url: node.url,
        auth: node.auth,
        secure: node.secure !== false,
        ...(node.group ? { group: node.group } : {})
    };
}

/**
 * Äá»c danh sÃ¡ch node tá»« biáº¿n mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng `LAVALINK_NODES` (JSON).
 * @returns {object[]|null} Danh sÃ¡ch node hoáº·c null náº¿u khÃ´ng cáº¥u hÃ¬nh/biáº¿n khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡
 */
function parseNodesFromEnv() {
    const raw = process.env.LAVALINK_NODES;
    if (!raw || !String(raw).trim()) return null;

    try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) throw new Error('giÃ¡ trá»‹ pháº£i lÃ  má»™t máº£ng JSON');
        const nodes = parsed.filter(isValidNode).map(normaliseNode);
        if (!nodes.length) throw new Error('khÃ´ng cÃ³ node há»£p lá»‡ nÃ o');
        return nodes;
    } catch (err) {
        log.error('LAVALINK_NODES khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡, sáº½ dÃ¹ng cáº¥u hÃ¬nh máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh.', err.message);
        return null;
    }
}

/**
 * XÃ¢y dá»±ng danh sÃ¡ch node hoÃ n chá»‰nh.
 * @returns {object[]}
 */
function buildNodes() {
    const fromEnv = parseNodesFromEnv();
    if (fromEnv) {
        log.info(`DÃ¹ng ${fromEnv.length} node Lavalink tá»« biáº¿n mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng LAVALINK_NODES.`);
        return fromEnv;
    }

    /** @type {object[]} */
    const nodes = [];

    // Node chÃ­nh cáº¥u hÃ¬nh thá»§ cÃ´ng luÃ´n Ä‘Æ°á»£c Æ°u tiÃªn
    if (process.env.LAVALINK_HOST) {
        nodes.push(
            normaliseNode({
                name: 'Custom-Main',
                url: process.env.LAVALINK_HOST,
                auth: process.env.LAVALINK_AUTH || 'youshallnotpass',
                secure: process.env.LAVALINK_SECURE ? process.env.LAVALINK_SECURE === 'true' : true
            })
        );
    }

    const usePublicFallback = process.env.LAVALINK_USE_PUBLIC_FALLBACK !== 'false';
    if (usePublicFallback) {
        nodes.push(...PUBLIC_FALLBACK_NODES.map(normaliseNode));
        log.warn('Äang dÃ¹ng node Lavalink cÃ´ng khai miá»…n phÃ­ â€” khÃ´ng khuyáº¿n khÃ­ch cho production.');
    }

    if (!nodes.length) {
        log.error('KhÃ´ng cÃ³ node Lavalink nÃ o Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº¥u hÃ¬nh! Bot sáº½ khÃ´ng phÃ¡t Ä‘Æ°á»£c nháº¡c.');
    }

    return nodes;
}

const Nodes = buildNodes();

module.exports = { Nodes, PUBLIC_FALLBACK_NODES, isValidNode };

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\core\ButtonHandler.js ===
/**
 * ButtonHandler â€” Äá»‹nh tuyáº¿n nÃºt báº¥m theo tiá»n tá»‘ customId.
 *
 * Má»—i nhÃ³m nÃºt Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ má»™t tiá»n tá»‘ (vÃ­ dá»¥ `np_`, `queue_`) cÃ¹ng hÃ m xá»­ lÃ½.
 * Nhá» váº­y index.js khÃ´ng cÃ²n pháº£i chá»©a má»™t khá»‘i `switch` khá»•ng lá»“.
 */

const { respondError } = require('../utils/reply');

class ButtonHandler {
    /**
     * @param {object} ctx - Ngá»¯ cáº£nh bot
     */
    constructor(ctx) {
        this.ctx = ctx;
        /** @type {{ prefix: string, handler: Function }[]} */
        this.routes = [];
    }

    /**
     * ÄÄƒng kÃ½ má»™t nhÃ³m nÃºt theo tiá»n tá»‘.
     * @param {string} prefix - Tiá»n tá»‘ customId
     * @param {(interaction: import('discord.js').ButtonInteraction, ctx: object, customId: string) => Promise<void>} handler
     */
    register(prefix, handler) {
        this.routes.push({ prefix, handler });
    }

    /**
     * Xá»­ lÃ½ má»™t tÆ°Æ¡ng tÃ¡c nÃºt báº¥m.
     * @param {import('discord.js').ButtonInteraction} interaction
     */
    async dispatch(interaction) {
        const customId = interaction.customId;
        const route = this.routes.find((r) => customId.startsWith(r.prefix));
        if (!route) return;

        try {
            await route.handler(interaction, this.ctx, customId);
        } catch (err) {
            this.ctx.logger.error(`Lá»—i khi xá»­ lÃ½ nÃºt "${customId}":`, err);
            await respondError(interaction, err);
        }
    }
}

module.exports = ButtonHandler;

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\core\CommandHandler.js ===
/**
 * CommandHandler â€” Sá»• Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ slash command + lá»›p kiá»ƒm tra dÃ¹ng chung.
 *
 * Nhá» `meta`, má»—i command khÃ´ng cÃ²n pháº£i láº·p láº¡i cÃ¡c bÆ°á»›c:
 * kiá»ƒm tra voice â†’ kiá»ƒm tra player â†’ deferReply â†’ try/catch.
 *
 * Äá»‹nh dáº¡ng má»™t module command:
 * ```js
 * module.exports = {
 *   definitions: [{ name: 'play', description: '...', options: [...] }],
 *   meta: {
 *     requiresVoice: true,   // ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng pháº£i á»Ÿ trong kÃªnh thoáº¡i
 *     requiresPlayer: true,  // pháº£i cÃ³ player trong guild
 *     requiresPlaying: true, // pháº£i Ä‘ang phÃ¡t hoáº·c Ä‘ang táº¡m dá»«ng
 *     sameVoice: true,       // pháº£i á»Ÿ cÃ¹ng kÃªnh thoáº¡i vá»›i bot
 *     defer: 'ephemeral',    // 'ephemeral' | 'public' | false
 *     cooldown: 3000         // ms, máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh láº¥y tá»« constants
 *   },
 *   execute: async (interaction, ctx) => { ... }   // 1 definition
 *   // hoáº·c
 *   handlers: { skip: fn, skipto: fn }             // nhiá»u definition
 * };
 * ```
 */

const { MessageFlags } = require('discord.js');
const { respondEphemeral, respondError } = require('../utils/reply');

class CommandHandler {
    /**
     * @param {object} ctx - Ngá»¯ cáº£nh bot
     */
    constructor(ctx) {
        this.ctx = ctx;
        /** @type {Map<string, { meta: object, execute: Function, module: string }>} */
        this.registry = new Map();
        /** @type {object[]} */
        this.definitions = [];
    }

    /**
     * ÄÄƒng kÃ½ má»™t module command.
     * @param {object} commandModule
     */
    register(commandModule) {
        const { definitions = [], meta = {}, execute, handlers } = commandModule;

        if (!definitions.length) {
            this.ctx.logger.warn('Bá» qua module command khÃ´ng cÃ³ definitions.');
            return;
        }

        const moduleName = definitions[0].name;

        for (const definition of definitions) {
            const fn = handlers && handlers[definition.name] ? handlers[definition.name] : execute;
            if (typeof fn !== 'function') {
                this.ctx.logger.warn(`Command /${definition.name} khÃ´ng cÃ³ hÃ m execute, Ä‘Ã£ bá» qua.`);
                continue;
            }
            this.registry.set(definition.name, { meta, execute: fn, module: moduleName });
        }

        this.definitions.push(...definitions);
    }

    /**
     * ÄÄƒng kÃ½ nhiá»u module cÃ¹ng lÃºc.
     * @param {object[]} modules
     */
    registerAll(modules) {
        for (const module of modules) this.register(module);
        this.ctx.logger.info(`ÄÃ£ náº¡p ${this.registry.size} slash command vÃ o bá»™ Ä‘á»‹nh tuyáº¿n.`);
    }

    /** Danh sÃ¡ch definition Ä‘á»ƒ gá»­i lÃªn Discord API. */
    getDefinitions() {
        return this.definitions;
    }

    /**
     * Kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘iá»u kiá»‡n tiÃªn quyáº¿t dá»±a trÃªn `meta`.
     * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
     * @param {object} meta
     * @returns {Promise<{ ok: boolean, player?: object, voiceChannel?: object }>}
     */
    async preflight(interaction, meta) {
        const ctx = this.ctx;
        const { t } = ctx;

        // --- Cooldown ---
        if (meta.cooldown) {
            const result = ctx.cooldown.check(`${interaction.user.id}:${interaction.commandName}`, meta.cooldown);
            if (!result.ok) {
                await respondEphemeral(
                    interaction,
                    t('common.cooldown', { seconds: Math.ceil(result.retryAfterMs / 1000) })
                );
                return { ok: false };
            }
        }

        // --- KÃªnh thoáº¡i ---
        let voiceChannel = null;
        if (meta.requiresVoice || meta.sameVoice) {
            voiceChannel = interaction.member?.voice?.channel || null;
            if (!voiceChannel) {
                await respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.needVoice'));
                return { ok: false };
            }

            if (meta.requiresVoice && voiceChannel.permissionsFor && ctx.client.user) {
                const perms = voiceChannel.permissionsFor(ctx.client.user);
                if (perms) {
                    if (!perms.has('Connect')) {
                        await respondEphemeral(interaction, 'âŒ Bot khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n tham gia kÃªnh thoáº¡i nÃ y (cáº§n quyá»n **Connect**).');
                        return { ok: false };
                    }
                    if (!perms.has('Speak')) {
                        await respondEphemeral(interaction, 'âŒ Bot khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n phÃ¡t Ã¢m thanh trong kÃªnh thoáº¡i nÃ y (cáº§n quyá»n **Speak**).');
                        return { ok: false };
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // --- Player ---
        const player = ctx.manager.players.get(interaction.guild.id) || null;

        if (meta.requiresPlayer && !player) {
            await respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.noPlayer'));
            return { ok: false };
        }

        if (meta.requiresPlaying && !(player && (player.playing || player.paused))) {
            await respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.noPlaying'));
            return { ok: false };
        }

        if (meta.sameVoice && player && player.voiceId && voiceChannel.id !== player.voiceId) {
            await respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.needSameVoice'));
            return { ok: false };
        }

        return { ok: true, player, voiceChannel };
    }

    /**
     * Xá»­ lÃ½ má»™t slash command.
     * @param {import('discord.js').ChatInputCommandInteraction} interaction
     */
    async dispatch(interaction) {
        const entry = this.registry.get(interaction.commandName);
        if (!entry) {
            this.ctx.logger.warn(`KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y handler cho lá»‡nh /${interaction.commandName}.`);
            return;
        }

        const { meta, execute } = entry;
        const ctx = this.ctx;
        const start = Date.now();

        try {
            const pre = await this.preflight(interaction, meta);
            if (!pre.ok) return;

            if (meta.defer === 'ephemeral') {
                await interaction.deferReply({ flags: MessageFlags.Ephemeral });
            } else if (meta.defer === 'public') {
                await interaction.deferReply();
            }

            await execute(interaction, {
                ...ctx,
                player: pre.player,
                voiceChannel: pre.voiceChannel
            });
            ctx.logger.info(`[Command /${interaction.commandName}] hoÃ n thÃ nh sau ${Date.now() - start}ms.`);
        } catch (err) {
            ctx.logger.error(`Lá»—i khi cháº¡y lá»‡nh /${interaction.commandName} (${Date.now() - start}ms):`, err);
            await respondError(interaction, err);
        }
    }
}

module.exports = CommandHandler;

/**
 * context.js â€” Äá»‘i tÆ°á»£ng ngá»¯ cáº£nh dÃ¹ng chung, Ä‘Æ°á»£c truyá»n vÃ o má»i command/button.
 *
 * Nhá» cÃ³ ctx, cÃ¡c command khÃ´ng cáº§n `require` trá»±c tiáº¿p service ná»¯a â€” giÃºp
 * dá»… kiá»ƒm thá»­ (cÃ³ thá»ƒ truyá»n service giáº£) vÃ  trÃ¡nh phá»¥ thuá»™c vÃ²ng.
 */

const { t } = require('../locales');
const { createLogger } = require('../services/logger');
const cooldown = require('../services/cooldown/CooldownService');
const queueService = require('../services/queue/QueueService');

/**
 * Táº¡o Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng ngá»¯ cáº£nh.

 *   playerService: object,
 *   storage?: object,

function createContext(deps) {

        client: deps.client,
        manager: deps.manager,
        playerService: deps.playerService,
        storage: deps.storage || null,
        voicePresence: deps.voicePresence || null,
        cooldown,
        queueService,
        logger: deps.logger || createLogger('Bot'),
        t
    };
}

/**
 * SelectHandler â€” Äá»‹nh tuyáº¿n menu chá»n (StringSelectMenu) theo customId chÃ­nh xÃ¡c.
 */

const { respondError } = require('../utils/reply');

class SelectHandler {
    /**
     * @param {object} ctx - Ngá»¯ cáº£nh bot
     */
    constructor(ctx) {
        this.ctx = ctx;
        /** @type {Map<string, Function>} */
        this.registry = new Map();
    }

    /**
     * ÄÄƒng kÃ½ má»™t menu chá»n.
     * @param {string} customId
     * @param {(interaction: import('discord.js').StringSelectMenuInteraction, ctx: object) => Promise<void>} handler
     */
    register(customId, handler) {
        this.registry.set(customId, handler);
    }

    /**
     * Xá»­ lÃ½ má»™t tÆ°Æ¡ng tÃ¡c menu chá»n.
     * @param {import('discord.js').StringSelectMenuInteraction} interaction
     */
    async dispatch(interaction) {
        const handler = this.registry.get(interaction.customId);
        if (!handler) return;

        try {
            await handler(interaction, this.ctx);
        } catch (err) {
            this.ctx.logger.error(`Lá»—i khi xá»­ lÃ½ menu "${interaction.customId}":`, err);
            await respondError(interaction, err);
        }
    }
}

module.exports = SelectHandler;

/**
 * filterSelect â€” Xá»­ lÃ½ menu chá»n bá»™ lá»c Ã¢m thanh trÃªn báº£ng Now Playing.
 */

const { t } = require('../locales');
const NowPlayingUI = require('../services/ui/NowPlayingUI');
const { getState } = require('../services/player/PlayerState');
const { respondEphemeral } = require('../utils/reply');
const { isEphemeralMessage } = require('../utils/interactions');
const { refreshPanel } = require('./nowPlayingButtons');

/**
 * Xá»­ lÃ½ khi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng chá»n má»™t bá»™ lá»c.
 * @param {import('discord.js').StringSelectMenuInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx - Ngá»¯ cáº£nh bot
 */
async function handle(interaction, ctx) {
    const player = ctx.manager.players.get(interaction.guild.id);
    if (!player) {
        return respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.noPlayer'));
    }

    const memberVoice = interaction.member?.voice?.channel;
    if (!memberVoice || memberVoice.id !== player.voiceId) {
        return respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.needSameVoice'));
    }

    const name = interaction.values[0];
    const state = getState(interaction.guild.id);

    // Gia háº¡n token náº¿u báº£ng lÃ  ephemeral
    if (isEphemeralMessage(interaction.message)) {
        ctx.playerService.registerEphemeralContext(interaction, player);
    }
    state.touchPanel();

    await interaction.deferUpdate();

    const result = await ctx.playerService.setFilter(player, name);
    if (!result.ok) {
        await respondEphemeral(interaction, t('commands.filter.invalid', { name }));
        return;
    }

    await refreshPanel(interaction, player, state);
}

module.exports = { handle, customId: NowPlayingUI.FILTER_SELECT_ID };

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\interactions\nowPlayingButtons.js ===
/**
 * nowPlayingButtons â€” Xá»­ lÃ½ toÃ n bá»™ nÃºt trÃªn báº£ng Now Playing (tiá»n tá»‘ `np_`).
 *
 * Báº£ng cÃ³ thá»ƒ lÃ  ephemeral (chá»‰ ngÆ°á»i gá»i lá»‡nh tháº¥y) hoáº·c cÃ´ng khai (do
 * `/nowplaying` táº¡o). Má»i nÃºt Ä‘á»u yÃªu cáº§u ngÆ°á»i báº¥m á»Ÿ cÃ¹ng kÃªnh thoáº¡i vá»›i bot.
 */

const { MessageFlags } = require('discord.js');

const { t } = require('../locales');
const NowPlayingUI = require('../services/ui/NowPlayingUI');
const QueueUI = require('../services/ui/QueueUI');
const { getState } = require('../services/player/PlayerState');
const { respondEphemeral } = require('../utils/reply');
const { isEphemeralMessage } = require('../utils/interactions');

/** BÆ°á»›c thay Ä‘á»•i Ã¢m lÆ°á»£ng má»—i láº§n báº¥m nÃºt */
const VOLUME_STEP = 10;

/** Giá»›i háº¡n Ã¢m lÆ°á»£ng */
const VOLUME_MIN = 1;
const VOLUME_MAX = 200;

/**
 * Váº½ láº¡i báº£ng Now Playing ngay trÃªn tin nháº¯n chá»©a nÃºt vá»«a báº¥m.
 * @param {import('discord.js').ButtonInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
 * @param {import('../services/player/PlayerState').PlayerState} state
 */
async function refreshPanel(interaction, player, state) {
    const track = player.queue.current;
    if (!track) return;

    const { embed, components } = NowPlayingUI.create(player, track, state);
    await interaction
        .editReply({
            content: state.panelNote || '',
            embeds: [embed],
            components
        })
        .catch(() => {});
}

/**
 * Xá»­ lÃ½ nÃºt báº¥m trÃªn báº£ng Now Playing.
 * @param {import('discord.js').ButtonInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx - Ngá»¯ cáº£nh bot
 * @param {string} customId
 */
async function handle(interaction, ctx, customId) {
    const player = ctx.manager.players.get(interaction.guild.id);

    // NÃºt dá»«ng luÃ´n Ä‘Æ°á»£c phÃ©p thá»±c thi ngay láº­p tá»©c Ä‘á»ƒ giáº£i phÃ³ng tÃ i nguyÃªn
    if (customId === 'np_stop') {
        if (player) {
            await ctx.playerService.stop(player);
        } else {
            try { await ctx.manager.destroyPlayer(interaction.guild.id); } catch (_) {}
            try { await ctx.manager.shoukaku.leaveVoiceChannel(interaction.guild.id); } catch (_) {}
            try {
                const guild = ctx.client.guilds.cache.get(interaction.guild.id);
                if (guild?.members?.me?.voice?.channelId) {
                    await guild.members.me.voice.disconnect();
                }
            } catch (_) {}
        }
        const embed = NowPlayingUI.createStopped('stopped');
        return interaction.update({ content: '', embeds: [embed], components: [] }).catch(() => {
            return interaction.editReply({ content: '', embeds: [embed], components: [] }).catch(() => {});
        });
    }

    // Player Ä‘Ã£ cháº¿t hoáº·c khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i
    if (!player) {
        return respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.noPlayer'));
    }

    const memberVoice = interaction.member?.voice?.channel;
    if (!memberVoice || (player.voiceId && memberVoice.id !== player.voiceId)) {
        return respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.needSameVoice'));
    }

    const state = getState(interaction.guild.id);

    // Báº£ng ephemeral: má»—i láº§n báº¥m nÃºt táº¡o má»™t token má»›i (15 phÃºt) â†’
    // gia háº¡n Ä‘á»ƒ ticker tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh tiáº¿p tá»¥c cáº­p nháº­t Ä‘Æ°á»£c báº£ng nÃ y.
    if (isEphemeralMessage(interaction.message)) {
        ctx.playerService.registerEphemeralContext(interaction, player);
    }
    state.touchPanel();

    await interaction.deferUpdate();

    switch (customId) {
        case 'np_previous': {
            const result = await ctx.playerService.previous(player);
            if (!result.ok) {
                await respondEphemeral(interaction, t(result.error));
                return;
            }
            await refreshPanel(interaction, player, state);
            return;
        }

        case 'np_pause_resume': {
            if (player.paused) ctx.playerService.resume(player);
            else ctx.playerService.pause(player);
            await refreshPanel(interaction, player, state);
            return;
        }

        case 'np_skip': {
            const ok = ctx.playerService.skip(player);
            if (!ok) await respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.noPlaying'));
            return;
        }

        case 'np_repeat': {
            state.cycleRepeatMode();
            ctx.playerService.persistState(interaction.guild.id, state, player.volume);
            await refreshPanel(interaction, player, state);
            return;
        }

        case 'np_shuffle': {
            if (player.queue.length > 1) {
                const count = ctx.queueService.shuffle(player);
                await respondEphemeral(interaction, t('commands.shuffle.done', { count }));
            } else {
                await respondEphemeral(interaction, t('commands.shuffle.needMore'));
            }
            return;
        }

        case 'np_vol_down':
        case 'np_vol_up': {
            const delta = customId === 'np_vol_up' ? VOLUME_STEP : -VOLUME_STEP;
            const newVolume = Math.max(VOLUME_MIN, Math.min(VOLUME_MAX, (player.volume || 100) + delta));
            await player.setVolume(newVolume);
            ctx.playerService.persistState(interaction.guild.id, state, newVolume);
            await refreshPanel(interaction, player, state);
            return;
        }

        case 'np_autoplay': {
            ctx.playerService.setAutoplay(player, !state.autoplay);
            await refreshPanel(interaction, player, state);
            return;
        }

        case 'np_queue': {
            const { embed, components } = QueueUI.create(player, 1);
            await interaction.followUp({ embeds: [embed], components, flags: MessageFlags.Ephemeral });
            return;
        }

        case 'np_stop': {
            await ctx.playerService.stop(player);
            const embed = NowPlayingUI.createStopped('stopped');
            await interaction.editReply({ content: '', embeds: [embed], components: [] }).catch(() => {});
            return;
        }

        default:
            ctx.logger.debug(`NÃºt "${customId}" chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c xá»­ lÃ½.`);
    }
}

module.exports = { handle, refreshPanel, prefix: 'np_' };

/**
 * queueButtons â€” Xá»­ lÃ½ nÃºt phÃ¢n trang cá»§a báº£ng hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i (tiá»n tá»‘ `queue_`).

const QueueUI = require('../services/ui/QueueUI');
const { t } = require('../locales');
const { respondEphemeral } = require('../utils/reply');

/** customId cá»§a nÃºt chá»‰ hiá»ƒn thá»‹ sá»‘ trang (khÃ´ng cÃ³ hÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng) */
const PAGE_INDICATOR = 'queue_page_indicator';

/**
 * Xá»­ lÃ½ nÃºt phÃ¢n trang hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i.
 * @param {import('discord.js').ButtonInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx - Ngá»¯ cáº£nh bot
 * @param {string} customId

        return respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.noPlayer'));

    await interaction.deferUpdate();

    const targetPage = QueueUI.parsePageFromButton(customId);
    const { embed, components } = QueueUI.create(player, targetPage);

}

/**
 * searchSelect â€” Xá»­ lÃ½ menu chá»n bÃ i hÃ¡t cá»§a lá»‡nh /search.
 */

const { t } = require('../locales');
const SearchUI = require('../services/ui/SearchUI');
const { respondEphemeral } = require('../utils/reply');

/**
 * Xá»­ lÃ½ khi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng chá»n má»™t bÃ i trong menu /search.
 * @param {import('discord.js').StringSelectMenuInteraction} interaction
 * @param {object} ctx - Ngá»¯ cáº£nh bot
 */
async function handle(interaction, ctx) {
    const guildId = interaction.guild.id;
    const userId = interaction.user.id;

    const tracks = SearchUI.getResults(guildId, userId);
    if (!tracks) {
        await interaction.deferUpdate();
        await interaction
            .editReply({
                content: t('commands.search.expired'),
                embeds: [],
                components: []
            })
            .catch(() => {});
        return;
    }

    const index = Number.parseInt(interaction.values[0], 10);
    const track = tracks[index];
    if (!track) {
        return respondEphemeral(interaction, t('commands.search.expired'));
    }

    const memberVoice = interaction.member?.voice?.channel;
    if (!memberVoice) {
        return respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.needVoice'));
    }

    const existing = ctx.manager.players.get(guildId);
    if (existing && existing.voiceId && existing.voiceId !== memberVoice.id) {
        return respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.needSameVoice'));
    }

    await interaction.deferUpdate();

    try {
        const player = await ctx.playerService.getOrCreatePlayer({
            guildId,
            textId: interaction.channelId,
            voiceId: memberVoice.id
        });

        // Biáº¿n chÃ­nh tin nháº¯n chá»©a menu thÃ nh báº£ng Now Playing dáº¡ng ephemeral
        ctx.playerService.registerEphemeralContext(interaction, player);

        const result = await ctx.playerService.enqueue(player, track, { requesterId: userId });

        if (!result.ok) {
            const key = result.error === 'userQueueFull' ? 'common.userQueueFull' : 'common.queueFull';
            const vars = result.vars || { max: 0 };
            await interaction.editReply({ content: t(key, vars), embeds: [], components: [] }).catch(() => {});
            return;
        }

        SearchUI.clearResults(guildId, userId);

        await ctx.playerService.renderEphemeralPanel(
            player,
            player.queue.current || track,
            t('commands.search.added', { title: track.title })
        );
    } catch (err) {
        ctx.logger.error('Lá»—i khi phÃ¡t bÃ i tá»« menu /search:', err);
        await respondEphemeral(interaction, t('common.loadError', { message: err.message }));
    }
}

module.exports = { handle, customId: SearchUI.SELECT_ID };

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\locales\index.js ===
/**
 * i18n â€” Bá»™ dá»‹ch chuá»—i giao diá»‡n.
 *
 * CÃ¡ch dÃ¹ng:
 *   const { t } = require('../locales');
 *   t('commands.play.added', { title: 'BÃ i hÃ¡t' });
 *
 * Náº¿u thiáº¿u khoÃ¡, hÃ m tráº£ vá» chÃ­nh khoÃ¡ Ä‘Ã³ (kÃ¨m cáº£nh bÃ¡o á»Ÿ má»©c debug) Ä‘á»ƒ
 * bot khÃ´ng bao giá» hiá»ƒn thá»‹ `undefined` cho ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng.
 */

const vi = require('./vi.json');

/** Tá»« Ä‘iá»ƒn theo mÃ£ ngÃ´n ngá»¯ */
const dictionaries = { vi };

/** NgÃ´n ngá»¯ máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh, cÃ³ thá»ƒ Ä‘á»•i báº±ng biáº¿n mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng */
const DEFAULT_LOCALE = dictionaries[process.env.DEFAULT_LOCALE] ? process.env.DEFAULT_LOCALE : 'vi';

/**
 * Tra má»™t khoÃ¡ dáº¡ng "a.b.c" trong object lá»“ng nhau.
 * @param {object} source
 * @param {string} key
 * @returns {unknown}
 */
function lookup(source, key) {
    return key.split('.').reduce((node, part) => {
        if (node && typeof node === 'object' && part in node) return node[part];
        return undefined;
    }, source);
}

/**
 * Thay cÃ¡c biáº¿n dáº¡ng `{ten}` trong chuá»—i báº±ng giÃ¡ trá»‹ tÆ°Æ¡ng á»©ng.
 * @param {string} template
 * @param {Record<string, unknown>} vars
 * @returns {string}
 */
function interpolate(template, vars) {
    if (!vars) return template;
    return template.replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (match, name) => {
        return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(vars, name) ? String(vars[name]) : match;
    });
}

/**
 * Dá»‹ch má»™t khoÃ¡ sang chuá»—i hiá»ƒn thá»‹.
 * @param {string} key - KhoÃ¡ dáº¡ng "commands.play.added"
 * @param {Record<string, unknown>} [vars] - Biáº¿n ná»™i suy
 * @param {string} [locale] - MÃ£ ngÃ´n ngá»¯, máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh láº¥y tá»« biáº¿n mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng
 * @returns {string}
 */
function t(key, vars = {}, locale = DEFAULT_LOCALE) {
    const dict = dictionaries[locale] || dictionaries[DEFAULT_LOCALE];
    let value = lookup(dict, key);

    // Fallback sang tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t náº¿u ngÃ´n ngá»¯ Ä‘ang dÃ¹ng thiáº¿u khoÃ¡
    if (value === undefined && locale !== DEFAULT_LOCALE) {
        value = lookup(dictionaries[DEFAULT_LOCALE], key);
    }

    if (typeof value !== 'string') return key;

    return interpolate(value, vars);
}

/**
 * Danh sÃ¡ch ngÃ´n ngá»¯ Ä‘ang há»— trá»£.
 * @returns {string[]}
 */
function getAvailableLocales() {
    return Object.keys(dictionaries);
}

module.exports = { t, getAvailableLocales, DEFAULT_LOCALE, dictionaries };
