=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\services\logger.js ===
/**
 * Logger â€” Ghi log cÃ³ cáº¥u trÃºc, chia theo má»©c Ä‘á»™ vÃ  pháº¡m vi (scope).
 *
 * KhÃ´ng phá»¥ thuá»™c thÆ° viá»‡n ngoÃ i Ä‘á»ƒ bot luÃ´n cháº¡y Ä‘Æ°á»£c ká»ƒ cáº£ khi cÃ i thiáº¿u gÃ³i.
 * - MÃ´i trÆ°á»ng production (NODE_ENV=production): xuáº¥t JSON má»™t dÃ²ng â†’ dá»… thu tháº­p log.
 * - MÃ´i trÆ°á»ng khÃ¡c: xuáº¥t dáº¡ng ngÆ°á»i Ä‘á»c Ä‘Æ°á»£c, cÃ³ mÃ u.
 *
 * CÃ³ thá»ƒ gáº¯n thÃªm bá»™ thu tháº­p lá»—i bÃªn ngoÃ i (vÃ­ dá»¥ Sentry) qua `attachErrorReporter`.
 */

/** Thá»© tá»± Æ°u tiÃªn cá»§a cÃ¡c má»©c log (sá»‘ nhá» = nghiÃªm trá»ng hÆ¡n) */
const LEVELS = {
    error: 0,
    warn: 1,
    info: 2,
    debug: 3
};

/** MÃ u ANSI cho tá»«ng má»©c log */
const COLORS = {
    error: '\x1b[31m',
    warn: '\x1b[33m',
    info: '\x1b[36m',
    debug: '\x1b[90m'
};

const RESET = '\x1b[0m';
const DIM = '\x1b[2m';

const isProduction = process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';

/** Má»©c log tá»‘i thiá»ƒu Ä‘Æ°á»£c in ra */
const minLevel = LEVELS[String(process.env.LOG_LEVEL || 'info').toLowerCase()] ?? LEVELS.info;

/**
 * HÃ m thu tháº­p lá»—i bÃªn ngoÃ i (Sentry, ...). Máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh khÃ´ng lÃ m gÃ¬.
 * @type {(error: Error, context: object) => void}
 */
let errorReporter = () => {};

/**
 * Gáº¯n bá»™ thu tháº­p lá»—i bÃªn ngoÃ i (tÃ¹y chá»n).
 * @param {(error: Error, context: object) => void} reporter
 */
function attachErrorReporter(reporter) {
    if (typeof reporter === 'function') errorReporter = reporter;
}

/**
 * Chuáº©n hoÃ¡ Ä‘á»‘i sá»‘ log thÃ nh object cÃ³ thá»ƒ tuáº§n tá»± hoÃ¡.
 * @param {unknown[]} args
 * @returns {{ message: string, meta: object }}
 */
function normalise(args) {
    const parts = [];
    const meta = {};

    for (const arg of args) {
        if (arg instanceof Error) {
            parts.push(arg.message);
            meta.err = { name: arg.name, message: arg.message, stack: arg.stack };
        } else if (arg && typeof arg === 'object') {
            Object.assign(meta, arg);
        } else {
            parts.push(String(arg));
        }
    }

    return { message: parts.join(' '), meta };
}

/**
 * Ghi má»™t dÃ²ng log.
 * @param {'error'|'warn'|'info'|'debug'} level
 * @param {string} scope
 * @param {unknown[]} args
 */
function write(level, scope, args) {
    if (LEVELS[level] > minLevel) return;

    const { message, meta } = normalise(args);

    if (level === 'error') {
        try {
            errorReporter(meta.err ? Object.assign(new Error(meta.err.message), meta.err) : new Error(message), {
                scope,
                ...meta
            });
        } catch {
            /* Bá»™ thu tháº­p lá»—i khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c phÃ©p lÃ m sáº­p bot */
        }
    }

    const time = new Date().toISOString();

    if (isProduction) {
        const record = { time, level, scope, message, ...meta };
        const line = JSON.stringify(record);
        if (level === 'error') process.stderr.write(line + '\n');
        else process.stdout.write(line + '\n');
        return;
    }

    const color = COLORS[level] || '';
    const tag = level.toUpperCase().padEnd(5);
    const extraKeys = Object.keys(meta).filter((k) => k !== 'err');
    const extra = extraKeys.length
        ? ' ' +
          DIM +
          JSON.stringify(
              extraKeys.reduce((acc, k) => {
                  acc[k] = meta[k];
                  return acc;
              }, {})
          ) +
          RESET
        : '';
    const stack = meta.err && level === 'error' ? '\n' + meta.err.stack : '';
    const line = `${DIM}${time}${RESET} ${color}${tag}${RESET} ${DIM}[${scope}]${RESET} ${message}${extra}${stack}`;

    if (level === 'error') process.stderr.write(line + '\n');
    else process.stdout.write(line + '\n');
}

/**
 * Táº¡o logger cho má»™t pháº¡m vi cá»¥ thá»ƒ.
 * @param {string} scope - TÃªn module, vÃ­ dá»¥ "PlayerService"
 * @returns {{ error: Function, warn: Function, info: Function, debug: Function, child: (sub: string) => object }}
 */
function createLogger(scope) {
    return {
        error: (...args) => write('error', scope, args),
        warn: (...args) => write('warn', scope, args),
        info: (...args) => write('info', scope, args),
        debug: (...args) => write('debug', scope, args),
        /** Táº¡o logger con vá»›i pháº¡m vi ná»‘i tiáº¿p, vÃ­ dá»¥ "PlayerService:play" */
        child: (sub) => createLogger(`${scope}:${sub}`)
    };
}

module.exports = {
    createLogger,
    attachErrorReporter,
    LEVELS,
    /** Logger gá»‘c khÃ´ng gáº¯n pháº¡m vi */
    root: createLogger('bot')
};

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\services\autoplay\AutoplayService.js ===
/**
 * AutoplayService â€” Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng tÃ¬m vÃ  phÃ¡t bÃ i hÃ¡t liÃªn quan khi hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i háº¿t bÃ i.
 *
 * CÃ¡ch hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng: láº¥y bÃ i vá»«a phÃ¡t lÃ m "háº¡t giá»‘ng", tÃ¬m trÃªn YouTube theo vÃ i
 * máº«u truy váº¥n (mix / radio / tÃªn bÃ i + nghá»‡ sÄ©), rá»“i chá»n káº¿t quáº£ Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn
 * chÆ°a tá»«ng phÃ¡t gáº§n Ä‘Ã¢y Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh láº·p láº¡i.
 */

const { createLogger } = require('../logger');

const log = createLogger('AutoplayService');

/** CÃ¡c máº«u truy váº¥n, thá»­ láº§n lÆ°á»£t cho tá»›i khi cÃ³ káº¿t quáº£ phÃ¹ há»£p */
const QUERY_TEMPLATES = [
    (seed) => `${seed.author} mix`,
    (seed) => `${seed.author} radio`,
    (seed) => `${seed.title} ${seed.author}`
];

/** Láº¥y khoÃ¡ Ä‘á»‹nh danh duy nháº¥t cá»§a má»™t track */
function trackKey(track) {
    if (!track) return '';
    return track.identifier || track.uri || `${track.title}|${track.author}`;
}

/**
 * Chá»n bÃ i hÃ¡t chÆ°a tá»«ng phÃ¡t trong danh sÃ¡ch káº¿t quáº£.
 * HÃ m thuáº§n, tÃ¡ch riÃªng Ä‘á»ƒ dá»… kiá»ƒm thá»­.
 * @param {object[]} tracks - Danh sÃ¡ch track á»©ng viÃªn
 * @param {Set<string>} excludedKeys - CÃ¡c khoÃ¡ Ä‘á»‹nh danh cáº§n loáº¡i trá»«
 * @returns {object|null} Track Ä‘Æ°á»£c chá»n hoáº·c null
 */
function pickRelated(tracks, excludedKeys) {
    if (!Array.isArray(tracks) || !tracks.length) return null;
    for (const track of tracks) {
        if (!track) continue;
        if (track.isStream) continue; // Bá» qua livestream Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh phÃ¡t vÃ´ táº­n
        if (!excludedKeys.has(trackKey(track))) return track;
    }
    return null;
}

class AutoplayService {
    /**
     * @param {import('kazagumo').Kazagumo} manager - Kazagumo manager
     */
    constructor(manager) {
        this.manager = manager;
    }

    /**
     * TÃ¬m má»™t bÃ i hÃ¡t liÃªn quan tá»›i bÃ i vá»«a phÃ¡t.
     * @param {object} seedTrack - BÃ i hÃ¡t vá»«a phÃ¡t xong
     * @param {Set<string>|string[]} excluded - KhoÃ¡ Ä‘á»‹nh danh cÃ¡c bÃ i cáº§n trÃ¡nh
     * @param {object} [requester] - NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c gÃ¡n lÃ m ngÆ°á»i yÃªu cáº§u
     * @returns {Promise<object|null>} Track tÃ¬m Ä‘Æ°á»£c hoáº·c null
     */
    async findRelated(seedTrack, excluded, requester) {
        if (!seedTrack) return null;

        const excludedKeys = excluded instanceof Set ? excluded : new Set(excluded || []);
        excludedKeys.add(trackKey(seedTrack));

        for (const buildQuery of QUERY_TEMPLATES) {
            const query = buildQuery(seedTrack);
            if (!query || !query.trim()) continue;

            try {
                const result = await this.manager.search(query, { requester });
                const picked = pickRelated(result?.tracks, excludedKeys);
                if (picked) {
                    log.debug(`Autoplay chá»n "${picked.title}" tá»« truy váº¥n "${query}"`);
                    return picked;
                }
            } catch (err) {
                log.warn(`Autoplay khÃ´ng tÃ¬m Ä‘Æ°á»£c káº¿t quáº£ cho "${query}":`, err.message);
            }
        }

        return null;
    }
}

module.exports = AutoplayService;
module.exports.pickRelated = pickRelated;
module.exports.trackKey = trackKey;

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\services\cache\SearchCache.js ===
/**
 * SearchCache â€” Cache káº¿t quáº£ tÃ¬m kiáº¿m theo thuáº­t toÃ¡n LRU kÃ¨m thá»i gian sá»‘ng (TTL).
 *
 * Má»¥c Ä‘Ã­ch: ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng hay tÃ¬m láº¡i cÃ¹ng má»™t bÃ i/playlist, cache giÃºp pháº£n há»“i
 * tá»©c thÃ¬ vÃ  giáº£m táº£i cho cÃ¡c node Lavalink.
 *
 * DÃ¹ng Map (giá»¯ thá»© tá»± chÃ¨n) lÃ m danh sÃ¡ch LRU: khoÃ¡ vá»«a Ä‘á»c sáº½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘Æ°a vá» cuá»‘i.
 */

class SearchCache {
    /**
     * @param {{ max?: number, ttlMs?: number }} [options]
     */
    constructor({ max = 200, ttlMs = 5 * 60 * 1000 } = {}) {
        this.max = Math.max(1, max);
        this.ttlMs = Math.max(1000, ttlMs);
        /** @type {Map<string, { value: unknown, expiresAt: number }>} */
        this.store = new Map();
        this.hits = 0;
        this.misses = 0;
    }

    /**
     * Chuáº©n hoÃ¡ khoÃ¡ cache Ä‘á»ƒ "BÃ i HÃ¡t" vÃ  "bÃ i hÃ¡t" dÃ¹ng chung má»™t má»¥c.
     * @param {string} query
     * @param {string} [engine]
     * @returns {string}
     */
    static normaliseKey(query, engine = 'default') {
        return `${engine}:${String(query).trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ')}`;
    }

    /**
     * Láº¥y giÃ¡ trá»‹ trong cache.
     * @param {string} key
     * @returns {unknown|undefined} `undefined` náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ³ hoáº·c Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n
     */
    get(key) {
        const entry = this.store.get(key);
        if (!entry) {
            this.misses++;
            return undefined;
        }

        if (entry.expiresAt <= Date.now()) {
            this.store.delete(key);
            this.misses++;
            return undefined;
        }

        // ÄÆ°a lÃªn vá»‹ trÃ­ má»›i nháº¥t (LRU)
        this.store.delete(key);
        this.store.set(key, entry);
        this.hits++;
        return entry.value;
    }

    /**
     * LÆ°u giÃ¡ trá»‹ vÃ o cache.
     * @param {string} key
     * @param {unknown} value
     */
    set(key, value) {
        if (this.store.has(key)) this.store.delete(key);
        this.store.set(key, { value, expiresAt: Date.now() + this.ttlMs });

        // Loáº¡i bá» cÃ¡c má»¥c cÅ© nháº¥t khi vÆ°á»£t ngÆ°á»¡ng
        while (this.store.size > this.max) {
            const oldest = this.store.keys().next();
            if (oldest.done) break;
            this.store.delete(oldest.value);
        }
    }

    /**
     * XoÃ¡ cÃ¡c má»¥c Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n.
     * @returns {number} Sá»‘ má»¥c Ä‘Ã£ xoÃ¡
     */
    prune() {
        const now = Date.now();
        let removed = 0;
        for (const [key, entry] of this.store) {
            if (entry.expiresAt <= now) {
                this.store.delete(key);
                removed++;
            }
        }
        return removed;
    }

    /** XoÃ¡ toÃ n bá»™ cache. */
    clear() {
        this.store.clear();
        this.hits = 0;
        this.misses = 0;
    }

    /** Sá»‘ má»¥c Ä‘ang lÆ°u. */
    get size() {
        return this.store.size;
    }

    /** Thá»‘ng kÃª hiá»‡u quáº£ cache. */
    get stats() {
        const total = this.hits + this.misses;
        return {
            size: this.store.size,
            max: this.max,
            hits: this.hits,
            misses: this.misses,
            hitRate: total === 0 ? 0 : Number((this.hits / total).toFixed(3))
        };
    }
}

module.exports = SearchCache;

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\services\cooldown\CooldownService.js ===
/**
 * CooldownService â€” Chá»‘ng spam lá»‡nh báº±ng cooldown theo (ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng, lá»‡nh).
 *
 * LÆ°u trong bá»™ nhá»› vá»›i cÆ¡ cháº¿ dá»n rÃ¡c lÆ°á»i (lazy sweep) Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng phÃ¬nh to
 * khi bot cháº¡y lÃ¢u ngÃ y.
 */

/** NgÆ°á»¡ng sá»‘ khoÃ¡ tá»‘i Ä‘a trÆ°á»›c khi kÃ­ch hoáº¡t dá»n rÃ¡c */
const SWEEP_THRESHOLD = 1000;

class CooldownService {
    constructor() {
        /** @type {Map<string, number>} KhoÃ¡ â†’ thá»i Ä‘iá»ƒm háº¿t cooldown (epoch ms) */
        this.entries = new Map();
    }

    /**
     * Kiá»ƒm tra vÃ  Ä‘áº·t cooldown cho má»™t khoÃ¡.
     * @param {string} key - KhoÃ¡ Ä‘á»‹nh danh, vÃ­ dá»¥ `${userId}:play`
     * @param {number} durationMs - Thá»i gian cooldown (ms)
     * @returns {{ ok: boolean, retryAfterMs: number }} `ok=false` nghÄ©a lÃ  Ä‘ang trong cooldown
     */
    check(key, durationMs) {
        if (!durationMs || durationMs <= 0) return { ok: true, retryAfterMs: 0 };

        const now = Date.now();

        if (this.entries.size > SWEEP_THRESHOLD) this.sweep(now);

        const expiresAt = this.entries.get(key);
        if (expiresAt && expiresAt > now) {
            return { ok: false, retryAfterMs: expiresAt - now };
        }

        this.entries.set(key, now + durationMs);
        return { ok: true, retryAfterMs: 0 };
    }

    /**
     * XoÃ¡ cooldown cá»§a má»™t khoÃ¡ (dÃ¹ng khi muá»‘n miá»…n pháº¡t, vÃ­ dá»¥ ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng cÃ³ role DJ).
     * @param {string} key
     */
    reset(key) {
        this.entries.delete(key);
    }

    /**
     * Dá»n cÃ¡c khoÃ¡ Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n.
     * @param {number} [now]
     * @returns {number} Sá»‘ khoÃ¡ Ä‘Ã£ xoÃ¡
     */
    sweep(now = Date.now()) {
        let removed = 0;
        for (const [key, expiresAt] of this.entries) {
            if (expiresAt <= now) {
                this.entries.delete(key);
                removed++;
            }
        }
        return removed;
    }

    /** XoÃ¡ toÃ n bá»™ cooldown. */
    clear() {
        this.entries.clear();
    }

    /** Sá»‘ khoÃ¡ Ä‘ang Ä‘Æ°á»£c theo dÃµi. */
    get size() {
        return this.entries.size;
    }
}

module.exports = new CooldownService();
module.exports.CooldownService = CooldownService;

/**
 * FilterService â€” Ãp dá»¥ng cÃ¡c bá»™ lá»c Ã¢m thanh cá»§a Lavalink (bassboost, nightcore, 8D...).
 *
 * Má»—i láº§n Ã¡p dá»¥ng má»™t bá»™ lá»c, ta gá»­i lÃªn Lavalink Má»˜T payload Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ (Ä‘Ã£ null hoÃ¡
 * cÃ¡c bá»™ lá»c khÃ¡c) thay vÃ¬ gá»i `clearFilters()` rá»“i `setFilters()`.
 * LÃ½ do: `clearFilters()` cá»§a Shoukaku Ä‘áº·t luÃ´n `volume = 1`, sáº½ ghi Ä‘Ã¨ Ã¢m lÆ°á»£ng
 * mÃ  Kazagumo Ä‘ang quáº£n lÃ½. Payload Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ giá»¯ Ä‘Ãºng Ã¢m lÆ°á»£ng hiá»‡n táº¡i.
 */

/** Bá»™ Ä‘á»‡m equalizer dÃ¹ng cho cÃ¡c preset cáº§n chá»‰nh dáº£i táº§n */
const BASSBOOST_EQ = [
    { band: 0, gain: 0.6 },
    { band: 1, gain: 0.67 },
    { band: 2, gain: 0.67 },
    { band: 3, gain: 0.0 },
    { band: 4, gain: -0.5 },
    { band: 5, gain: 0.15 },
    { band: 6, gain: -0.45 },
    { band: 7, gain: 0.23 },
    { band: 8, gain: 0.35 },
    { band: 9, gain: 0.45 },
    { band: 10, gain: 0.4 },
    { band: 11, gain: 0.1 },
    { band: 12, gain: 0.0 },
    { band: 13, gain: 0.0 },
    { band: 14, gain: 0.0 }

/**
 * Danh sÃ¡ch preset bá»™ lá»c.
 * `payload` chá»‰ chá»©a cÃ¡c trÆ°á»ng khÃ¡c rá»—ng; cÃ¡c trÆ°á»ng cÃ²n láº¡i sáº½ bá»‹ null hoÃ¡.
 */
const PRESETS = {
    off: {
        label: 'Táº¯t',
        emoji: 'ðŸš«',
        payload: {}
    },
    bassboost: {

            tremolo: { frequency: 2.0, depth: 0.3 }
        }
    },
    '8d': {
        label: '8D Audio',
        emoji: 'ðŸŽ§',

    },
    karaoke: {

        emoji: 'ðŸŽ¤',

    },
    tremolo: {
        label: 'Tremolo',
        emoji: 'ã€°ï¸',
        payload: { tremolo: { frequency: 4.0, depth: 0.75 } }
    },
    vibrato: {
        label: 'Vibrato',
        emoji: 'ðŸ“³',
        payload: { vibrato: { frequency: 4.0, depth: 0.75 } }
    },
    soft: {
        label: 'Soft',
        emoji: 'ðŸ•Šï¸',
        payload: { lowPass: { smoothing: 20.0 } }
    }
};

/** Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i bá»™ lá»c khi khÃ´ng Ã¡p dá»¥ng gÃ¬ */
const DEFAULT_FILTER = 'off';

class FilterService {
    /**
     * Danh sÃ¡ch tÃªn bá»™ lá»c kháº£ dá»¥ng.
     * @returns {string[]}
     */
    static getNames() {
        return Object.keys(PRESETS);
    }

    /**
     * Kiá»ƒm tra tÃªn bá»™ lá»c cÃ³ há»£p lá»‡ khÃ´ng.
     * @param {string} name
     * @returns {boolean}
     */
    static isValid(name) {
        return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(PRESETS, String(name || '').toLowerCase());
    }

    /**
     * Chuáº©n hoÃ¡ tÃªn bá»™ lá»c (viáº¿t thÆ°á»ng, bá» khoáº£ng tráº¯ng).
     * @param {string} name
     * @returns {string}
     */
    static normalise(name) {
        return String(name || '')
            .trim()
            .toLowerCase();
    }

    /**
     * NhÃ£n hiá»ƒn thá»‹ cá»§a má»™t bá»™ lá»c.
     * @param {string} name
     * @returns {string}
     */
    static getLabel(name) {
        const preset = PRESETS[FilterService.normalise(name)];
        return preset ? preset.label : PRESETS[DEFAULT_FILTER].label;
    }

    /**
     * Biá»ƒu tÆ°á»£ng cá»§a má»™t bá»™ lá»c.
     * @param {string} name
     * @returns {string}
     */
    static getEmoji(name) {
        const preset = PRESETS[FilterService.normalise(name)];
        return preset ? preset.emoji : PRESETS[DEFAULT_FILTER].emoji;
    }

    /**
     * Táº¡o payload filter Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§ (Ä‘Ã£ null hoÃ¡ má»i bá»™ lá»c khÃ¡c).
     * @param {string} name - TÃªn bá»™ lá»c
     * @param {number} [volumePercent=100] - Ã‚m lÆ°á»£ng hiá»‡n táº¡i cá»§a player (pháº§n trÄƒm)
     * @returns {object} Payload gá»­i cho `player.setFilters()`
     */
    static buildPayload(name, volumePercent = 100) {
        const preset = PRESETS[FilterService.normalise(name)] || PRESETS[DEFAULT_FILTER];
        const safeVolume = Number.isFinite(volumePercent) && volumePercent > 0 ? volumePercent : 100;

        return {
            volume: safeVolume / 100,
            equalizer: [],
            karaoke: null,
            timescale: null,
            tremolo: null,
            vibrato: null,
            rotation: null,
            distortion: null,
            channelMix: null,
            lowPass: null,
            ...preset.payload
        };
    }

    /**
     * Ãp dá»¥ng bá»™ lá»c lÃªn player.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {string} name - TÃªn bá»™ lá»c
     * @returns {Promise<{ ok: boolean, filter: string, label: string }>}
     */
    static async apply(player, name) {
        const normalised = FilterService.normalise(name);

        if (!FilterService.isValid(normalised)) {
            return { ok: false, filter: DEFAULT_FILTER, label: PRESETS[DEFAULT_FILTER].label };
        }

        const payload = FilterService.buildPayload(normalised, player.volume || 100);
        await player.setFilters(payload);

        return { ok: true, filter: normalised, label: PRESETS[normalised].label };
    }

    /**
     * Táº¯t toÃ n bá»™ bá»™ lá»c.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @returns {Promise<{ ok: boolean, filter: string, label: string }>}
     */
    static async reset(player) {
        await player.setFilters(FilterService.buildPayload(DEFAULT_FILTER, player.volume || 100));
        return { ok: true, filter: DEFAULT_FILTER, label: PRESETS[DEFAULT_FILTER].label };
    }

    /** TÃªn bá»™ lá»c máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh. */
    static get default() {
        return DEFAULT_FILTER;
    }

    /** Báº£ng preset gá»‘c (chá»‰ Ä‘á»c). */
    static get presets() {
        return PRESETS;
    }
}

module.exports = FilterService;

/**
 * PlayerService â€” Äiá»u phá»‘i phÃ¡t nháº¡c, tÃ­ch há»£p Generation Guard.
 *
 * LÃ  lá»›p trung gian giá»¯a commands/buttons vÃ  Kazagumo Player.
 * Má»i hÃ nh Ä‘á»™ng phÃ¡t nháº¡c Ä‘á»u Ä‘i qua service nÃ y.
 *
 * TrÃ¡ch nhiá»‡m chÃ­nh:
 *  - Táº¡o/quáº£n lÃ½ player, Ã¡p dá»¥ng cÃ i Ä‘áº·t Ä‘Ã£ lÆ°u cá»§a guild
 *  - TÃ¬m kiáº¿m cÃ³ cache + fallback cÃ´ng khai (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng API ná»™i bá»™ cá»§a Kazagumo)
 *  - Thá»±c thi giá»›i háº¡n hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i
 *  - Váº½ báº£ng Now Playing dáº¡ng ephemeral / cÃ´ng khai vÃ  tá»± vÃ´ hiá»‡u hoÃ¡ khi háº¿t háº¡n
 *  - Autoplay khi háº¿t hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i
 *  - LÆ°u cÃ i Ä‘áº·t + lá»‹ch sá»­ xuá»‘ng Ä‘Ä©a
 */

const { KazagumoTrack } = require('kazagumo');

const { getState, removeState } = require('./PlayerState');
const queueService = require('../queue/QueueService');
const NowPlayingUI = require('../ui/NowPlayingUI');
const FilterService = require('../filters/FilterService');
const AutoplayService = require('../autoplay/AutoplayService');
const SearchCache = require('../cache/SearchCache');
const { createLogger } = require('../logger');
const {
    SEARCH_TIMEOUT_MS,
    PANEL_TICK_MS,
    DEFAULT_VOLUME,
    SEARCH_CACHE_MAX,
    SEARCH_CACHE_TTL_MS
} = require('../../config/constants');

class PlayerService {
    /**
     * @param {import('discord.js').Client} client - Discord client
     * @param {import('kazagumo').Kazagumo} manager - Kazagumo manager
     * @param {{ storage?: object, searchCache?: SearchCache, autoplayService?: object, logger?: object }} [deps]
     */
    constructor(client, manager, deps = {}) {
        this.client = client;
        this.manager = manager;
        this.log = deps.logger || createLogger('PlayerService');
        this.storage = deps.storage || null;
        this.searchCache = deps.searchCache || new SearchCache({ max: SEARCH_CACHE_MAX, ttlMs: SEARCH_CACHE_TTL_MS });
        this.autoplay = deps.autoplayService || new AutoplayService(manager);
    }

    // ==========================================================
    // Tiá»‡n Ã­ch ná»™i bá»™
    // ==========================================================

    /**
     * Bá»c má»™t promise vá»›i thá»i gian chá» tá»‘i Ä‘a.
     * @template T
     * @param {Promise<T>} promise
     * @param {number} ms
     * @param {string} desc - MÃ´ táº£ dÃ¹ng cho thÃ´ng bÃ¡o lá»—i
     * @returns {Promise<T>}
     */
    withTimeout(promise, ms, desc) {
        let timer;
        const timeout = new Promise((_, reject) => {
            timer = setTimeout(() => reject(new Error(`QuÃ¡ thá»i gian (${ms / 1000}s): ${desc}`)), ms);
        });
        return Promise.race([promise, timeout]).finally(() => clearTimeout(timer));
    }

    /**
     * LÆ°u cÃ i Ä‘áº·t + lá»‹ch sá»­ cá»§a guild xuá»‘ng Ä‘Ä©a.
     * @param {string} guildId
     * @param {import('./PlayerState').PlayerState} state
     * @param {number} [volume]
     */
    persistState(guildId, state, volume) {
        if (!this.storage) return;
        const history = state.history.map((entry) => {
            const copy = { ...entry };
            delete copy.raw; // KhÃ´ng lÆ°u object track gá»‘c Ä‘á»ƒ file dá»¯ liá»‡u gá»n nháº¹
            return copy;
        });
        this.storage.setGuild(guildId, {
            repeatMode: state.repeatMode,
            filter: state.filter,
            autoplay: state.autoplay,
            volume: Number.isFinite(volume) ? volume : null,
            history
        });
    }

    // ==========================================================
    // TÃ¬m kiáº¿m
    // ==========================================================

    /**
     * Chuyá»ƒn káº¿t quáº£ Kazagumo thÃ nh dáº¡ng dá»¯ liá»‡u thÃ´ Ä‘á»ƒ cache (khÃ´ng kÃ¨m requester).
     * @param {{ type?: string, playlistName?: string, tracks?: object[] }} result
     * @returns {{ type: string, playlistName: string|undefined, raws: object[] }}
     */
    toRawResult(result) {
        return {
            type: result?.type || 'SEARCH',
            playlistName: result?.playlistName,
            raws: (result?.tracks || []).map((track) => track.raw).filter(Boolean)
        };
    }

    /**
     * Dá»±ng láº¡i káº¿t quáº£ tÃ¬m kiáº¿m vá»›i ngÆ°á»i yÃªu cáº§u má»›i (khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng láº¡i instance cÅ©).
     * @param {{ type: string, playlistName?: string, raws: object[] }} raw
     * @param {object} requester
     * @returns {{ type: string, playlistName?: string, tracks: object[] }}
     */
    buildResult(raw, requester) {
        return {
            type: raw.type,
            playlistName: raw.playlistName,
            tracks: raw.raws.map((trackRaw) => new KazagumoTrack(trackRaw, requester))
        };
    }

    /**
     * PhÃ¢n giáº£i truy váº¥n trá»±c tiáº¿p qua Kazagumo (cÃ³ fallback Ã©p tiá»n tá»‘ tÃ¬m kiáº¿m).
     * Tráº£ vá» dá»¯ liá»‡u thÃ´, KHÃ”NG gáº¯n requester Ä‘á»ƒ cache dÃ¹ng chung Ä‘Æ°á»£c.
     * @param {string} query
     * @returns {Promise<{ type: string, playlistName?: string, raws: object[] }>}
     */
    async resolveRaw(query) {
        const empty = { type: 'SEARCH', playlistName: undefined, raws: [] };

        try {
            const result = await this.withTimeout(this.manager.search(query), SEARCH_TIMEOUT_MS, 'Kazagumo search');
            if (result && result.tracks && result.tracks.length) return this.toRawResult(result);
        } catch (err) {
            this.log.warn(`TÃ¬m kiáº¿m tháº¥t báº¡i cho "${query}":`, err.message);
        }

        // Fallback: náº¿u lÃ  tá»« khoÃ¡ (khÃ´ng pháº£i URL) thÃ¬ Ã©p tÃ¬m trÃªn YouTube
        if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(query)) {
            try {
                const forced = await this.withTimeout(
                    this.manager.search(`ytsearch:${query}`),
                    SEARCH_TIMEOUT_MS,
                    'ytsearch'
                );
                if (forced && forced.tracks && forced.tracks.length) return this.toRawResult(forced);
            } catch (err) {
                this.log.warn(`Fallback ytsearch tháº¥t báº¡i cho "${query}":`, err.message);
            }
        }

        return empty;
    }

    /**
     * TÃ¬m kiáº¿m bÃ i hÃ¡t kÃ¨m cache vÃ  fallback.
     * @param {string} query - Tá»« khÃ³a hoáº·c URL
     * @param {object} requester - Discord user
     * @returns {Promise<{ type: string, playlistName?: string, tracks: object[] }>}
     */
    async search(query, requester) {
        const key = SearchCache.normaliseKey(query);
        let raw = this.searchCache.get(key);

        if (raw) {
            this.log.debug(`Cache hit cho "${query}" (${raw.raws.length} káº¿t quáº£).`);
        } else {
            raw = await this.resolveRaw(query);
            if (raw.raws.length) this.searchCache.set(key, raw);
        }

        return this.buildResult(raw, requester);
    }

    // ==========================================================
    // Player & hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i
    // ==========================================================

    /**
     * Láº¥y hoáº·c táº¡o player cho guild, Ä‘á»“ng thá»i Ã¡p dá»¥ng cÃ i Ä‘áº·t Ä‘Ã£ lÆ°u.
     * @param {{ guildId: string, textId: string, voiceId: string }} opts
     * @returns {Promise<object>} Kazagumo player
     */
    async getOrCreatePlayer({ guildId, textId, voiceId }) {
        const state = getState(guildId);
        const stored = this.storage ? this.storage.getGuild(guildId) : null;

        // KhÃ´i phá»¥c cÃ i Ä‘áº·t + lá»‹ch sá»­ Ä‘Ã£ lÆ°u (chá»‰ Ã¡p dá»¥ng má»™t láº§n cho state má»›i)
        if (stored && !state.restored) {
            state.restored = true;
            if (stored.repeatMode) state.setRepeatMode(stored.repeatMode);
            if (stored.filter && FilterService.isValid(stored.filter)) {
                state.filter = FilterService.normalise(stored.filter);
                state.filterNeedsApply = state.filter !== FilterService.default;
            }
            if (typeof stored.autoplay === 'boolean') state.autoplay = stored.autoplay;
            if (!state.history.length && Array.isArray(stored.history)) state.history = stored.history;
            this.log.debug(`ÄÃ£ khÃ´i phá»¥c cÃ i Ä‘áº·t guild ${guildId}.`);
        }

        let player = this.manager.players.get(guildId);

        // Náº¿u player Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ destroy hoáº·c máº¥t káº¿t ná»‘i voice, dá»n dáº¹p Ä‘á»ƒ táº¡o má»›i
        // Trong Shoukaku / Kazagumo: State = CONNECTING: 0, CONNECTED: 1, DISCONNECTING: 2, DISCONNECTED: 3
        if (player && ((player.state !== 1 && player.state !== 0) || !player.voiceId)) {
            try {
                await player.destroy();
            } catch {
                /* player Ä‘Ã£ cháº¿t */
            }
            this.manager.players.delete(guildId);
            player = null;
        }

        if (!player) {
            this.log.info(`Äang káº¿t ná»‘i voice channel ${voiceId} táº¡i guild ${guildId}...`);
            player = await this.withTimeout(
                this.manager.createPlayer({
                    guildId,
                    textId,
                    voiceId,
                    volume: stored && Number.isFinite(stored.volume) ? stored.volume : DEFAULT_VOLUME,
                    deaf: true
                }),
                15000,
                'Káº¿t ná»‘i kÃªnh thoáº¡i (createPlayer)'
            );
            this.log.info(`ÄÃ£ táº¡o player má»›i cho guild ${guildId} trÃªn node "${player.shoukaku?.node?.name || 'unknown'}".`);
        } else {
            if (textId && player.textId !== textId) player.setTextChannel(textId);
            if (voiceId && player.voiceId !== voiceId) player.setVoiceChannel(voiceId);
        }

        return player;
    }

    /**
     * ThÃªm bÃ i hÃ¡t vÃ o hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i (cÃ³ kiá»ƒm tra giá»›i háº¡n) vÃ  phÃ¡t náº¿u Ä‘ang ráº£nh.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {object|object[]} tracks - Má»™t track hoáº·c máº£ng track
     * @param {{ requesterId?: string, top?: boolean }} [options]
     * @returns {Promise<{ ok: boolean, added: number, trimmed: number, error: string|null, vars: object|null }>}
     */
    async enqueue(player, tracks, options = {}) {
        const list = (Array.isArray(tracks) ? tracks : [tracks]).filter(Boolean);
        if (!list.length) return { ok: false, added: 0, trimmed: 0, error: 'empty', vars: null };

        const { requesterId = null, top = false } = options;
        const check = queueService.canAdd(player, list.length, requesterId);

        if (!check.ok) {
            return { ok: false, added: 0, trimmed: list.length, error: check.error, vars: check.vars };
        }

        const accepted = list.slice(0, check.allowed);
        if (top) {
            // ChÃ¨n ngÆ°á»£c Ä‘á»ƒ giá»¯ Ä‘Ãºng thá»© tá»± ban Ä‘áº§u á»Ÿ Ä‘áº§u hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i
            for (let i = accepted.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) queueService.addTop(player, accepted[i]);
        } else {
            queueService.add(player, accepted);
        }

        if (!player.playing && !player.paused) {
            await player.play().catch((err) => {
                this.log.error('Lá»—i khi báº¯t Ä‘áº§u phÃ¡t trong enqueue:', err);
            });
        }

        return {
            ok: true,
            added: accepted.length,
            trimmed: list.length - accepted.length,
            error: null,
            vars: null
        };
    }

    /**
     * Bá» qua bÃ i hiá»‡n táº¡i, phÃ¡t ngay bÃ i má»›i (Play Skip).
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {object} track - Kazagumo track
     */
    playSkip(player, track) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);
        state.incrementGeneration();

        queueService.addTop(player, track);
        if (player.playing || player.paused) {
            player.skip();
        } else {
            player.play();
        }
    }

    /**
     * Bá» qua bÃ i hiá»‡n táº¡i (Skip).
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @returns {boolean} true náº¿u thÃ nh cÃ´ng
     */
    skip(player) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);
        state.incrementGeneration();

        if (!player.playing && !player.paused) return false;
        player.skip();
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * Nháº£y Ä‘áº¿n vá»‹ trÃ­ bÃ i hÃ¡t trong queue (Skip To).
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {number} position - Vá»‹ trÃ­ bÃ i hÃ¡t (1-based, hiá»ƒn thá»‹ cho user)
     * @returns {{ ok: boolean, error?: string, vars?: object }}
     */
    skipTo(player, position) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);

        if (!player.playing && !player.paused) {
            return { ok: false, error: 'common.noPlaying' };
        }
        if (position < 1 || position > player.queue.length) {
            return { ok: false, error: 'commands.skipto.invalidPosition', vars: { count: player.queue.length } };
        }

        state.incrementGeneration();

        // XÃ³a (position - 1) bÃ i trÆ°á»›c vá»‹ trÃ­ Ä‘Ã³
        player.queue.splice(0, position - 1);
        player.skip();
        return { ok: true };
    }

    /**
     * Táº¡m dá»«ng phÃ¡t nháº¡c.
     * @param {object} player
     */
    pause(player) {
        player.pause(true);
    }

    /**
     * Tiáº¿p tá»¥c phÃ¡t nháº¡c.
     * @param {object} player
     */
    resume(player) {
        player.pause(false);
    }

    /**
     * Nháº£y tá»›i má»™t má»‘c thá»i gian trong bÃ i Ä‘ang phÃ¡t.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {number} positionMs - Vá»‹ trÃ­ Ä‘Ã­ch (ms)
     * @returns {Promise<{ ok: boolean, error?: string, vars?: object, position?: number }>}
     */
    async seek(player, positionMs) {
        const current = player.queue.current;
        if (!current) return { ok: false, error: 'common.noPlaying' };
        if (current.isStream || current.isSeekable === false) {
            return { ok: false, error: 'commands.seek.live' };
        }

        const duration = current.length || 0;
        if (positionMs < 0 || (duration && positionMs > duration)) {
            return { ok: false, error: 'commands.seek.outOfRange', vars: { duration: this.formatMs(duration) } };
        }

        await player.seek(positionMs);
        return { ok: true, position: positionMs };
    }

    /**
     * Äá»‹nh dáº¡ng nhanh mili-giÃ¢y thÃ nh mm:ss (dÃ¹ng cho thÃ´ng bÃ¡o lá»—i).
     * @param {number} ms
     * @returns {string}
     */
    formatMs(ms) {
        const total = Math.floor((ms || 0) / 1000);
        const m = String(Math.floor(total / 60)).padStart(2, '0');
        const s = String(total % 60).padStart(2, '0');
        return `${m}:${s}`;
    }

    /**
     * Ãp dá»¥ng bá»™ lá»c Ã¢m thanh cho guild.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {string} name - TÃªn bá»™ lá»c
     * @returns {Promise<{ ok: boolean, filter: string, label: string }>}
     */
    async setFilter(player, name) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);
        const result = await FilterService.apply(player, name);

        if (result.ok) {
            state.filter = result.filter;
            state.filterNeedsApply = false;
            this.persistState(player.guildId, state, player.volume);
        }

        return result;
    }

    /**
     * Báº­t/táº¯t autoplay cho guild.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {boolean} enabled
     * @returns {boolean} Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i má»›i
     */
    setAutoplay(player, enabled) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);
        state.autoplay = Boolean(enabled);
        this.persistState(player.guildId, state, player.volume);
        return state.autoplay;
    }

    /**
     * Dá»«ng phÃ¡t, xoÃ¡ hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i vÃ  ngáº¯t káº¿t ná»‘i.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     */
    async stop(player) {
        const guildId = player?.guildId;
        if (!guildId) return;

        const state = getState(guildId);
        state.incrementGeneration();

        await this.finalisePanels(state, 'stopped');
        state.clearPanels();

        if (player.queue) {
            player.queue.length = 0;
            player.queue.current = null;
        }

        try {
            await player.destroy();
        } catch {}
        try {
            await this.manager.destroyPlayer(guildId);
        } catch {}
        try {
            await this.manager.shoukaku.leaveVoiceChannel(guildId);
        } catch {}
        try {
            const guild = this.client.guilds.cache.get(guildId);
            if (guild?.members?.me?.voice?.channelId) {
                await guild.members.me.voice.disconnect();
            }
        } catch {}

        this.manager.players.delete(guildId);
        this.persistState(guildId, state, player.volume);
    }

    /**
     * PhÃ¡t láº¡i bÃ i vá»«a nghe gáº§n nháº¥t tá»« lá»‹ch sá»­.
     * Há»— trá»£ cáº£ lá»‹ch sá»­ khÃ´i phá»¥c tá»« Ä‘Ä©a (chÆ°a cÃ³ object track gá»‘c â†’ tÃ¬m láº¡i theo URI).
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @returns {Promise<{ ok: boolean, track?: object, error?: string }>}
     */
    async previous(player) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);

        if (state.history.length === 0) {
            return { ok: false, error: 'commands.previous.empty' };
        }

        const prevTrack = state.popFromHistory();

        let rawTrack = prevTrack.raw;
        if (!rawTrack) {
            // Lá»‹ch sá»­ náº¡p tá»« Ä‘Ä©a: tÃ¬m láº¡i bÃ i hÃ¡t theo URI hoáº·c tÃªn
            const query = prevTrack.uri || `${prevTrack.title} ${prevTrack.author || ''}`.trim();
            const result = await this.search(query, player.queue.current?.requester || null);
            if (!result.tracks.length) {
                return { ok: false, error: 'commands.previous.empty' };
            }
            rawTrack = result.tracks[0];
        }

        state.incrementGeneration();

        queueService.addTop(player, rawTrack);
        if (player.playing || player.paused) {
            player.skip();
        } else {
            await player.play();
        }

        return { ok: true, track: rawTrack };
    }

    // ==========================================================
    // Báº£ng Now Playing
    // ==========================================================

    /**
     * ÄÄƒng kÃ½ bá»‘i cáº£nh interaction lÃ m "kÃªnh" hiá»ƒn thá»‹ báº£ng Now Playing dáº¡ng ephemeral.
     * Gá»i ngay sau khi command Ä‘Ã£ deferReply vá»›i cá» EPHEMERAL.
     * @param {import('discord.js').Interaction} interaction
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @returns {import('./PlayerState').PlayerState}
     */
    registerEphemeralContext(interaction, player) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);
        state.setEphemeralContext({
            webhook: interaction.webhook,
            userId: interaction.user.id,
            channelId: interaction.channelId
        });
        return state;
    }

    /**
     * XoÃ¡ bá»‘i cáº£nh ephemeral cá»§a guild (dÃ¹ng khi gáº·p lá»—i Ä‘á»ƒ báº£ng khÃ´ng bá»‹ ghi Ä‘Ã¨).
     * @param {string} guildId
     */
    releaseEphemeralContext(guildId) {
        const state = getState(guildId);
        state.clearEphemeralContext();
        // Chá»‰ dá»«ng ticker náº¿u khÃ´ng cÃ²n báº£ng cÃ´ng khai nÃ o cáº§n cáº­p nháº­t
        if (!state.publicPanel) state.clearNowPlaying();
    }

    /**
     * ÄÄƒng kÃ½ báº£ng Now Playing cÃ´ng khai (do lá»‡nh /nowplaying táº¡o) Ä‘á»ƒ ticker
     * tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh tiáº¿p tá»¥c cáº­p nháº­t nÃ³.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {import('discord.js').Message} message - Tin nháº¯n cÃ´ng khai vá»«a gá»­i
     */
    trackPublicPanel(player, message) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);
        state.publicPanel = { channelId: message.channelId, message };
        state.touchPanel();
        this.startProgressTicker(player, state);
    }

    /**
     * Váº½ láº¡i báº£ng Now Playing (ephemeral + public náº¿u cÃ³).
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {object} track - Track cáº§n hiá»ƒn thá»‹
     * @param {import('./PlayerState').PlayerState} state
     * @param {string|null} [note] - Ghi chÃº hiá»ƒn thá»‹ phÃ­a trÃªn báº£ng (chá»‰ báº£ng ephemeral)
     */
    async renderPanels(player, track, state, note = null) {
        if (!track) return;

        // Ghi chÃº chá»‰ biáº¿n máº¥t khi CHUYá»‚N BÃ€I, trÃ¡nh bá»‹ xoÃ¡ ngay sau khi hiá»‡n
        const trackKey = track.uri || track.title || String(track.identifier || '');
        const trackChanged = state.panelTrackKey !== trackKey;
        state.panelTrackKey = trackKey;
        if (note) {
            state.panelNote = note;
        } else if (trackChanged) {
            state.panelNote = null;
        }

        // NÃºt tá»± vÃ´ hiá»‡u hoÃ¡ náº¿u báº£ng bá»‹ bá» quÃªn quÃ¡ lÃ¢u
        if (state.isPanelIdle()) state.panelDisabled = true;
        const disabled = state.panelDisabled;

        const { embed, components } = NowPlayingUI.create(player, track, state, { disabled });

        // --- 1. Báº£ng ephemeral (chá»‰ ngÆ°á»i gá»i lá»‡nh tháº¥y) ---
        const ctx = state.getEphemeralContext();
        if (ctx) {
            try {
                // '@original' = tin nháº¯n tráº£ lá»i gá»‘c cá»§a interaction (chÃ­nh lÃ  báº£ng nÃ y)
                await ctx.webhook.editMessage('@original', {
                    content: state.panelNote || '',
                    embeds: [embed],
                    components
                });
                state.nowPlayingChannelId = ctx.channelId;
            } catch (err) {
                // 10062: interaction háº¿t háº¡n, 10008: tin nháº¯n Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ xoÃ¡
                this.log.warn('Báº£ng Now Playing ephemeral khÃ´ng cÃ²n chá»‰nh sá»­a Ä‘Æ°á»£c:', err.message);
                state.clearEphemeralContext();
            }
        }

        // --- 2. Báº£ng cÃ´ng khai trong kÃªnh chat (chá»‰ cÃ³ khi dÃ¹ng /nowplaying) ---
        if (state.publicPanel) {
            try {
                await state.publicPanel.message.edit({ embeds: [embed], components });
            } catch (err) {
                if (err && err.code === 10008) {
                    // Tin nháº¯n Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng xoÃ¡
                    state.publicPanel = null;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Váº½ láº¡i má»i báº£ng Now Playing Ä‘ang má»Ÿ cá»§a guild (tiá»‡n dá»¥ng cho cÃ¡c lá»‡nh
     * thay Ä‘á»•i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i nhÆ° /pause, /volume, /repeat).
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     */
    async refreshPanels(player) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);
        await this.renderPanels(player, player.queue.current, state);
    }

    /**
     * Váº½ láº¡i báº£ng Now Playing ephemeral vÃ  khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng ticker tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {object} track - Track cáº§n hiá»ƒn thá»‹
     * @param {string|null} [note] - Ghi chÃº hiá»ƒn thá»‹ phÃ­a trÃªn báº£ng
     * @returns {Promise<boolean>} true náº¿u Ä‘Ã£ váº½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c báº£ng ephemeral
     */
    async renderEphemeralPanel(player, track, note = null) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);
        const hasEphemeral = Boolean(state.getEphemeralContext());
        await this.renderPanels(player, track, state, note);
        this.startProgressTicker(player, state);
        return hasEphemeral;
    }

    /**
     * Chuyá»ƒn má»i báº£ng Ä‘ang má»Ÿ sang tráº¡ng thÃ¡i "Ä‘Ã£ káº¿t thÃºc" vÃ  xoÃ¡ nÃºt báº¥m,
     * trÃ¡nh Ä‘á»ƒ láº¡i nÃºt cháº¿t khiáº¿n ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng báº¥m vÃ o bá»‹ lá»—i.
     * @param {import('./PlayerState').PlayerState} state
     * @param {'stopped'|'ended'} reason
     */
    async finalisePanels(state, reason) {
        const embed = NowPlayingUI.createStopped(reason);
        const payload = { content: '', embeds: [embed], components: [] };

        const ctx = state.getEphemeralContext();
        if (ctx) {
            await ctx.webhook.editMessage('@original', payload).catch(() => {});
        }

        if (state.publicPanel) {
            await state.publicPanel.message.edit({ embeds: [embed], components: [] }).catch(() => {});
        }
    }

    /**
     * Khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng (hoáº·c khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng láº¡i) ticker cáº­p nháº­t thanh tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh.
     * Chá»‰ cháº¡y khi thá»±c sá»± cÃ³ báº£ng cáº§n cáº­p nháº­t.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {import('./PlayerState').PlayerState} state
     */
    startProgressTicker(player, state) {
        if (state.progressInterval) {
            clearInterval(state.progressInterval);
            state.progressInterval = null;
        }
        if (!state.getEphemeralContext() && !state.publicPanel) return;

        state.progressInterval = setInterval(async () => {
            try {
                if (player.paused) return; // Äang táº¡m dá»«ng thÃ¬ giá»¯ nguyÃªn UI
                const currentTrack = player.queue.current;
                if (!currentTrack) {
                    state.clearNowPlaying();
                    return;
                }
                await this.renderPanels(player, currentTrack, state);
                if (!state.getEphemeralContext() && !state.publicPanel) {
                    state.clearNowPlaying();
                }
            } catch (err) {
                this.log.error('Lá»—i cáº­p nháº­t tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh Now Playing:', err);
            }
        }, PANEL_TICK_MS);

        // KhÃ´ng giá»¯ tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh sá»‘ng chá»‰ vÃ¬ ticker nÃ y
        if (typeof state.progressInterval.unref === 'function') state.progressInterval.unref();
    }

    // ==========================================================
    // VÃ²ng Ä‘á»i bÃ i hÃ¡t
    // ==========================================================

    /**
     * Xá»­ lÃ½ sá»± kiá»‡n khi bÃ i hÃ¡t báº¯t Ä‘áº§u phÃ¡t.
     * KHÃ”NG gá»­i tin nháº¯n vÃ o kÃªnh chat â€” chá»‰ cáº­p nháº­t báº£ng Now Playing Ä‘ang má»Ÿ.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {object} track - Track Ä‘ang phÃ¡t
     */
    async onTrackStart(player, track) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);
        this.log.debug(`Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u phÃ¡t "${track?.title}" táº¡i guild ${player.guildId}.`);

        // Ãp dá»¥ng bá»™ lá»c Ä‘Ã£ lÆ°u (chá»‰ má»™t láº§n sau khi khÃ´i phá»¥c tá»« Ä‘Ä©a)
        if (state.filterNeedsApply && state.filter !== FilterService.default) {
            try {
                await FilterService.apply(player, state.filter);
            } catch (err) {
                this.log.warn('KhÃ´ng Ã¡p dá»¥ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c bá»™ lá»c Ä‘Ã£ lÆ°u:', err.message);
            }
            state.filterNeedsApply = false;
        }

        if (state.progressInterval) {
            clearInterval(state.progressInterval);
            state.progressInterval = null;
        }

        await this.renderPanels(player, track, state);
        this.startProgressTicker(player, state);
    }

    /**
     * Xá»­ lÃ½ sá»± kiá»‡n khi bÃ i hÃ¡t káº¿t thÃºc.
     * ÄÆ°a bÃ i vÃ o history, lÆ°u xuá»‘ng Ä‘Ä©a, xá»­ lÃ½ repeat mode.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {object} track - Track vá»«a phÃ¡t xong
     */
    onTrackEnd(player, track) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);
        state.clearNowPlaying();

        if (track) {
            state.addToHistory({
                title: track.title,
                author: track.author,
                uri: track.uri,
                identifier: track.identifier,
                duration: track.length || track.duration || 0,
                thumbnail: track.thumbnail,
                requesterId: track.requester ? track.requester.id : null,
                requesterName: track.requester ? track.requester.globalName || track.requester.username : 'Unknown',
                raw: track,
                source: track.sourceName || 'unknown'
            });
            this.persistState(player.guildId, state, player.volume);
        }

        // Xá»­ lÃ½ Repeat mode
        if (state.repeatMode === 'track' && track) {
            queueService.addTop(player, track);
        } else if (state.repeatMode === 'queue' && track) {
            queueService.add(player, track);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Xá»­ lÃ½ khi player háº¿t bÃ i trong queue: thá»­ autoplay, náº¿u khÃ´ng thÃ¬ dá»n dáº¹p.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     */
    async onPlayerEmpty(player) {
        const state = getState(player.guildId);

        if (state.autoplay) {
            const seed = state.history[0];
            if (seed) {
                try {
                    const next = await this.autoplay.findRelated(
                        seed.raw || seed,
                        state.getRecentKeys(),
                        seed.raw?.requester || null
                    );
                    if (next) {
                        this.log.info(`Autoplay phÃ¡t tiáº¿p "${next.title}" táº¡i guild ${player.guildId}.`);
                        state.incrementGeneration();
                        queueService.add(player, next);
                        await player.play();
                        return;
                    }
                } catch (err) {
                    this.log.warn('Autoplay tháº¥t báº¡i, sáº½ dá»«ng player:', err.message);
                }
            }
        }

        await this.finalisePanels(state, 'ended');
        state.clearPanels();
        this.persistState(player.guildId, state, player.volume);
        await this.stop(player);
    }

    /**
     * Xá»­ lÃ½ khi player bá»‹ há»§y.
     * @param {string} guildId
     */
    onPlayerDestroy(guildId) {
        removeState(guildId);
    }
}

module.exports = PlayerService;

const FilterService = require('../filters/FilterService');

class PlayerState {
    /**
     * @param {string} guildId - ID cá»§a Discord guild

    constructor(guildId) {

         * trÆ°á»›c khi phÃ¡t Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh phÃ¡t nháº§m bÃ i hÃ¡t cÅ©.

        this.publicPanel = null;

        this.restored = false;

    incrementGeneration() {
        return ++this.playbackGeneration;
    }

    /**
     * Kiá»ƒm tra xem generation cÃ³ cÃ²n há»£p lá»‡ khÃ´ng.
     * @param {number} generation - Generation lÃºc báº¯t Ä‘áº§u resolve
     * @returns {boolean} true náº¿u váº«n há»£p lá»‡
     */
    isGenerationValid(generation) {
        return generation === this.playbackGeneration;
    }

    /**
     * Äáº©y bÃ i hÃ¡t vá»«a phÃ¡t xong vÃ o lá»‹ch sá»­ (Ä‘áº§u máº£ng).
     * Giá»›i háº¡n tá»‘i Ä‘a MAX_HISTORY bÃ i.

    addToHistory(track) {
        if (!track) return;
        this.history.unshift(track);
        if (this.history.length > MAX_HISTORY) {
            this.history.length = MAX_HISTORY;
        }
    }

    /**
     * Láº¥y bÃ i hÃ¡t gáº§n nháº¥t tá»« lá»‹ch sá»­ (vÃ  xoÃ¡ khá»i history).
     * @returns {object|null} Track hoáº·c null náº¿u history trá»‘ng
     */
    popFromHistory() {
        if (this.history.length === 0) return null;
        return this.history.shift();
    }

    /**

     * @returns {Set<string>}
     */
    getRecentKeys() {
        const keys = new Set();
        for (const entry of this.history) {
            const key = entry.identifier || entry.uri || `${entry.title}|${entry.author}`;
            if (key) keys.add(key);
        }
        return keys;
    }

    /**
     * Chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»•i cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ láº·p theo vÃ²ng: off â†’ track â†’ queue â†’ off
     * @returns {string} Cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ láº·p má»›i
     */
    cycleRepeatMode() {
        const modes = ['off', 'track', 'queue'];
        const currentIndex = modes.indexOf(this.repeatMode);
        this.repeatMode = modes[(currentIndex + 1) % modes.length];
        return this.repeatMode;
    }

    /**
     * Äáº·t cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ láº·p trá»±c tiáº¿p.
     * @param {'off'|'track'|'queue'} mode

    setRepeatMode(mode) {
        if (['off', 'track', 'queue'].includes(mode)) {
            this.repeatMode = mode;
        }
    }

    /**
     * LÆ°u bá»‘i cáº£nh interaction Ä‘á»ƒ gá»­i/cáº­p nháº­t báº£ng Now Playing dáº¡ng ephemeral.
     * @param {{ webhook: object, userId: string, channelId: string }} ctx
     */
    setEphemeralContext({ webhook, userId, channelId }) {
        this.ephemeralContext = {
            webhook,
            userId,
            channelId,
            // Token interaction sá»‘ng 15 phÃºt â†’ chá»«a 1 phÃºt an toÃ n
            expiresAt: Date.now() + 14 * 60 * 1000
        };
        this.touchPanel();
    }

    /**
     * Láº¥y bá»‘i cáº£nh ephemeral náº¿u váº«n cÃ²n hiá»‡u lá»±c (tá»± dá»n náº¿u Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n).
     * @returns {{ webhook: object, userId: string, channelId: string, expiresAt: number }|null}
     */
    getEphemeralContext() {
        if (!this.ephemeralContext) return null;
        if (Date.now() >= this.ephemeralContext.expiresAt) {
            this.ephemeralContext = null;
            return null;
        }
        return this.ephemeralContext;
    }

    /** XoÃ¡ bá»‘i cáº£nh ephemeral (token háº¿t háº¡n hoáº·c báº£ng Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ xoÃ¡). */
    clearEphemeralContext() {
        this.ephemeralContext = null;
    }

    /** Ghi nháº­n cÃ³ tÆ°Æ¡ng tÃ¡c vá»›i báº£ng Now Playing (lÃ m má»›i Ä‘á»“ng há»“ Ä‘áº¿m háº¿t háº¡n nÃºt). */
    touchPanel() {
        this.panelLastInteractionAt = Date.now();
        this.panelDisabled = false;
    }

    /**
     * Báº£ng Ä‘Ã£ quÃ¡ háº¡n tÆ°Æ¡ng tÃ¡c chÆ°a?
     * @returns {boolean}
     */
    isPanelIdle() {
        return Date.now() - this.panelLastInteractionAt >= PANEL_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS;
    }

    /**
     * Dá»n dáº¹p interval vÃ  reset tin nháº¯n Now Playing.
     */
    clearNowPlaying() {
        if (this.progressInterval) {
            clearInterval(this.progressInterval);
            this.progressInterval = null;
        }
        this.nowPlayingChannelId = null;
    }

    /**
     * Dá»«ng ticker vÃ  xoÃ¡ má»i tham chiáº¿u báº£ng Now Playing (cáº£ ephemeral láº«n public).
     */
    clearPanels() {
        this.clearNowPlaying();
        this.clearEphemeralContext();
        this.publicPanel = null;
        this.panelNote = null;
        this.panelTrackKey = null;
        this.panelDisabled = false;
    }

    /**
     * Reset toÃ n bá»™ state vá» máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh.
     */
    reset() {
        this.clearPanels();
        this.playbackGeneration = 0;
        this.history = [];
        this.repeatMode = 'off';
        this.filter = FilterService.default;
        this.autoplay = AUTOPLAY_DEFAULT;
        this.restored = false;
        this.filterNeedsApply = false;
    }

// --- Registry: Quáº£n lÃ½ PlayerState theo Guild ---

/** @type {Map<string, PlayerState>} */
const stateMap = new Map();

/**
 * Láº¥y hoáº·c táº¡o PlayerState cho má»™t guild.
 * @param {string} guildId
 * @returns {PlayerState}
 */
function getState(guildId) {
    if (!stateMap.has(guildId)) {
        stateMap.set(guildId, new PlayerState(guildId));
    }
    return stateMap.get(guildId);
}

/**
 * XÃ³a PlayerState cá»§a má»™t guild (khi bot rá»i khá»i guild).
 * @param {string} guildId
 */
function removeState(guildId) {
    const state = stateMap.get(guildId);
    if (state) {
        state.reset();
        stateMap.delete(guildId);
    }
}

/**
 * Sá»‘ guild Ä‘ang cÃ³ state (dÃ¹ng cho endpoint /health).
 * @returns {number}
 */
function getStateCount() {
    return stateMap.size;
}

module.exports = {
    PlayerState,
    getState,
    removeState,
    getStateCount,
    MAX_HISTORY
};

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\services\queue\QueueService.js ===
/**
 * QueueService â€” Quáº£n lÃ½ táº­p trung má»i thao tÃ¡c hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i (queue).
 *
 * KhÃ´ng cho command gá»i trá»±c tiáº¿p player.queue.add()/splice().
 * Táº¥t cáº£ Ä‘i qua service nÃ y Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘áº£m báº£o nháº¥t quÃ¡n vÃ  dá»… má»Ÿ rá»™ng.
 */

const { MAX_QUEUE_SIZE, MAX_QUEUE_SIZE_PER_USER, MAX_PLAYLIST_LOAD } = require('../../config/constants');

class QueueService {
    /**
     * ThÃªm má»™t hoáº·c nhiá»u track vÃ o cuá»‘i hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {object|object[]} tracks - Track(s) Kazagumo hoáº·c máº£ng
     */
    add(player, tracks) {
        if (Array.isArray(tracks)) {
            for (const t of tracks) {
                player.queue.add(t);
            }
        } else {
            player.queue.add(tracks);
        }
    }

    /**
     * ChÃ¨n track vÃ o Ä‘áº§u hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i (vá»‹ trÃ­ 0 = phÃ¡t káº¿ tiáº¿p).
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {object} track - Track Kazagumo
     */
    addTop(player, track) {
        // Kazagumo queue lÃ  má»™t máº£ng, unshift Ä‘á»ƒ chÃ¨n Ä‘áº§u
        player.queue.unshift(track);
    }

    /**
     * XÃ³a má»™t track táº¡i vá»‹ trÃ­ index (0-based).
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {number} index - Vá»‹ trÃ­ (0-based)
     * @returns {object|null} Track bá»‹ xÃ³a hoáº·c null náº¿u index khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡
     */
    remove(player, index) {
        if (index < 0 || index >= player.queue.length) return null;
        const removed = player.queue.splice(index, 1);
        return removed[0] || null;
    }

    /**
     * Di chuyá»ƒn track tá»« vá»‹ trÃ­ from sang vá»‹ trÃ­ to (0-based).
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {number} from - Vá»‹ trÃ­ nguá»“n (0-based)
     * @param {number} to - Vá»‹ trÃ­ Ä‘Ã­ch (0-based)
     * @returns {boolean} true náº¿u thÃ nh cÃ´ng
     */
    move(player, from, to) {
        if (from < 0 || from >= player.queue.length) return false;
        if (to < 0 || to >= player.queue.length) return false;
        if (from === to) return true;

        const [track] = player.queue.splice(from, 1);
        player.queue.splice(to, 0, track);
        return true;
    }

    /**
     * XÃ³a toÃ n bá»™ hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i nhÆ°ng KHÃ”NG dá»«ng bÃ i Ä‘ang phÃ¡t.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @returns {number} Sá»‘ bÃ i Ä‘Ã£ xÃ³a
     */
    clear(player) {
        const count = player.queue.length;
        player.queue.length = 0;
        return count;
    }

    /**
     * Shuffle (xÃ¡o trá»™n) hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {string} [requesterId] - Náº¿u cung cáº¥p, chá»‰ shuffle bÃ i do user nÃ y thÃªm
     * @returns {number} Sá»‘ bÃ i Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c xÃ¡o trá»™n
     */
    shuffle(player, requesterId = null) {
        if (player.queue.length <= 1) return player.queue.length;

        if (requesterId) {
            // Shuffle chá»‰ bÃ i cá»§a ngÆ°á»i yÃªu cáº§u, giá»¯ nguyÃªn vá»‹ trÃ­ bÃ i ngÆ°á»i khÃ¡c
            const myIndices = [];
            const myTracks = [];

            for (let i = 0; i < player.queue.length; i++) {
                const track = player.queue[i];
                const reqId = track.requesterId || (track.requester && track.requester.id);
                if (reqId === requesterId) {
                    myIndices.push(i);
                    myTracks.push(track);
                }
            }

            if (myTracks.length <= 1) return myTracks.length;

            // Fisher-Yates shuffle trÃªn subset
            for (let i = myTracks.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
                const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
                [myTracks[i], myTracks[j]] = [myTracks[j], myTracks[i]];
            }

            // Äáº·t láº¡i cÃ¡c bÃ i Ä‘Ã£ shuffle vÃ o Ä‘Ãºng vá»‹ trÃ­ index gá»‘c
            for (let k = 0; k < myIndices.length; k++) {
                player.queue[myIndices[k]] = myTracks[k];
            }

            return myTracks.length;
        }

        // Shuffle toÃ n bá»™ (Fisher-Yates)
        const queue = player.queue;
        for (let i = queue.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
            const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
            [queue[i], queue[j]] = [queue[j], queue[i]];
        }

        return queue.length;
    }

    /**
     * Láº¥y sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng bÃ i trong hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @returns {number}
     */
    size(player) {
        return player.queue.length;
    }

    /**
     * Äáº¿m sá»‘ bÃ i trong hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i do má»™t ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng thÃªm vÃ o.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {string} requesterId
     * @returns {number}
     */
    countByRequester(player, requesterId) {
        if (!requesterId) return 0;
        return Array.from(player.queue).filter((track) => {
            const id = track.requesterId || (track.requester && track.requester.id);
            return id === requesterId;
        }).length;
    }

    /**
     * Kiá»ƒm tra cÃ²n chá»— Ä‘á»ƒ thÃªm bÃ i hay khÃ´ng (theo giá»›i háº¡n guild vÃ  theo ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng).
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {number} count - Sá»‘ bÃ i muá»‘n thÃªm
     * @param {string} [requesterId] - ID ngÆ°á»i yÃªu cáº§u (bá» qua náº¿u khÃ´ng cáº§n giá»›i háº¡n theo ngÆ°á»i)
     * @returns {{ ok: boolean, allowed: number, error?: 'queueFull'|'userQueueFull', vars?: object }}
     */
    canAdd(player, count, requesterId) {
        const currentSize = player.queue.length;
        const guildSpace = MAX_QUEUE_SIZE - currentSize;

        if (guildSpace <= 0) {
            return { ok: false, allowed: 0, error: 'queueFull', vars: { max: MAX_QUEUE_SIZE } };
        }

        let allowed = Math.min(count, guildSpace, MAX_PLAYLIST_LOAD);

        if (requesterId) {
            const userCount = this.countByRequester(player, requesterId);
            const userSpace = MAX_QUEUE_SIZE_PER_USER - userCount;
            if (userSpace <= 0) {
                return {
                    ok: false,
                    allowed: 0,
                    error: 'userQueueFull',
                    vars: { count: userCount, max: MAX_QUEUE_SIZE_PER_USER }
                };
            }
            allowed = Math.min(allowed, userSpace);
        }

        return { ok: allowed > 0, allowed };
    }

    /**
     * TÃ­nh tá»•ng thá»i lÆ°á»£ng hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i (mili-giÃ¢y).
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @returns {number}
     */
    getTotalDuration(player) {
        return player.queue.reduce((sum, track) => {
            return sum + (track.length || track.duration || 0);
        }, 0);
    }

    /**
     * Láº¥y slice (pháº§n cáº¯t) cá»§a hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i cho phÃ¢n trang.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {number} page - Sá»‘ trang (1-based)
     * @param {number} [perPage=10] - Sá»‘ bÃ i má»—i trang
     * @returns {{ tracks: object[], page: number, totalPages: number, totalTracks: number }}
     */
    getPage(player, page = 1, perPage = 10) {
        const totalTracks = player.queue.length;
        const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(totalTracks / perPage));
        const safePage = Math.max(1, Math.min(page, totalPages));

        const start = (safePage - 1) * perPage;
        const end = Math.min(start + perPage, totalTracks);
        const tracks = player.queue.slice(start, end);

        return {
            tracks,
            page: safePage,
            totalPages,
            totalTracks,
            startIndex: start
        };
    }
}

// Singleton instance
module.exports = new QueueService();

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\services\storage\StorageService.js ===
/**
 * StorageService â€” LÆ°u trá»¯ bá»n vá»¯ng (persistence) báº±ng file JSON.
 *
 * Pháº¡m vi lÆ°u trá»¯:
 *  - CÃ i Ä‘áº·t theo guild: cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ láº·p, Ã¢m lÆ°á»£ng, bá»™ lá»c, autoplay
 *  - Lá»‹ch sá»­ bÃ i hÃ¡t Ä‘Ã£ phÃ¡t (Ä‘á»ƒ `/history` vÃ  `/previous` sá»‘ng sÃ³t qua restart)
 *
 * Ghi file theo cÆ¡ cháº¿ debounce + ghi nguyÃªn tá»­ (ghi file táº¡m rá»“i rename)
 * Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng bao giá» Ä‘á»ƒ láº¡i file JSON há»ng náº¿u tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh bá»‹ kill giá»¯a chá»«ng.
 */

const fs = require('node:fs/promises');
const path = require('node:path');
const { createLogger } = require('../logger');

const log = createLogger('StorageService');

/** PhiÃªn báº£n schema dá»¯ liá»‡u â€” tÄƒng lÃªn khi cáº¥u trÃºc thay Ä‘á»•i */
const SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;

/** Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh cho má»™t guild chÆ°a tá»«ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c lÆ°u */
function defaultGuildRecord() {
    return {
        repeatMode: 'off',
        volume: null,
        filter: 'off',
        autoplay: null,
        history: []
    };
}

class StorageService {
    /**
     * @param {{ filePath: string, flushDebounceMs?: number, maxHistory?: number }} options
     */
    constructor({ filePath, flushDebounceMs = 2000, maxHistory = 50 }) {
        this.filePath = filePath;
        this.tmpPath = `${filePath}.tmp`;
        this.flushDebounceMs = flushDebounceMs;
        this.maxHistory = maxHistory;

        /** @type {{ version: number, guilds: Record<string, object> }} */
        this.data = { version: SCHEMA_VERSION, guilds: {} };

        /** @type {NodeJS.Timeout|null} */
        this.flushTimer = null;
        this.dirty = false;
        this.ready = false;
    }

    /**
     * Náº¡p dá»¯ liá»‡u tá»« Ä‘Ä©a. An toÃ n khi gá»i nhiá»u láº§n.
     * @returns {Promise<void>}
     */
    async init() {
        try {
            await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(this.filePath), { recursive: true });
        } catch (err) {
            log.warn('KhÃ´ng táº¡o Ä‘Æ°á»£c thÆ° má»¥c dá»¯ liá»‡u, persistence sáº½ táº¡m táº¯t.', err.message);
            this.ready = false;
            return;
        }

        try {
            const raw = await fs.readFile(this.filePath, 'utf8');
            const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
            if (parsed && typeof parsed === 'object' && parsed.guilds) {
                this.data = { version: parsed.version || SCHEMA_VERSION, guilds: parsed.guilds };
                log.info(`ÄÃ£ náº¡p dá»¯ liá»‡u cho ${Object.keys(this.data.guilds).length} guild tá»« ${this.filePath}`);
            }
        } catch (err) {
            if (err.code === 'ENOENT') {
                log.info('ChÆ°a cÃ³ file dá»¯ liá»‡u, sáº½ táº¡o má»›i khi cáº§n.');
            } else {
                // File há»ng: sao lÆ°u láº¡i rá»“i báº¯t Ä‘áº§u tá»« Ä‘áº§u thay vÃ¬ crash
                log.warn('File dá»¯ liá»‡u khÃ´ng Ä‘á»c Ä‘Æ°á»£c, sao lÆ°u vÃ  táº¡o má»›i.', err.message);
                await fs.rename(this.filePath, `${this.filePath}.corrupt-${Date.now()}`).catch(() => {});
            }
        }

        this.ready = true;
    }

    /**
     * Láº¥y báº£n ghi cá»§a má»™t guild (tá»± táº¡o náº¿u chÆ°a cÃ³).
     * @param {string} guildId
     * @returns {object}
     */
    getGuild(guildId) {
        if (!this.data.guilds[guildId]) {
            this.data.guilds[guildId] = defaultGuildRecord();
        }
        return this.data.guilds[guildId];
    }

    /**
     * Ghi Ä‘Ã¨ má»™t pháº§n báº£n ghi cá»§a guild.
     * @param {string} guildId
     * @param {object} patch
     */
    setGuild(guildId, patch) {
        this.data.guilds[guildId] = { ...this.getGuild(guildId), ...patch };
        this.scheduleFlush();
    }

    /**
     * LÆ°u lá»‹ch sá»­ phÃ¡t nháº¡c cá»§a guild (chá»‰ giá»¯ tá»‘i Ä‘a `maxHistory` bÃ i).
     * @param {string} guildId
     * @param {object[]} history
     */
    setHistory(guildId, history) {
        const trimmed = Array.isArray(history) ? history.slice(0, this.maxHistory) : [];
        this.setGuild(guildId, { history: trimmed });
    }

    /**
     * XoÃ¡ dá»¯ liá»‡u cá»§a má»™t guild.
     * @param {string} guildId
     */
    removeGuild(guildId) {
        if (this.data.guilds[guildId]) {
            delete this.data.guilds[guildId];
            this.scheduleFlush();
        }
    }

    /** Äáº·t lá»‹ch ghi Ä‘Ä©a (gá»™p nhiá»u thay Ä‘á»•i liÃªn tiáº¿p thÃ nh má»™t láº§n ghi). */
    scheduleFlush() {
        if (!this.ready) return;
        this.dirty = true;
        if (this.flushTimer) return;
        this.flushTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            this.flushTimer = null;
            this.flush().catch((err) => log.error('Ghi dá»¯ liá»‡u tháº¥t báº¡i:', err));
        }, this.flushDebounceMs);
        // KhÃ´ng giá»¯ tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh sá»‘ng chá»‰ vÃ¬ timer nÃ y
        if (typeof this.flushTimer.unref === 'function') this.flushTimer.unref();
    }

    /**
     * Ghi dá»¯ liá»‡u xuá»‘ng Ä‘Ä©a ngay láº­p tá»©c (ghi nguyÃªn tá»­).
     * @returns {Promise<boolean>} true náº¿u cÃ³ ghi thÃ nh cÃ´ng
     */
    async flush() {
        if (!this.ready || !this.dirty) return false;

        if (this.flushTimer) {
            clearTimeout(this.flushTimer);
            this.flushTimer = null;
        }

        try {
            const payload = JSON.stringify({ version: SCHEMA_VERSION, guilds: this.data.guilds }, null, 2);
            await fs.writeFile(this.tmpPath, payload, 'utf8');
            await fs.rename(this.tmpPath, this.filePath);
            this.dirty = false;
            return true;
        } catch (err) {
            log.error('KhÃ´ng ghi Ä‘Æ°á»£c file dá»¯ liá»‡u:', err);
            return false;
        }
    }

    /** Ghi ná»‘t dá»¯ liá»‡u cÃ²n tá»“n rá»“i dá»n timer (dÃ¹ng khi shutdown). */
    async close() {
        await this.flush();
    }
}

module.exports = StorageService;

                : 'KhÃ´ng cÃ²n bÃ i nÃ o Ä‘á»ƒ phÃ¡t. DÃ¹ng `/play` Ä‘á»ƒ thÃªm bÃ i má»›i.';

/**
 * QueueUI â€” Táº¡o Embed hiá»ƒn thá»‹ danh sÃ¡ch chá» vá»›i phÃ¢n trang thÃ´ng minh.
 */

const { EmbedBuilder, ActionRowBuilder, ButtonBuilder, ButtonStyle } = require('discord.js');
const { formatDuration, truncate, totalDuration } = require('../../utils/formatters');
const { t } = require('../../locales');

/** Sá»‘ bÃ i má»—i trang */
const TRACKS_PER_PAGE = 10;

class QueueUI {
    /**
     * Táº¡o embed danh sÃ¡ch chá» phÃ¢n trang.
     * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
     * @param {number} [page=1] - Sá»‘ trang hiá»‡n táº¡i (1-based)
     * @returns {{ embed: EmbedBuilder, components: ActionRowBuilder[] }}
     */
    static create(player, page = 1) {
        const queue = player.queue;
        const current = player.queue.current;

        if (!current && queue.length === 0) {
            return {
                embed: new EmbedBuilder()
                    .setColor(0x95a5a6)
                    .setTitle(t('ui.queue.emptyTitle'))
                    .setDescription(t('ui.queue.emptyHint'))
                    .setTimestamp(),
                components: []
            };
        }

        const totalTracks = queue.length;
        const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(totalTracks / TRACKS_PER_PAGE));
        const safePage = Math.max(1, Math.min(page, totalPages));

        const start = (safePage - 1) * TRACKS_PER_PAGE;
        const end = Math.min(start + TRACKS_PER_PAGE, totalTracks);

        // --- XÃ¢y dá»±ng Embed ---
        const embed = new EmbedBuilder().setColor(0x5865f2).setTitle(t('ui.queue.title')).setTimestamp();

        // BÃ i Ä‘ang phÃ¡t
        if (current) {
            const curDur = formatDuration(current.length || current.duration || 0);
            const curRequester = current.requester
                ? current.requester.globalName || current.requester.username
                : t('ui.queue.notAvailable');
            embed.addFields({
                name: t('ui.queue.nowPlaying'),
                value:
                    `**${truncate(current.title, 55)}** â€” ${current.author || t('ui.queue.notAvailable')}\n` +
                    `\`${curDur}\` â€¢ ðŸ‘¤ ${curRequester}`,
                inline: false
            });
        }

        // Danh sÃ¡ch hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i
        if (totalTracks > 0) {
            const lines = [];
            for (let i = start; i < end; i++) {
                const track = queue[i];
                const num = String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0');
                const dur = formatDuration(track.length || track.duration || 0);
                const title = truncate(track.title || 'Unknown', 45);
                const requester = track.requester
                    ? track.requester.globalName || track.requester.username
                    : t('ui.queue.notAvailable');

                lines.push(`\`${num}.\` **${title}**\nã€€ã€€\`${dur}\` â€¢ ðŸ‘¤ ${requester}`);
            }
            embed.addFields({
                name: t('ui.queue.listName', { count: totalTracks }),
                value: lines.join('\n') || 'â€”',
                inline: false
            });
        }

        // Tá»•ng thá»i lÆ°á»£ng
        const queueDuration = totalDuration(Array.from(queue));
        const currentRemaining = current
            ? Math.max(0, (current.length || current.duration || 0) - (player.position || 0))
            : 0;

        embed.setFooter({
            text: t('ui.queue.footer', {
                page: safePage,
                totalPages,
                duration: formatDuration(queueDuration + currentRemaining),
                count: totalTracks
            })
        });

        // --- NÃºt phÃ¢n trang ---
        const components = [];

        if (totalPages > 1) {
            const row = new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(
                new ButtonBuilder()
                    .setCustomId('queue_first')
                    .setEmoji('â®')
                    .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
                    .setDisabled(safePage <= 1),

                new ButtonBuilder()
                    .setCustomId(`queue_prev_${safePage}`)
                    .setEmoji('â—€ï¸')
                    .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
                    .setDisabled(safePage <= 1),

                new ButtonBuilder()
                    .setCustomId('queue_page_indicator')
                    .setLabel(`${safePage} / ${totalPages}`)
                    .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Primary)
                    .setDisabled(true),

                new ButtonBuilder()
                    .setCustomId(`queue_next_${safePage}`)
                    .setEmoji('â–¶ï¸')
                    .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
                    .setDisabled(safePage >= totalPages),

                new ButtonBuilder()
                    .setCustomId(`queue_last_${totalPages}`)
                    .setEmoji('â­')
                    .setStyle(ButtonStyle.Secondary)
                    .setDisabled(safePage >= totalPages)
            );
            components.push(row);
        }

        return { embed, components };
    }

    /**
     * PhÃ¢n tÃ­ch customId cá»§a nÃºt phÃ¢n trang Ä‘á»ƒ láº¥y sá»‘ trang Ä‘Ã­ch.
     * @param {string} customId - VÃ­ dá»¥: "queue_next_3", "queue_prev_5", "queue_first", "queue_last_10"
     * @param {number} [currentPage=1] - Trang hiá»‡n táº¡i
     * @returns {number} Trang Ä‘Ã­ch
     */
    static parsePageFromButton(customId, currentPage = 1) {
        if (customId === 'queue_first') return 1;
        if (customId.startsWith('queue_last_')) {
            return parseInt(customId.split('_')[2], 10) || 1;
        }
        if (customId.startsWith('queue_next_')) {
            const fromPage = parseInt(customId.split('_')[2], 10) || currentPage;
            return fromPage + 1;
        }
        if (customId.startsWith('queue_prev_')) {
            const fromPage = parseInt(customId.split('_')[2], 10) || currentPage;
            return Math.max(1, fromPage - 1);
        }
        return currentPage;
    }

    /** Sá»‘ bÃ i má»—i trang. */
    static get TRACKS_PER_PAGE() {
        return TRACKS_PER_PAGE;
    }
}
