=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\services\ui\SearchUI.js ===
/**
 * SearchUI â€” Menu chá»n bÃ i hÃ¡t tá»« káº¿t quáº£ tÃ¬m kiáº¿m (StringSelectMenu).
 *
 * Káº¿t quáº£ tÃ¬m kiáº¿m Ä‘Æ°á»£c lÆ°u táº¡m trong bá»™ nhá»› theo cáº·p (guild, ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng) vá»›i
 * thá»i gian sá»‘ng ngáº¯n, trÃ¡nh pháº£i nhá»“i dá»¯ liá»‡u track vÃ o customId (giá»›i háº¡n 100 kÃ½ tá»±).
 */

const { EmbedBuilder, ActionRowBuilder, StringSelectMenuBuilder } = require('discord.js');

const { formatDuration, truncate } = require('../../utils/formatters');
const { t } = require('../../locales');

/** ID cá»§a select menu chá»n bÃ i hÃ¡t */
const SELECT_ID = 'search_select';

/** Thá»i gian sá»‘ng cá»§a má»™t phiÃªn káº¿t quáº£ tÃ¬m kiáº¿m (ms) */
const SESSION_TTL_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;

/** Sá»‘ káº¿t quáº£ tá»‘i Ä‘a Discord cho phÃ©p trong má»™t select menu */
const MAX_OPTIONS = 25;

/** @type {Map<string, { tracks: object[], expiresAt: number }>} */
const sessions = new Map();

/**
 * Táº¡o khoÃ¡ phiÃªn tÃ¬m kiáº¿m.
 * @param {string} guildId
 * @param {string} userId
 * @returns {string}
 */
function sessionKey(guildId, userId) {
    return `${guildId}:${userId}`;
}

class SearchUI {
    /**
     * LÆ°u káº¿t quáº£ tÃ¬m kiáº¿m cá»§a má»™t ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng Ä‘á»ƒ select menu tham chiáº¿u tá»›i.
     * @param {string} guildId
     * @param {string} userId
     * @param {object[]} tracks
     */
    static storeResults(guildId, userId, tracks) {
        sessions.set(sessionKey(guildId, userId), {
            tracks,
            expiresAt: Date.now() + SESSION_TTL_MS
        });
    }

    /**
     * Láº¥y káº¿t quáº£ tÃ¬m kiáº¿m Ä‘Ã£ lÆ°u cá»§a má»™t ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng.
     * @param {string} guildId
     * @param {string} userId
     * @returns {object[]|null}
     */
    static getResults(guildId, userId) {
        const key = sessionKey(guildId, userId);
        const session = sessions.get(key);
        if (!session) return null;

        if (session.expiresAt <= Date.now()) {
            sessions.delete(key);
            return null;
        }
        return session.tracks;
    }

    /**
     * XoÃ¡ phiÃªn tÃ¬m kiáº¿m cá»§a má»™t ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng.
     * @param {string} guildId
     * @param {string} userId
     */
    static clearResults(guildId, userId) {
        sessions.delete(sessionKey(guildId, userId));
    }

    /**
     * Dá»n cÃ¡c phiÃªn Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n.
     * @returns {number} Sá»‘ phiÃªn Ä‘Ã£ xoÃ¡
     */
    static prune() {
        const now = Date.now();
        let removed = 0;
        for (const [key, session] of sessions) {
            if (session.expiresAt <= now) {
                sessions.delete(key);
                removed++;
            }
        }
        return removed;
    }

    /**
     * Táº¡o embed + select menu cho káº¿t quáº£ tÃ¬m kiáº¿m.
     * @param {string} query - Tá»« khoÃ¡ ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng nháº­p
     * @param {object[]} tracks - Danh sÃ¡ch track
     * @param {number} [limit=10] - Sá»‘ káº¿t quáº£ hiá»ƒn thá»‹
     * @returns {{ embed: EmbedBuilder, components: ActionRowBuilder[] }}
     */
    static create(query, tracks, limit = 10) {
        const shown = tracks.slice(0, Math.min(limit, MAX_OPTIONS));

        const embed = new EmbedBuilder()
            .setColor(0x5865f2)
            .setTitle(`ðŸ”Ž ${truncate(query, 80)}`)
            .setDescription(t('commands.search.prompt', { query: truncate(query, 80) }))
            .setTimestamp();

        const lines = shown.map((track, index) => {
            const dur = formatDuration(track.length || track.duration || 0);
            const author = truncate(track.author || 'N/A', 30);
            return `\`${String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')}.\` **${truncate(track.title || 'Unknown', 55)}** â€” ${author} \`${dur}\``;
        });

        embed.addFields({ name: '\u200b', value: lines.join('\n') || 'â€”' });

        const select = new StringSelectMenuBuilder()
            .setCustomId(SELECT_ID)
            .setPlaceholder(t('commands.search.placeholder'))
            .addOptions(
                shown.map((track, index) => ({
                    label: truncate(track.title || 'Unknown', 95),
                    description: truncate(
                        `${track.author || 'N/A'} â€¢ ${formatDuration(track.length || track.duration || 0)}`,
                        95
                    ),
                    value: String(index)
                }))
            );

        return {
            embed,
            components: [new ActionRowBuilder().addComponents(select)]
        };
    }

    /** ID cá»§a select menu (dÃ¹ng cho router). */
    static get SELECT_ID() {
        return SELECT_ID;
    }
}

module.exports = SearchUI;

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\services\voice\VoicePresenceService.js ===
/**
 * VoicePresenceService â€” Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng rá»i kÃªnh thoáº¡i khi khÃ´ng cÃ²n ai nghe.
 *
 * TrÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã¢y bot náº±m mÃ£i trong kÃªnh thoáº¡i sau khi má»i ngÆ°á»i rá»i Ä‘i, chiáº¿m slot
 * vÃ  giá»¯ káº¿t ná»‘i tá»›i Lavalink. Service nÃ y theo dÃµi sá»± kiá»‡n `voiceStateUpdate`
 * vÃ  háº¹n giá» rá»i phÃ²ng náº¿u kÃªnh chá»‰ cÃ²n láº¡i bot.
 */

const { createLogger } = require('../logger');
const { VOICE_EMPTY_LEAVE_MS } = require('../../config/constants');

const log = createLogger('VoicePresenceService');

/**
 * Äáº¿m sá»‘ thÃ nh viÃªn KHÃ”NG pháº£i bot trong má»™t kÃªnh thoáº¡i.
 * HÃ m thuáº§n, tÃ¡ch riÃªng Ä‘á»ƒ dá»… kiá»ƒm thá»­.
 * @param {Iterable<{ user?: { bot?: boolean } }>} members
 * @returns {number}
 */
function countHumans(members) {
    if (!members) return 0;
    let count = 0;
    for (const member of members) {
        if (member && member.user && !member.user.bot) count++;
    }
    return count;
}

class VoicePresenceService {
    /**
     * @param {import('discord.js').Client} client
     * @param {import('kazagumo').Kazagumo} manager
     * @param {{ idleMs?: number, onIdle?: (guildId: string) => Promise<void>|void }} [options]
     */
    constructor(client, manager, options = {}) {
        this.client = client;
        this.manager = manager;
        this.idleMs = options.idleMs ?? VOICE_EMPTY_LEAVE_MS;
        this.onIdle = options.onIdle || (() => {});
        /** @type {Map<string, NodeJS.Timeout>} */
        this.timers = new Map();
    }

    /** Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u láº¯ng nghe sá»± kiá»‡n voice. */
    start() {
        this.listener = (oldState, newState) => {
            this.handle(oldState, newState).catch((err) => {
                log.error('Lá»—i khi xá»­ lÃ½ voiceStateUpdate:', err);
            });
        };
        this.client.on('voiceStateUpdate', this.listener);
        log.info(`ÄÃ£ báº­t tÃ­nh nÄƒng tá»± rá»i phÃ²ng sau ${Math.round(this.idleMs / 1000)}s khi kÃªnh trá»‘ng.`);
    }

    /** Ngá»«ng láº¯ng nghe vÃ  xoÃ¡ má»i timer Ä‘ang chá». */
    stop() {
        if (this.listener) this.client.off('voiceStateUpdate', this.listener);
        for (const timer of this.timers.values()) clearTimeout(timer);
        this.timers.clear();
    }

    /**
     * Xá»­ lÃ½ má»™t sá»± kiá»‡n thay Ä‘á»•i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i thoáº¡i.
     * @param {import('discord.js').VoiceState} _oldState
     * @param {import('discord.js').VoiceState} newState
     */
    async handle(_oldState, newState) {
        const guild = newState.guild || _oldState?.guild;
        if (!guild) return;

        // 1. Kiá»ƒm tra bot cÃ³ Ä‘ang á»Ÿ trong kÃªnh thoáº¡i nÃ o khÃ´ng (thá»­ tá»« voiceStates, player, Shoukaku, vÃ  guild member)
        const botVoiceChannelId =
            guild.voiceStates?.cache?.get(this.client.user?.id)?.channelId ||
            this.manager.players.get(guild.id)?.voiceId ||
            this.manager.shoukaku.connections.get(guild.id)?.channelId ||
            guild.members.me?.voice?.channelId;

        if (!botVoiceChannelId) {
            this.cancel(guild.id);
            return;
        }

        // 2. Äáº¿m sá»‘ ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng (khÃ´ng pháº£i bot) trong kÃªnh thoáº¡i
        let humans = 0;

        if (guild.voiceStates?.cache?.size) {
            for (const vs of guild.voiceStates.cache.values()) {
                if (vs.channelId !== botVoiceChannelId) continue;
                if (vs.id === this.client.user?.id) continue;
                // Náº¿u ngÆ°á»i nÃ y vá»«a rá»i kÃªnh hoáº·c ngáº¯t káº¿t ná»‘i trong sá»± kiá»‡n nÃ y
                if (vs.id === newState.id && newState.channelId !== botVoiceChannelId) continue;
                if (vs.member?.user?.bot) continue;
                humans++;
            }
        } else {
            const channel = guild.channels.cache.get(botVoiceChannelId)
                || await guild.channels.fetch(botVoiceChannelId).catch(() => null);
            if (channel?.members) {
                for (const member of channel.members.values()) {
                    if (member.id === this.client.user?.id || member.user?.bot) continue;
                    if (member.id === newState.id && newState.channelId !== botVoiceChannelId) continue;
                    humans++;
                }
            }
        }

        if (humans > 0) {
            this.cancel(guild.id);
            return;
        }

        this.schedule(guild.id);
    }

    /**
     * Háº¹n giá» rá»i phÃ²ng cho má»™t guild (bá» qua náº¿u Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ timer).
     * @param {string} guildId
     */
    schedule(guildId) {
        if (this.timers.has(guildId)) return;

        log.debug(`KÃªnh thoáº¡i cá»§a guild ${guildId} Ä‘Ã£ trá»‘ng, háº¹n rá»i sau ${Math.round(this.idleMs / 1000)}s.`);

        const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
            this.timers.delete(guildId);
            try {
                await this.onIdle(guildId);
            } catch (err) {
                log.error(`KhÃ´ng rá»i Ä‘Æ°á»£c kÃªnh thoáº¡i cá»§a guild ${guildId}:`, err);
            }
        }, this.idleMs);

        if (typeof timer.unref === 'function') timer.unref();
        this.timers.set(guildId, timer);
    }

    /**
     * Huá»· háº¹n giá» rá»i phÃ²ng (khi cÃ³ ngÆ°á»i quay láº¡i).
     * @param {string} guildId
     */
    cancel(guildId) {
        const timer = this.timers.get(guildId);
        if (timer) {
            clearTimeout(timer);
            this.timers.delete(guildId);
            log.debug(`CÃ³ ngÆ°á»i quay láº¡i kÃªnh thoáº¡i guild ${guildId}, Ä‘Ã£ huá»· háº¹n rá»i phÃ²ng.`);
        }
    }

    /** Sá»‘ guild Ä‘ang trong tráº¡ng thÃ¡i chá» rá»i phÃ²ng. */
    get pendingCount() {
        return this.timers.size;
    }
}

module.exports = VoicePresenceService;
module.exports.countHumans = countHumans;

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\utils\formatters.js ===
/**
 * Utility functions â€” Äá»‹nh dáº¡ng thá»i gian vÃ  thanh tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh.
 */

/**
 * Chuyá»ƒn mili-giÃ¢y sang chuá»—i thá»i gian Ä‘á»c Ä‘Æ°á»£c.
 * @param {number} ms - Thá»i gian tÃ­nh báº±ng mili-giÃ¢y
 * @returns {string} Chuá»—i dáº¡ng "mm:ss" hoáº·c "hh:mm:ss"
 *
 * @example
 * formatDuration(215000)  // "03:35"
 * formatDuration(3723000) // "01:02:03"
 */
function formatDuration(ms) {
    if (!ms || ms <= 0) return '00:00';

    const totalSeconds = Math.floor(ms / 1000);
    const hours = Math.floor(totalSeconds / 3600);
    const minutes = Math.floor((totalSeconds % 3600) / 60);
    const seconds = totalSeconds % 60;

    const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

    if (hours > 0) {
        return `${pad(hours)}:${pad(minutes)}:${pad(seconds)}`;
    }
    return `${pad(minutes)}:${pad(seconds)}`;
}

/**
 * Táº¡o thanh tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh dáº¡ng kÃ½ tá»±.
 * @param {number} currentMs - Vá»‹ trÃ­ phÃ¡t hiá»‡n táº¡i (mili-giÃ¢y)
 * @param {number} totalMs - Tá»•ng thá»i lÆ°á»£ng (mili-giÃ¢y)
 * @param {number} [length=15] - Sá»‘ kÃ½ tá»± cá»§a thanh tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh
 * @returns {string} Thanh tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh, VD: "â–¶ â–ˆâ–ˆâ–ˆâ–ˆâ–ˆâ–ˆâ–ˆâ–ˆâ–‘â–‘â–‘â–‘â–‘â–‘â–‘ 02:15 / 04:30"
 */
function createProgressBar(currentMs, totalMs, length = 15) {
    if (!totalMs || totalMs <= 0) {
        return `ðŸ”´ LIVE â€” ${formatDuration(currentMs)}`;
    }

    const progress = Math.min(currentMs / totalMs, 1);
    const filledLength = Math.round(progress * length);
    const emptyLength = length - filledLength;

    const filled = 'â–ˆ'.repeat(filledLength);
    const empty = 'â–‘'.repeat(emptyLength);

    return `â–¶ ${filled}${empty} ${formatDuration(currentMs)} / ${formatDuration(totalMs)}`;
}

/**
 * RÃºt gá»n chuá»—i náº¿u quÃ¡ dÃ i.
 * @param {string} str - Chuá»—i gá»‘c
 * @param {number} [maxLength=50] - Äá»™ dÃ i tá»‘i Ä‘a
 * @returns {string} Chuá»—i Ä‘Ã£ rÃºt gá»n
 */
function truncate(str, maxLength = 50) {
    if (!str) return '';
    if (str.length <= maxLength) return str;
    return str.slice(0, maxLength - 3) + '...';
}

/**
 * TÃ­nh tá»•ng thá»i lÆ°á»£ng cá»§a má»™t máº£ng tracks (mili-giÃ¢y).
 * @param {Array} tracks - Máº£ng cÃ¡c track object cÃ³ thuá»™c tÃ­nh duration hoáº·c length
 * @returns {number} Tá»•ng thá»i lÆ°á»£ng (ms)
 */
function totalDuration(tracks) {
    if (!tracks || !tracks.length) return 0;
    return tracks.reduce((sum, t) => {
        const dur = t.duration || t.length || 0;
        return sum + dur;
    }, 0);
}

/**
 * PhÃ¢n tÃ­ch chuá»—i thá»i gian do ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng nháº­p thÃ nh mili-giÃ¢y.
 *
 * Há»— trá»£ cÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng:
 *  - `90`        â†’ 90 giÃ¢y
 *  - `1:30`      â†’ 1 phÃºt 30 giÃ¢y
 *  - `1:02:03`   â†’ 1 giá» 2 phÃºt 3 giÃ¢y
 *  - `1h2m3s`    â†’ 1 giá» 2 phÃºt 3 giÃ¢y
 *
 * @param {string} input - Chuá»—i thá»i gian
 * @returns {number|null} Thá»i gian (ms) hoáº·c null náº¿u khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡
 */
function parseTime(input) {
    if (input === null || input === undefined) return null;

    const raw = String(input).trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!raw) return null;

    // Dáº¡ng 1: 90  |  1:30  |  1:02:03
    if (/^\d+(:\d{1,2}){0,2}$/.test(raw)) {
        const parts = raw.split(':').map((n) => Number.parseInt(n, 10));
        if (parts.some((n) => !Number.isFinite(n))) return null;

        // Äáº£o ngÆ°á»£c Ä‘á»ƒ tÃ­nh theo trá»ng sá»‘ 1 / 60 / 3600
        const seconds = parts.reverse().reduce((total, value, index) => total + value * 60 ** index, 0);
        return seconds * 1000;
    }

    // Dáº¡ng 2: 1h2m3s (má»i thÃ nh pháº§n Ä‘á»u tÃ¹y chá»n)
    const match = raw.match(/^(?:(\d+)h)?(?:(\d+)m)?(?:(\d+)s)?$/);
    if (!match || (!match[1] && !match[2] && !match[3])) return null;

    const hours = Number.parseInt(match[1] || '0', 10);
    const minutes = Number.parseInt(match[2] || '0', 10);
    const seconds = Number.parseInt(match[3] || '0', 10);

    return (hours * 3600 + minutes * 60 + seconds) * 1000;
}

module.exports = {
    formatDuration,
    createProgressBar,
    truncate,
    totalDuration,
    parseTime
};

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\utils\interactions.js ===
/**
 * interactions.js â€” Tiá»‡n Ã­ch nháº­n diá»‡n tÆ°Æ¡ng tÃ¡c.
 */

const { MessageFlags } = require('discord.js');

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm tra má»™t tin nháº¯n cÃ³ pháº£i dáº¡ng ephemeral (chá»‰ ngÆ°á»i gá»i tháº¥y) hay khÃ´ng.
 * Há»— trá»£ cáº£ trÆ°á»ng há»£p `message.flags` lÃ  sá»‘ (báº£n discord.js cÅ©) láº«n BitField.
 * @param {import('discord.js').Message} message
 * @returns {boolean}
 */
function isEphemeralMessage(message) {
    if (!message || message.flags === undefined || message.flags === null) return false;

    const flags = message.flags;
    if (typeof flags === 'number') return (flags & MessageFlags.Ephemeral) === MessageFlags.Ephemeral;
    return typeof flags.has === 'function' && flags.has(MessageFlags.Ephemeral);
}

module.exports = { isEphemeralMessage };

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\utils\permissions.js ===
/**
 * permissions.js â€” Kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘iá»u kiá»‡n kÃªnh thoáº¡i vÃ  tráº¡ng thÃ¡i player.
 *
 * CÃ¡c hÃ m á»Ÿ Ä‘Ã¢y tráº£ vá» KHOÃ Dá»ŠCH (vÃ­ dá»¥ `common.needVoice`) chá»© khÃ´ng tráº£ vá»
 * chuá»—i Ä‘Ã£ dá»‹ch, Ä‘á»ƒ táº§ng gá»i tá»± quyáº¿t Ä‘á»‹nh ngÃ´n ngá»¯ hiá»ƒn thá»‹.
 */

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm tra xem member cÃ³ á»Ÿ trong voice channel hay khÃ´ng.
 * @param {import('discord.js').GuildMember} member
 * @returns {{ ok: boolean, channel?: import('discord.js').VoiceBasedChannel, error?: string }}
 */
function checkVoiceChannel(member) {
    const channel = member?.voice?.channel;
    if (!channel) {
        return { ok: false, error: 'common.needVoice' };
    }
    return { ok: true, channel };
}

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm tra xem member cÃ³ á»Ÿ CÃ™NG voice channel vá»›i bot hay khÃ´ng.
 * @param {import('discord.js').GuildMember} member
 * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
 * @returns {{ ok: boolean, channel?: import('discord.js').VoiceBasedChannel, error?: string }}
 */
function checkSameVoiceChannel(member, player) {
    const voiceCheck = checkVoiceChannel(member);
    if (!voiceCheck.ok) return voiceCheck;

    if (player && player.voiceId && voiceCheck.channel.id !== player.voiceId) {
        return { ok: false, error: 'common.needSameVoice' };
    }
    return { ok: true, channel: voiceCheck.channel };
}

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm tra xem cÃ³ player Ä‘ang hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng trong guild hay khÃ´ng.
 * @param {import('kazagumo').Kazagumo} manager - Kazagumo manager
 * @param {string} guildId
 * @returns {{ ok: boolean, player?: object, error?: string }}
 */
function checkPlayer(manager, guildId) {
    const player = manager.players.get(guildId);
    if (!player) {
        return { ok: false, error: 'common.noPlayer' };
    }
    return { ok: true, player };
}

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm tra xem cÃ³ bÃ i nháº¡c Ä‘ang phÃ¡t khÃ´ng.
 * @param {object} player - Kazagumo player
 * @returns {{ ok: boolean, error?: string }}
 */
function checkPlaying(player) {
    if (!player || (!player.playing && !player.paused)) {
        return { ok: false, error: 'common.noPlaying' };
    }
    return { ok: true };
}

module.exports = {
    checkVoiceChannel,
    checkSameVoiceChannel,
    checkPlayer,
    checkPlaying
};

=== FILE: C:\Users\ad\Documents\Workspace\Discord-Music-Bot-main\src\utils\reply.js ===
/**
 * reply.js â€” Tiá»‡n Ã­ch pháº£n há»“i interaction an toÃ n.
 *
 * Má»i pháº£n há»“i Ä‘á»u Ä‘Æ°á»£c bá»c try/catch vÃ¬ interaction cÃ³ thá»ƒ Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n (10062)
 * hoáº·c Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c pháº£n há»“i trÆ°á»›c Ä‘Ã³ â€” nhá»¯ng lá»—i nÃ y khÃ´ng nÃªn lÃ m sáº­p luá»“ng xá»­ lÃ½.
 */

const { MessageFlags } = require('discord.js');
const { t } = require('../locales');

/** Cá» ephemeral cá»§a Discord */
const EPHEMERAL = MessageFlags.Ephemeral;

/**
 * Gá»­i má»™t pháº£n há»“i ephemeral, tá»± chá»n reply hay editReply tuá»³ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i interaction.
 * @param {import('discord.js').Interaction} interaction
 * @param {string|object} payload - Ná»™i dung hoáº·c payload Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§
 * @returns {Promise<unknown>}
 */
async function respondEphemeral(interaction, payload) {
    const body = typeof payload === 'string' ? { content: payload, embeds: [], components: [] } : payload;

    try {
        if (interaction.deferred || interaction.replied) {
            return await interaction.editReply(body);
        }
        return await interaction.reply({ ...body, flags: EPHEMERAL });
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

/**
 * Chuyá»ƒn lá»—i há»‡ thá»‘ng thÃ nh thÃ´ng bÃ¡o thÃ¢n thiá»‡n vá»›i ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng.
 * @param {Error|unknown} err
 * @returns {string}
 */
function describeError(err) {
    const message = (err && err.message) || String(err);
    if (/no node/i.test(message)) return t('common.lavalinkDown');
    if (/quÃ¡ thá»i gian|timeout/i.test(message)) return t('common.lavalinkDown');
    return t('common.loadError', { message });
}

/**
 * Gá»­i thÃ´ng bÃ¡o lá»—i cho ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng vÃ  tráº£ vá» ná»™i dung Ä‘Ã£ gá»­i.
 * @param {import('discord.js').Interaction} interaction
 * @param {Error|unknown} err
 * @returns {Promise<string>}
 */
async function respondError(interaction, err) {
    const content = describeError(err);
    await respondEphemeral(interaction, content);
    return content;
}

module.exports = { respondEphemeral, respondError, describeError, EPHEMERAL };

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm thá»­ CommandHandler â€” sá»• Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ lá»‡nh vÃ  lá»›p kiá»ƒm tra dÃ¹ng chung.
 */

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const { MessageFlags } = require('discord.js');

const CommandHandler = require('../src/core/CommandHandler');
const { createContext } = require('../src/core/context');

/** Logger im láº·ng Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng lÃ m á»“n output kiá»ƒm thá»­ */
const silentLogger = {
    error: () => {},
    warn: () => {},
    info: () => {},
    debug: () => {},
    child() {
        return this;
    }
};

/**
 * Táº¡o ngá»¯ cáº£nh bot giáº£.
 * @param {{ players?: Map<string, object> }} [options]
 */
function mockContext(options = {}) {
    const players = options.players || new Map();
    return createContext({
        client: { channels: { cache: new Map() } },
        manager: { players },
        playerService: {},
        logger: silentLogger
    });
}

/**
 * Táº¡o interaction giáº£.
 * @param {{ commandName?: string, voice?: object|null, userId?: string, guildId?: string }} [options]
 */
function mockInteraction(options = {}) {
    const { commandName = 'play', voice = { id: 'voice-1' }, userId = 'user-1', guildId = 'guild-1' } = options;

    return {
        commandName,
        user: { id: userId },
        guild: { id: guildId },
        member: { voice: voice ? { channel: voice } : null },
        deferred: false,
        replied: false,
        calls: { reply: [], editReply: [], deferReply: [] },
        async reply(payload) {
            this.calls.reply.push(payload);
            this.replied = true;
        },
        async editReply(payload) {
            this.calls.editReply.push(payload);
        },
        async deferReply(payload) {
            this.calls.deferReply.push(payload);
            this.deferred = true;
        }
    };
}

test('CommandHandler â€” Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ vÃ  gom definition', () => {
    const handler = new CommandHandler(mockContext());
    handler.register({
        definitions: [{ name: 'alpha', description: 'A' }],
        meta: {},
        execute: async () => {}
    });
    handler.register({
        definitions: [
            { name: 'beta', description: 'B' },
            { name: 'gamma', description: 'C' }
        ],
        meta: {},
        handlers: { beta: async () => {}, gamma: async () => {} }
    });

    assert.equal(handler.getDefinitions().length, 3);
    assert.equal(handler.registry.size, 3);
});

    const handler = new CommandHandler(mockContext());
    handler.register({ definitions: [{ name: 'broken', description: 'X' }], meta: {} });

    assert.equal(handler.registry.size, 0);
    assert.equal(handler.getDefinitions().length, 1);
});

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm thá»­ Ä‘á»‹nh nghÄ©a slash command.
 *
 * CÃ¡c rÃ ng buá»™c dÆ°á»›i Ä‘Ã¢y lÃ  cá»§a Discord API â€” vi pháº¡m sáº½ khiáº¿n viá»‡c Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½
 * lá»‡nh tháº¥t báº¡i trÃªn production, nÃªn cáº§n cháº·n ngay tá»« CI.
 */

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');

const commandModules = require('../src/commands');
const SearchUI = require('../src/services/ui/SearchUI');
const NowPlayingUI = require('../src/services/ui/NowPlayingUI');
const { PlayerState } = require('../src/services/player/PlayerState');
const { t } = require('../src/locales');

/** Discord giá»›i háº¡n mÃ´ táº£ trong khoáº£ng 1..100 kÃ½ tá»± */
const NAME_PATTERN = /^[\w-]{1,32}$/;

/**
 * Thu tháº­p má»i definition tá»« táº¥t cáº£ module.
 * @returns {{ module: object, definition: object }[]}
 */
function collectDefinitions() {
    const list = [];
    for (const module of commandModules) {
        for (const definition of module.definitions || []) {
            list.push({ module, definition });
        }
    }
    return list;
}

const definitions = collectDefinitions();

test('commands â€” cÃ³ Ã­t nháº¥t 15 lá»‡nh Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½', () => {
    assert.ok(definitions.length >= 15, `chá»‰ cÃ³ ${definitions.length} lá»‡nh`);
});

test('commands â€” tÃªn lá»‡nh há»£p lá»‡ vÃ  khÃ´ng trÃ¹ng nhau', () => {
    const seen = new Set();

    for (const { definition } of definitions) {
        assert.match(definition.name, NAME_PATTERN, `tÃªn lá»‡nh "${definition.name}" khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡`);
        assert.equal(seen.has(definition.name), false, `lá»‡nh "${definition.name}" bá»‹ trÃ¹ng`);
        seen.add(definition.name);
    }
});

test('commands â€” mÃ´ táº£ lá»‡nh dÃ i tá»« 1 Ä‘áº¿n 100 kÃ½ tá»±', () => {
    for (const { definition } of definitions) {
        assert.ok(typeof definition.description === 'string', `lá»‡nh /${definition.name} thiáº¿u mÃ´ táº£`);
        assert.ok(definition.description.length >= 1, `lá»‡nh /${definition.name} mÃ´ táº£ rá»—ng`);
        assert.ok(
            definition.description.length <= 100,
            `lá»‡nh /${definition.name} mÃ´ táº£ dÃ i ${definition.description.length} kÃ½ tá»± (tá»‘i Ä‘a 100)`
        );
    }
});

test('commands â€” tÃ¹y chá»n há»£p lá»‡, choice khÃ´ng vÆ°á»£t 25 má»¥c', () => {
    for (const { definition } of definitions) {
        for (const option of definition.options || []) {
            const label = `/${definition.name} â†’ ${option.name}`;
            assert.match(option.name, /^[\w-]{1,32}$/, `${label}: tÃªn tÃ¹y chá»n khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡`);
            assert.ok(
                typeof option.description === 'string' && option.description.length <= 100,
                `${label}: mÃ´ táº£ khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡`
            );
            assert.ok(Number.isInteger(option.type), `${label}: thiáº¿u type`);

            if (option.choices) {
                assert.ok(option.choices.length <= 25, `${label}: cÃ³ ${option.choices.length} choice (tá»‘i Ä‘a 25)`);
                for (const choice of option.choices) {
                    assert.ok(choice.name.length <= 100, `${label}: choice name quÃ¡ dÃ i`);
                    assert.ok(String(choice.value).length <= 100, `${label}: choice value quÃ¡ dÃ i`);
                }
            }
        }
    }
});

test('commands â€” má»i module Ä‘á»u cÃ³ hÃ m xá»­ lÃ½ cho tá»«ng definition', () => {
    for (const { module, definition } of definitions) {
        const hasHandler = typeof module.execute === 'function';
        const hasNamedHandler = module.handlers && typeof module.handlers[definition.name] === 'function';

        assert.ok(hasHandler || hasNamedHandler, `lá»‡nh /${definition.name} khÃ´ng cÃ³ hÃ m xá»­ lÃ½`);
    }
});

test('commands â€” meta.defer chá»‰ nháº­n giÃ¡ trá»‹ há»£p lá»‡', () => {
    for (const { module, definition } of definitions) {
        const defer = module.meta?.defer;
        assert.ok(
            defer === undefined || defer === false || defer === 'ephemeral' || defer === 'public',
            `lá»‡nh /${definition.name} cÃ³ meta.defer khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡: ${defer}`
        );
    }
});

test('commands â€” lá»‡nh phÃ¡t nháº¡c Ä‘á»u yÃªu cáº§u á»Ÿ kÃªnh thoáº¡i', () => {
    const musicCommands = ['play', 'playtop', 'playskip', 'search'];

    for (const name of musicCommands) {
        const entry = definitions.find((d) => d.definition.name === name);
        assert.ok(entry, `thiáº¿u lá»‡nh /${name}`);
        assert.equal(entry.module.meta?.requiresVoice, true, `/${name} pháº£i yÃªu cáº§u requiresVoice`);
    }
});

test('commands â€” /nowplaying lÃ  lá»‡nh duy nháº¥t gá»­i cÃ´ng khai', () => {
    for (const { module, definition } of definitions) {
        if (definition.name === 'nowplaying' || definition.name === 'np') {
            assert.equal(module.meta?.defer, false, `/${definition.name} pháº£i tá»± reply cÃ´ng khai`);
        } else if (module.meta?.defer) {
            assert.equal(module.meta.defer, 'ephemeral', `/${definition.name} khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c defer cÃ´ng khai`);
        }
    }
});

            }
        }

    const service = new CooldownService();
    const result = service.check('u1:play', 1000);

    assert.equal(result.ok, true);
    assert.equal(result.retryAfterMs, 0);
});

test('CooldownService â€” láº§n thá»© hai bá»‹ cháº·n kÃ¨m thá»i gian chá»', () => {
    const service = new CooldownService();
    service.check('u1:play', 5000);

    const second = service.check('u1:play', 5000);
    assert.equal(second.ok, false);
    assert.ok(second.retryAfterMs > 0);
    assert.ok(second.retryAfterMs <= 5000);
});

test('CooldownService â€” khoÃ¡ khÃ¡c nhau khÃ´ng áº£nh hÆ°á»Ÿng nhau', () => {
    const service = new CooldownService();
    service.check('u1:play', 5000);

    assert.equal(service.check('u2:play', 5000).ok, true);
    assert.equal(service.check('u1:search', 5000).ok, true);
});

test('CooldownService â€” duration = 0 nghÄ©a lÃ  khÃ´ng giá»›i háº¡n', () => {
    const service = new CooldownService();
    assert.equal(service.check('u1:play', 0).ok, true);
    assert.equal(service.check('u1:play', 0).ok, true);
    assert.equal(service.size, 0);
});

test('CooldownService â€” reset xoÃ¡ cooldown cá»§a má»™t khoÃ¡', () => {
    const service = new CooldownService();
    service.check('u1:play', 5000);
    service.reset('u1:play');

    assert.equal(service.check('u1:play', 5000).ok, true);
});

test('CooldownService â€” sweep dá»n cÃ¡c khoÃ¡ Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n', () => {
    const service = new CooldownService();
    service.entries.set('expired', Date.now() - 1);
    service.entries.set('alive', Date.now() + 60000);

    assert.equal(service.sweep(), 1);
    assert.equal(service.size, 1);
    assert.equal(service.entries.has('alive'), true);
});

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm thá»­ cÃ¡c hÃ m Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng thá»i gian vÃ  thanh tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh.
 */

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');

const { formatDuration, createProgressBar, truncate, totalDuration, parseTime } = require('../src/utils/formatters');

test('formatDuration â€” Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng mm:ss vÃ  hh:mm:ss', () => {
    assert.equal(formatDuration(0), '00:00');
    assert.equal(formatDuration(-5), '00:00');
    assert.equal(formatDuration(215000), '03:35');
    assert.equal(formatDuration(3723000), '01:02:03');
});

test('truncate â€” cáº¯t chuá»—i quÃ¡ dÃ i', () => {
    assert.equal(truncate('abc', 10), 'abc');
    assert.equal(truncate('', 10), '');
    assert.equal(truncate('abcdefghij', 5), 'ab...');
    assert.equal(truncate(null, 5), '');
});

test('totalDuration â€” cá»™ng dá»“n thá»i lÆ°á»£ng', () => {
    assert.equal(totalDuration([]), 0);
    assert.equal(totalDuration(null), 0);
    assert.equal(totalDuration([{ duration: 1000 }, { length: 2000 }]), 3000);

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm thá»­ tÃ­ch há»£p â€” cháº¡y trá»n luá»“ng lá»‡nh vá»›i Discord/Kazagumo giáº£ láº­p.
 *
 * Má»¥c tiÃªu: xÃ¡c minh hÃ nh vi cá»‘t lÃµi mÃ  ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng yÃªu cáº§u
 * "khi bot phÃ¡t nháº¡c thÃ¬ báº£ng Now Playing lÃ  ephemeral, chá»‰ /nowplaying má»›i cÃ´ng khai".
 */

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const { MessageFlags } = require('discord.js');
const { KazagumoTrack: Track } = require('kazagumo');
const CommandHandler = require('../src/core/CommandHandler');
const PlayerService = require('../src/services/player/PlayerService');
const SearchCache = require('../src/services/cache/SearchCache');
const StorageService = require('../src/services/storage/StorageService');
const { createContext } = require('../src/core/context');
const { getState } = require('../src/services/player/PlayerState');
const commandModules = require('../src/commands');

const silentLogger = {
    error: () => {},
    warn: () => {},
    info: () => {},
    debug: () => {},
    child() {
        return this;
    }
};

/** Táº¡o dá»¯ liá»‡u track thÃ´ Ä‘Ãºng Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng Lavalink mÃ  KazagumoTrack yÃªu cáº§u */
function rawTrack(id, title) {
    return {
        encoded: `encoded-${id}`,
        info: {
            title: title || `BÃ i hÃ¡t ${id}`,
            uri: `https://example.com/${id}`,
            identifier: id,
            author: 'Ca sÄ© thá»­ nghiá»‡m',
            sourceName: 'youtube',
            isSeekable: true,
            isStream: false,
            length: 180000,
            artworkUrl: 'https://example.com/thumb.jpg'
        }
    };
}

/** Táº¡o player giáº£ mÃ´ phá»ng KazagumoPlayer */
function fakePlayer({ guildId, textId, voiceId, volume = 130 }) {
    const queue = [];
    queue.current = null;
    queue.add = (track) => {
        if (!queue.current) {
            queue.current = track;
            return queue;
        }
        queue.push(track);
        return queue;
    };

    return {
        guildId,
        textId,
        voiceId,
        volume,
        state: 1,
        playing: false,
        paused: false,
        queue,
        position: 0,
        get pausedOrPlaying() {
            return this.playing || this.paused;
        },
        async play() {
            if (!queue.current) queue.current = queue.shift() || null;
            this.playing = true;
            return this;
        },
        skip() {
            this.playing = false;
            queue.current = queue.shift() || null;
            return this;
        },
        pause(state) {
            this.paused = Boolean(state);
            return this;
        },
        async setVolume(v) {
            this.volume = v;
            return this;
        },
        async seek(position) {
            this.position = position;
            return this;
        },
        async setFilters() {
            return this;
        },
        setTextChannel(id) {
            this.textId = id;
            return this;
        },
        setVoiceChannel(id) {
            this.voiceId = id;
            return this;
        },
        async destroy() {
            this.state = 3;
            return this;
        }
    };
}

/** Táº¡o manager giáº£ vá»›i káº¿t quáº£ tÃ¬m kiáº¿m cá»‘ Ä‘á»‹nh */
function fakeManager(result) {
    const players = new Map();

    return {
        players,
        lastQuery: null,
        async search(query) {
            this.lastQuery = query;
            return typeof result === 'function' ? result(query) : result;
        },
        async createPlayer(options) {
            const player = fakePlayer(options);
            players.set(options.guildId, player);
            return player;
        }
    };
}

/** Táº¡o interaction giáº£ ghi láº¡i má»i lá»i gá»i */
function fakeInteraction({ commandName, query, guildId = 'guild-int', userId = 'user-int' }) {
    const calls = { reply: [], editReply: [], deferReply: [], followUp: [], webhookEdit: [] };

    return {
        commandName,
        guild: { id: guildId },
        user: { id: userId, username: 'tester', globalName: 'Tester' },
        member: { voice: { channel: { id: 'voice-int' } } },
        channelId: 'text-int',
        deferred: false,
        replied: false,
        calls,
        options: {
            getString: (name) => (name === 'query' ? query : null),
            getInteger: () => null,
            getBoolean: () => null
        },
        async reply(payload) {
            calls.reply.push(payload);
            this.replied = true;
            return { id: 'msg-reply', channelId: this.channelId, edit: async () => {} };
        },
        async editReply(payload) {
            calls.editReply.push(payload);
            return { id: 'msg-edit', channelId: this.channelId, edit: async () => {} };
        },
        async deferReply(payload) {
            calls.deferReply.push(payload);
            this.deferred = true;
        },
        async fetchReply() {
            return { id: 'msg-reply', channelId: this.channelId, edit: async () => {} };
        },
        async followUp(payload) {
            calls.followUp.push(payload);
        },
        webhook: {
            async editMessage(messageId, payload) {
                calls.webhookEdit.push({ messageId, payload });
            }
        }
    };
}

/**
 * Dá»±ng mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng tÃ­ch há»£p hoÃ n chá»‰nh.
 * @param {object} searchResult - Káº¿t quáº£ mÃ  manager.search tráº£ vá»
 */
async function setup(searchResult) {
    const manager = fakeManager(searchResult);
    const client = { channels: { cache: new Map() } };

    const storage = new StorageService({ filePath: `./data/test-${Date.now()}-${Math.random()}.json` });
    // KhÃ´ng gá»i init() Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh ghi Ä‘Ä©a trong lÃºc kiá»ƒm thá»­
    storage.ready = false;

    const playerService = new PlayerService(client, manager, {
        storage,
        searchCache: new SearchCache({ max: 10, ttlMs: 60000 }),
        logger: silentLogger
    });

    const ctx = createContext({ client, manager, playerService, storage, logger: silentLogger });
    const handler = new CommandHandler(ctx);
    handler.registerAll(commandModules);

    // Cooldown lÃ  singleton dÃ¹ng chung â€” xoÃ¡ Ä‘á»ƒ cÃ¡c bÃ i kiá»ƒm thá»­ khÃ´ng cháº·n nhau
    ctx.cooldown.clear();

    return { manager, client, storage, playerService, handler, ctx };
}

/** Dá»n ticker Ä‘á»ƒ tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh kiá»ƒm thá»­ káº¿t thÃºc sáº¡ch sáº½ */
function cleanup(guildId) {
    getState(guildId).clearPanels();
}

// /play
// ============================================================

test('tÃ­ch há»£p â€” /play defer ephemeral vÃ  váº½ báº£ng Now Playing dáº¡ng ephemeral', async () => {
    const track = new Track(rawTrack('a1', 'BÃ i hÃ¡t A'), null);
    const { handler, manager } = await setup({ type: 'TRACK', tracks: [track] });

    const interaction = fakeInteraction({ commandName: 'play', query: 'bÃ i hÃ¡t a' });
    await handler.dispatch(interaction);

    // 1. Pháº£i defer dáº¡ng ephemeral
    assert.equal(interaction.calls.deferReply.length, 1);
    assert.equal(interaction.calls.deferReply[0].flags, MessageFlags.Ephemeral);

    // 2. KhÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c gá»­i gÃ¬ vÃ o kÃªnh chat
    assert.equal(interaction.calls.reply.length, 0);

    // 3. Báº£ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c váº½ qua webhook trÃªn chÃ­nh tin nháº¯n pháº£n há»“i ephemeral
    assert.ok(interaction.calls.webhookEdit.length >= 1, 'pháº£i váº½ báº£ng Now Playing');

    const last = interaction.calls.webhookEdit.at(-1);
    assert.equal(last.messageId, '@original');
    assert.match(last.payload.embeds[0].data.title, /BÃ i hÃ¡t A/);
    assert.match(last.payload.content, /ÄÃ£ thÃªm/);

    // 4. Track Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c náº¡p vÃ o player
    const player = manager.players.get('guild-int');
    assert.ok(player.queue.current, 'player pháº£i cÃ³ bÃ i Ä‘ang phÃ¡t');

    cleanup('guild-int');
});

test('tÃ­ch há»£p â€” /play bÃ¡o lá»—i khi khÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y káº¿t quáº£', async () => {
    const { handler } = await setup({ type: 'SEARCH', tracks: [] });

    const interaction = fakeInteraction({ commandName: 'play', query: 'khong co gi' });
    await handler.dispatch(interaction);

    const lastReply = interaction.calls.editReply[interaction.calls.editReply.length - 1];
    assert.match(lastReply, /KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y/);

    cleanup('guild-int');
});

// ============================================================

test('tÃ­ch há»£p â€” /nowplaying gá»­i CÃ”NG KHAI vÃ o kÃªnh chat', async () => {
    const track = new Track(rawTrack('n1', 'BÃ i hÃ¡t N'), null);
    const { handler, manager } = await setup({ type: 'TRACK', tracks: [track] });

    // Chuáº©n bá»‹ player Ä‘ang phÃ¡t
    const player = await manager.createPlayer({
        guildId: 'guild-int',
        textId: 'text-int',
        voiceId: 'voice-int',
        volume: 130
    });
    player.queue.current = track;
    player.playing = true;

    const interaction = fakeInteraction({ commandName: 'nowplaying' });
    await handler.dispatch(interaction);

    // 1. KhÃ´ng defer
    assert.equal(interaction.calls.deferReply.length, 0);

    // 2. Reply cÃ´ng khai â€” KHÃ”NG cÃ³ cá» ephemeral
    assert.equal(interaction.calls.reply.length, 1);
    assert.equal(interaction.calls.reply[0].flags, undefined);
    assert.match(interaction.calls.reply[0].embeds[0].data.title, /BÃ i hÃ¡t N/);

    // 3. Báº£ng cÃ´ng khai Ä‘Æ°á»£c theo dÃµi Ä‘á»ƒ ticker cáº­p nháº­t tiáº¿p
    assert.ok(getState('guild-int').publicPanel, 'pháº£i Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ báº£ng cÃ´ng khai');

    cleanup('guild-int');
});

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm thá»­ PlayerState â€” tráº¡ng thÃ¡i phÃ¡t nháº¡c theo guild.
 */

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');

const { PlayerState, getState, removeState } = require('../src/services/player/PlayerState');
const FilterService = require('../src/services/filters/FilterService');

test('PlayerState â€” giÃ¡ trá»‹ khá»Ÿi táº¡o há»£p lÃ½', () => {
    const state = new PlayerState('g1');
    assert.equal(state.repeatMode, 'off');
    assert.equal(state.filter, FilterService.default);
    assert.equal(state.playbackGeneration, 0);
    assert.deepEqual(state.history, []);
    assert.equal(state.getEphemeralContext(), null);
    assert.equal(state.publicPanel, null);
});

test('PlayerState â€” generation guard tÄƒng vÃ  kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘Ãºng', () => {
    const state = new PlayerState('g1');
    const first = state.incrementGeneration();
    assert.equal(first, 1);
    assert.equal(state.isGenerationValid(first), true);

    const second = state.incrementGeneration();
    assert.equal(second, 2);
    assert.equal(state.isGenerationValid(first), false);
    assert.equal(state.isGenerationValid(second), true);
});

test('PlayerState â€” cycleRepeatMode xoay vÃ²ng off â†’ track â†’ queue â†’ off', () => {
    const state = new PlayerState('g1');
    assert.equal(state.cycleRepeatMode(), 'track');
    assert.equal(state.cycleRepeatMode(), 'queue');
    assert.equal(state.cycleRepeatMode(), 'off');
});

test('PlayerState â€” setRepeatMode chá»‰ nháº­n giÃ¡ trá»‹ há»£p lá»‡', () => {
    const state = new PlayerState('g1');
    state.setRepeatMode('track');
    assert.equal(state.repeatMode, 'track');
    state.setRepeatMode('khong-hop-le');
    assert.equal(state.repeatMode, 'track');
});

test('PlayerState â€” history thÃªm vÃ o Ä‘áº§u vÃ  giá»›i háº¡n sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng', () => {
    const state = new PlayerState('g1');
    state.addToHistory({ title: 'A' });
    state.addToHistory({ title: 'B' });

    assert.equal(state.history[0].title, 'B');
    assert.equal(state.history.length, 2);
    assert.equal(state.popFromHistory().title, 'B');
    assert.equal(state.history.length, 1);
});

test('PlayerState â€” getRecentKeys gom khoÃ¡ Ä‘á»‹nh danh', () => {
    const state = new PlayerState('g1');
    state.addToHistory({ identifier: 'id-1', title: 'A', author: 'X' });
    state.addToHistory({ uri: 'https://x/y', title: 'B', author: 'Y' });

    const keys = state.getRecentKeys();
    assert.equal(keys.has('id-1'), true);
    assert.equal(keys.has('https://x/y'), true);
});

test('PlayerState â€” ephemeral context há»£p lá»‡ rá»“i tá»± háº¿t háº¡n', () => {
    const state = new PlayerState('g1');
    state.setEphemeralContext({ webhook: {}, userId: 'u1', channelId: 'c1' });

    const ctx = state.getEphemeralContext();
    assert.ok(ctx);
    assert.equal(ctx.userId, 'u1');

    // Giáº£ láº­p token Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n
    state.ephemeralContext.expiresAt = Date.now() - 1;
    assert.equal(state.getEphemeralContext(), null);
});

test('PlayerState â€” clearPanels xoÃ¡ toÃ n bá»™ tham chiáº¿u báº£ng', () => {
    const state = new PlayerState('g1');
    state.setEphemeralContext({ webhook: {}, userId: 'u1', channelId: 'c1' });
    state.publicPanel = { channelId: 'c1', message: {} };
    state.panelNote = 'ghi chÃº';
    state.panelTrackKey = 'k';

    state.clearPanels();

    assert.equal(state.getEphemeralContext(), null);
    assert.equal(state.publicPanel, null);
    assert.equal(state.panelNote, null);
    assert.equal(state.panelTrackKey, null);
    assert.equal(state.progressInterval, null);
});

    state.panelDisabled = true;
    state.panelLastInteractionAt = Date.now() - 10 * 60 * 1000;

    assert.equal(state.isPanelIdle(), true);

    state.touchPanel();
    assert.equal(state.panelDisabled, false);
    assert.equal(state.isPanelIdle(), false);
});

    const b = getState('guild-abc');
    assert.equal(a, b);

    removeState('guild-abc');
    const c = getState('guild-abc');
    assert.notEqual(a, c);

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm thá»­ QueueService â€” thao tÃ¡c vÃ  giá»›i háº¡n hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i.
 */

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');

const queueService = require('../src/services/queue/QueueService');
const { MAX_QUEUE_SIZE, MAX_QUEUE_SIZE_PER_USER, MAX_PLAYLIST_LOAD } = require('../src/config/constants');

/**
 * Táº¡o player giáº£ vá»›i hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i cho trÆ°á»›c.
 *
 * KazagumoQueue lÃ  má»™t máº£ng cÃ³ thÃªm phÆ°Æ¡ng thá»©c `add()`, nÃªn mock cÅ©ng mÃ´ phá»ng
 * Ä‘Ãºng nhÆ° váº­y Ä‘á»ƒ kiá»ƒm thá»­ QueueService sÃ¡t vá»›i thá»±c táº¿.
 * @param {object[]} tracks
 */
function fakePlayer(tracks = []) {
    const queue = [...tracks];
    queue.current = null;
    queue.add = (track) => {
        queue.push(track);
        return queue;
    };
    return { queue };
}

test('QueueService â€” add má»™t vÃ  nhiá»u track', () => {
    const player = fakePlayer();
    queueService.add(player, { title: 'A' });
    queueService.add(player, [{ title: 'B' }, { title: 'C' }]);

    assert.equal(player.queue.length, 3);
    assert.equal(player.queue[2].title, 'C');
});

test('QueueService â€” addTop chÃ¨n lÃªn Ä‘áº§u', () => {
    const player = fakePlayer([{ title: 'A' }]);
    queueService.addTop(player, { title: 'B' });
    assert.equal(player.queue[0].title, 'B');
});

test('QueueService â€” remove theo index 0-based', () => {
    const player = fakePlayer([{ title: 'A' }, { title: 'B' }]);
    const removed = queueService.remove(player, 0);
    assert.equal(removed.title, 'A');
    assert.equal(player.queue.length, 1);
    assert.equal(queueService.remove(player, 5), null);
});

test('QueueService â€” move Ä‘á»•i vá»‹ trÃ­', () => {
    const player = fakePlayer([{ title: 'A' }, { title: 'B' }, { title: 'C' }]);
    assert.equal(queueService.move(player, 0, 2), true);
    assert.deepEqual(
        player.queue.map((t) => t.title),
        ['B', 'C', 'A']
    );
    assert.equal(queueService.move(player, 9, 0), false);
});

test('QueueService â€” clear xoÃ¡ toÃ n bá»™', () => {
    const player = fakePlayer([{ title: 'A' }, { title: 'B' }]);
    assert.equal(queueService.clear(player), 2);
    assert.equal(player.queue.length, 0);
});

test('QueueService â€” shuffle toÃ n bá»™ giá»¯ nguyÃªn sá»‘ lÆ°á»£ng', () => {
    const player = fakePlayer(Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => ({ title: `T${i}` })));
    const count = queueService.shuffle(player);
    assert.equal(count, 20);
    assert.equal(new Set(player.queue.map((t) => t.title)).size, 20);
});

test('QueueService â€” shuffle theo ngÆ°á»i yÃªu cáº§u chá»‰ Ä‘á»¥ng bÃ i cá»§a há»', () => {
    const player = fakePlayer([
        { title: 'A', requesterId: 'u1' },
        { title: 'B', requesterId: 'u2' },
        { title: 'C', requesterId: 'u1' },
        { title: 'D', requesterId: 'u1' }
    ]);

    queueService.shuffle(player, 'u1');

    // BÃ i cá»§a u2 pháº£i giá»¯ nguyÃªn vá»‹ trÃ­
    assert.equal(player.queue[1].title, 'B');
    // BÃ i cá»§a u1 váº«n náº±m á»Ÿ cÃ¡c vá»‹ trÃ­ 0, 2, 3
    assert.deepEqual(
        [player.queue[0].requesterId, player.queue[2].requesterId, player.queue[3].requesterId],
        ['u1', 'u1', 'u1']
    );
});

test('QueueService â€” countByRequester Ä‘áº¿m Ä‘Ãºng', () => {
    const player = fakePlayer([{ requesterId: 'u1' }, { requester: { id: 'u1' } }, { requesterId: 'u2' }]);
    assert.equal(queueService.countByRequester(player, 'u1'), 2);
    assert.equal(queueService.countByRequester(player, 'u2'), 1);
    assert.equal(queueService.countByRequester(player, null), 0);
});

test('QueueService â€” canAdd cho phÃ©p khi hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i cÃ²n chá»—', () => {
    const player = fakePlayer([{ requesterId: 'u1' }]);
    const result = queueService.canAdd(player, 3, 'u1');

    assert.equal(result.ok, true);
    assert.equal(result.allowed, 3);
});

test('QueueService â€” canAdd cáº¯t bá»›t theo giá»›i háº¡n playlist', () => {
    const player = fakePlayer();
    const result = queueService.canAdd(player, MAX_PLAYLIST_LOAD + 50, null);

    assert.equal(result.ok, true);
    assert.equal(result.allowed, MAX_PLAYLIST_LOAD);
});

test('QueueService â€” canAdd bÃ¡o lá»—i khi hÃ ng Ä‘á»£i Ä‘áº§y', () => {
    const full = Array.from({ length: MAX_QUEUE_SIZE }, () => ({ requesterId: 'u2' }));
    const result = queueService.canAdd(fakePlayer(full), 1, 'u1');

    assert.equal(result.ok, false);
    assert.equal(result.error, 'queueFull');
    assert.equal(result.vars.max, MAX_QUEUE_SIZE);
});

test('QueueService â€” canAdd bÃ¡o lá»—i khi ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng vÆ°á»£t háº¡n má»©c', () => {
    const mine = Array.from({ length: MAX_QUEUE_SIZE_PER_USER }, () => ({ requesterId: 'u1' }));
    const result = queueService.canAdd(fakePlayer(mine), 1, 'u1');

    assert.equal(result.ok, false);
    assert.equal(result.error, 'userQueueFull');
    assert.equal(result.vars.max, MAX_QUEUE_SIZE_PER_USER);
});

test('QueueService â€” getPage phÃ¢n trang Ä‘Ãºng', () => {
    const player = fakePlayer(Array.from({ length: 25 }, (_, i) => ({ title: `T${i}` })));
    const page2 = queueService.getPage(player, 2, 10);

    assert.equal(page2.page, 2);
    assert.equal(page2.totalPages, 3);
    assert.equal(page2.tracks.length, 10);
    assert.equal(page2.tracks[0].title, 'T10');
});

test('QueueService â€” getPage káº¹p trang vÆ°á»£t biÃªn', () => {
    const player = fakePlayer([{ title: 'A' }]);
    assert.equal(queueService.getPage(player, 99, 10).page, 1);
    assert.equal(queueService.getPage(player, 0, 10).page, 1);
});

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm thá»­ SearchCache â€” LRU kÃ¨m TTL.
 */

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');

const SearchCache = require('../src/services/cache/SearchCache');

test('SearchCache â€” normaliseKey bá» khÃ¡c biá»‡t hoa thÆ°á»ng vÃ  khoáº£ng tráº¯ng', () => {
    assert.equal(SearchCache.normaliseKey('  BÃ i   HÃ¡t '), SearchCache.normaliseKey('bÃ i hÃ¡t'));
    assert.notEqual(SearchCache.normaliseKey('abc', 'youtube'), SearchCache.normaliseKey('abc', 'soundcloud'));
});

test('SearchCache â€” lÆ°u vÃ  Ä‘á»c láº¡i giÃ¡ trá»‹', () => {
    const cache = new SearchCache({ max: 10, ttlMs: 60000 });
    cache.set('k1', { value: 42 });

    assert.deepEqual(cache.get('k1'), { value: 42 });
    assert.equal(cache.get('khong-co'), undefined);
});

test('SearchCache â€” má»¥c háº¿t háº¡n bá»‹ coi nhÆ° khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i', () => {
    const cache = new SearchCache({ max: 10, ttlMs: 60000 });
    cache.set('k1', 'v1');
    cache.store.get('k1').expiresAt = Date.now() - 1;

    assert.equal(cache.get('k1'), undefined);
    assert.equal(cache.size, 0);
});

test('SearchCache â€” loáº¡i bá» má»¥c cÅ© nháº¥t khi vÆ°á»£t ngÆ°á»¡ng (LRU)', () => {
    const cache = new SearchCache({ max: 2, ttlMs: 60000 });
    cache.set('a', 1);
    cache.set('b', 2);
    cache.set('c', 3);

    assert.equal(cache.size, 2);
    assert.equal(cache.get('a'), undefined);
    assert.equal(cache.get('b'), 2);
    assert.equal(cache.get('c'), 3);
});

test('SearchCache â€” Ä‘á»c láº¡i Ä‘Æ°a má»¥c lÃªn vá»‹ trÃ­ má»›i nháº¥t', () => {
    const cache = new SearchCache({ max: 2, ttlMs: 60000 });
    cache.set('a', 1);
    cache.set('b', 2);

    cache.get('a'); // 'a' trá»Ÿ thÃ nh má»›i nháº¥t
    cache.set('c', 3); // nÃªn 'b' bá»‹ loáº¡i

    assert.equal(cache.get('a'), 1);
    assert.equal(cache.get('b'), undefined);
    assert.equal(cache.get('c'), 3);
});

test('SearchCache â€” thá»‘ng kÃª hit/miss', () => {
    const cache = new SearchCache({ max: 10, ttlMs: 60000 });
    cache.set('a', 1);

    cache.get('a');
    cache.get('a');
    cache.get('x');

    const stats = cache.stats;
    assert.equal(stats.hits, 2);
    assert.equal(stats.misses, 1);
    assert.equal(stats.size, 1);
    assert.ok(stats.hitRate > 0.6);
});

test('SearchCache â€” prune xoÃ¡ má»¥c háº¿t háº¡n', () => {
    const cache = new SearchCache({ max: 10, ttlMs: 60000 });
    cache.set('a', 1);
    cache.set('b', 2);
    cache.store.get('a').expiresAt = Date.now() - 1;

    assert.equal(cache.prune(), 1);
    assert.equal(cache.size, 1);
});

test('SearchCache â€” clear xoÃ¡ táº¥t cáº£ vÃ  reset thá»‘ng kÃª', () => {
    const cache = new SearchCache({ max: 10, ttlMs: 60000 });
    cache.set('a', 1);
    cache.get('a');
    cache.clear();

    assert.equal(cache.size, 0);
    assert.equal(cache.stats.hits, 0);
    assert.equal(cache.stats.misses, 0);
});

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm thá»­ StorageService â€” lÆ°u trá»¯ bá»n vá»¯ng báº±ng file JSON.
 */

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs/promises');
const os = require('node:os');
const path = require('node:path');

const StorageService = require('../src/services/storage/StorageService');

/**
 * Táº¡o thÆ° má»¥c táº¡m riÃªng cho má»—i bÃ i kiá»ƒm thá»­.
 * @returns {Promise<string>}
 */
async function tempDir() {
    return fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'musicbot-test-'));
}

test('StorageService â€” táº¡o báº£n ghi máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh cho guild má»›i', async () => {
    const dir = await tempDir();
    const storage = new StorageService({ filePath: path.join(dir, 'guilds.json') });
    await storage.init();

    const record = storage.getGuild('g1');
    assert.equal(record.repeatMode, 'off');
    assert.equal(record.filter, 'off');
    assert.deepEqual(record.history, []);

    await fs.rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('StorageService â€” ghi vÃ  Ä‘á»c láº¡i sau khi khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng láº¡i', async () => {
    const dir = await tempDir();
    const filePath = path.join(dir, 'guilds.json');

    const first = new StorageService({ filePath });
    await first.init();
    first.setGuild('g1', { repeatMode: 'queue', volume: 75, filter: 'bassboost', autoplay: true });
    first.setHistory('g1', [{ title: 'BÃ i A' }, { title: 'BÃ i B' }]);
    await first.flush();

    const second = new StorageService({ filePath });
    await second.init();
    const record = second.getGuild('g1');

    assert.equal(record.repeatMode, 'queue');
    assert.equal(record.volume, 75);
    assert.equal(record.filter, 'bassboost');
    assert.equal(record.autoplay, true);
    assert.equal(record.history.length, 2);

    await fs.rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('StorageService â€” setHistory cáº¯t bá»›t theo maxHistory', async () => {
    const dir = await tempDir();
    const storage = new StorageService({ filePath: path.join(dir, 'guilds.json'), maxHistory: 3 });
    await storage.init();

    storage.setHistory('g1', [{ title: 'A' }, { title: 'B' }, { title: 'C' }, { title: 'D' }]);
    assert.equal(storage.getGuild('g1').history.length, 3);

    await fs.rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('StorageService â€” flush khÃ´ng ghi gÃ¬ khi dá»¯ liá»‡u khÃ´ng Ä‘á»•i', async () => {
    const dir = await tempDir();
    const storage = new StorageService({ filePath: path.join(dir, 'guilds.json') });
    await storage.init();

    assert.equal(await storage.flush(), false);

    storage.setGuild('g1', { volume: 50 });
    assert.equal(await storage.flush(), true);
    assert.equal(await storage.flush(), false);

    await fs.rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('StorageService â€” file há»ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c sao lÆ°u thay vÃ¬ lÃ m sáº­p bot', async () => {
    const dir = await tempDir();
    const filePath = path.join(dir, 'guilds.json');
    await fs.writeFile(filePath, '{ day khong phai json', 'utf8');

    const storage = new StorageService({ filePath });
    await storage.init();

    assert.deepEqual(storage.data.guilds, {});

    const files = await fs.readdir(dir);
    assert.ok(
        files.some((f) => f.includes('corrupt-')),
        'pháº£i cÃ³ file sao lÆ°u .corrupt-*'
    );

    await fs.rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('StorageService â€” removeGuild xoÃ¡ dá»¯ liá»‡u cá»§a guild', async () => {
    const dir = await tempDir();
    const storage = new StorageService({ filePath: path.join(dir, 'guilds.json') });
    await storage.init();

    storage.setGuild('g1', { volume: 50 });
    storage.removeGuild('g1');

    assert.equal(storage.data.guilds.g1, undefined);

    await fs.rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

/**
 * Kiá»ƒm thá»­ cÃ¡c lá»›p UI: NowPlayingUI, QueueUI, SearchUI.
 */

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');

const NowPlayingUI = require('../src/services/ui/NowPlayingUI');
const QueueUI = require('../src/services/ui/QueueUI');
const SearchUI = require('../src/services/ui/SearchUI');
const { PlayerState } = require('../src/services/player/PlayerState');

/**
 * Táº¡o player giáº£ Ä‘á»§ trÆ°á»ng cho lá»›p UI.
 * @param {object[]} [queue]
 */
function fakePlayer(queue = []) {
    return { paused: false, position: 30000, volume: 130, queue };
}

const track = {
    title: 'BÃ i hÃ¡t thá»­ nghiá»‡m',
    author: 'Nghá»‡ sÄ© thá»­ nghiá»‡m',
    uri: 'https://example.com/track',
    length: 180000,
    thumbnail: 'https://example.com/thumb.jpg',
    isStream: false,
    requester: {
        id: 'u1',
        username: 'tester',
        globalName: 'Tester',
        displayAvatarURL: () => 'https://example.com/a.png'
    }
};

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// NowPlayingUI
// ------------------------------------------------------------

test('NowPlayingUI â€” táº¡o embed vÃ  3 hÃ ng thÃ nh pháº§n', () => {
    const state = new PlayerState('g-ui-1');
    const { embed, components } = NowPlayingUI.create(fakePlayer(), track, state);

    const data = embed.toJSON();
    assert.equal(data.title, 'BÃ i hÃ¡t thá»­ nghiá»‡m');
    assert.equal(data.thumbnail.url, 'https://example.com/thumb.jpg');
    // 2 hÃ ng nÃºt + 1 hÃ ng select menu bá»™ lá»c
    assert.equal(components.length, 3);
    assert.equal(components[0].components.length, 5);
    assert.equal(components[1].components.length, 5);
});

test('NowPlayingUI â€” nÃºt bá»‹ vÃ´ hiá»‡u hoÃ¡ khi truyá»n disabled', () => {
    const state = new PlayerState('g-ui-2');
    const { components } = NowPlayingUI.create(fakePlayer(), track, state, { disabled: true });

    for (const row of components) {
        for (const component of row.components) {
            assert.equal(component.data.disabled, true);
        }
    }
});

test('NowPlayingUI â€” hiá»ƒn thá»‹ Ä‘Ãºng khi khÃ´ng cÃ³ bÃ i nÃ o', () => {
    const state = new PlayerState('g-ui-3');
    const { embed, components } = NowPlayingUI.create(fakePlayer(), null, state);

    assert.equal(components.length, 0);
    assert.match(embed.toJSON().title, /KhÃ´ng cÃ³ bÃ i nÃ o/);
});

test('SearchUI â€” táº¡o menu chá»n tá»« káº¿t quáº£ tÃ¬m kiáº¿m', () => {
    const tracks = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => ({
        title: `Káº¿t quáº£ ${i}`,

    }));

test('SearchUI â€” lÆ°u vÃ  láº¥y láº¡i phiÃªn tÃ¬m kiáº¿m', () => {
    const tracks = [{ title: 'A' }, { title: 'B' }];
    SearchUI.storeResults('g-search', 'u-search', tracks);

    assert.deepEqual(SearchUI.getResults('g-search', 'u-search'), tracks);
    assert.equal(SearchUI.getResults('g-search', 'nguoi-khac'), null);

    SearchUI.clearResults('g-search', 'u-search');
    assert.equal(SearchUI.getResults('g-search', 'u-search'), null);
});

test('SearchUI â€” phiÃªn háº¿t háº¡n bá»‹ coi nhÆ° khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i', () => {
    SearchUI.storeResults('g-exp', 'u-exp', [{ title: 'A' }]);
    SearchUI.prune(); // chÆ°a háº¿t háº¡n nÃªn váº«n cÃ²n
    assert.ok(SearchUI.getResults('g-exp', 'u-exp'));

    // Ã‰p háº¿t háº¡n
    const key = 'g-exp:u-exp';
    const session = SearchUI.getResults('g-exp', 'u-exp');
    assert.ok(session);
    assert.equal(SearchUI.prune() >= 0, true);
});
